### [1] [Helpful but Fallible: Developer Experiences of AI Tools Under a Coordinated Industrial Roll-out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20977)
*Andreas Bexell,Rushali Gupta,Lo Gullstrand Heander,Emma Söderberg,Per Runeson,Sigrid Eldh,Wei Shi,Konstantin Malysh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文通过对瑞典某大型电信公司AI开发工具（AI-devtools）协同部署的案例研究，基于12名软件专业人员的半结构化访谈，揭示开发者体验与职业预期，指出管理层与开发者之间存在期望落差，且感知风险是现有接受模型（如TAM2）未涵盖的重要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发工具正被工业界广泛采用，并伴随强烈的生产力提升预期，但开发者对这类部署的实际体验研究不足。当前证据主要来自工具评估、生产力指标和问卷调查，缺乏对持续、协同部署过程的定性现场描述，导致组织在预算投入、员工评估和实践调整上依据片面信息决策。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，针对瑞典某大型电信公司AI开发工具的协同部署展开调查。对三个站点的12名软件专业人员进行半结构化访谈，使用过程编码和主题分析进行数据处理，并以扩展技术接受模型（TAM2）作为事后分析框架进行解释。

Result: 研究发现，在用例、生产力、挫败感和工具局限性方面的结果与既有调查研究相互印证。此外，访谈揭示了管理层与开发者之间的期望落差，该落差对应TAM2中的主观规范和自愿性构念；同时发现参与者高度重视感知风险，而TAM2及类似接受模型并未体现这一因素。AI开发工具表现为有用但易出错的助手，其价值受组织期望、系统规模和开发者技能共同影响。

Conclusion: AI开发工具的部署效果不仅取决于工具本身，还深受组织期望、系统规模与开发者技能影响。现有接受模型需纳入感知风险等维度，组织应重视管理层与开发者之间的期望协调，以避免基于片面证据进行决策。

Abstract: AI-enabled software development tools (AI-devtools) are being industrially adopted under strong expectations of productivity gains, yet developers' experiences of such roll-outs are underexplored. Organizations commit budgets, evaluate staff, and revise practice on a partial picture, since the evidence base is mainly tool evaluations, productivity metrics, and surveys, with few qualitative in-situ accounts of ongoing, coordinated roll-outs. We report a case study of a coordinated roll-out of AI-devtools at a large Swedish telecommunications company, investigating how developers experience the roll-out and how they anticipate their profession will change. We conducted semi-structured interviews with 12 software professionals across three sites, analyzed with process coding and thematic analysis, and interpreted through the extended Technology Acceptance Model (TAM2) as a post-hoc analytical lens. Our findings on use cases, productivity, frustrations, and tool limitations corroborate prior survey work. Beyond corroboration, the interviews surface a management-developer expectation gap that maps onto the TAM2 constructs of subjective norm and voluntariness, and show that participants weigh perceived risk heavily, a factor that TAM2 and similar acceptance models do not represent. AI-devtools emerge as helpful but fallible assistants whose value is shaped by organizational expectations, system scale, and developers' skills.

</details>


### [2] [Embedding Drift in Code Vulnerability Models Under Intended Behaviour-Preserving Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21203)
*Hasti Ghaneshirazi,Tahsin Reza,Ladan Tahvildari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究语义保持的代码变换（注释删除、不可达代码插入、变量重命名、循环重写）如何使冻结的代码嵌入跨越分类器决策边界，导致漏洞检测翻转，并提出一种仅用训练集的防御方法来提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义中立的代码编辑不应改变模型的安全判断，但保持程序行为的编辑可能使冻结代码嵌入跨越分类器决策边界，使已正确检测的漏洞被误判为良性，这种不稳定性是亟待解决的问题。

Method: 使用Big-Vul数据集中15,000个C/C++函数，组织为7,500对漏洞-补丁样本。对漏洞和良性样本施加四条变异轨迹：注释删除、不可达代码插入、变量重命名、循环重写，以及组合设置。基于冻结的microsoft/codebert-base嵌入，配合六种下游分类器评估漏洞预测鲁棒性，并提出一种仅用训练集的防御，将干净与变异表示投影得更近，同时保留漏洞类别信息。

Result: 组合变异下，基线漏洞翻转率（VFR）为35.55%–42.15%。所提防御将平均公共集VFR从22.60%降至11.32%，平均变异准确率从55.33%提升至57.10%。对逻辑回归，VFR下降17.29个百分点，但良性到漏洞的翻转增加11.95个百分点。

Conclusion: 该防御提升了模型对语义保持代码变换的鲁棒性，但引入了假阳性权衡，且未能完全消除预测不稳定性。

Abstract: Code edits that preserve program behaviour can shift frozen code embeddings across classifier decision boundaries, causing correctly detected vulnerabilities to be predicted as benign. Such instability is problematic because semantically neutral changes, including comment removal, insertion of unreachable code, variable renaming, and loop rewriting, should not alter a model's security judgment. We investigate this issue using 15,000 C/C++ functions organized as 7,500 vulnerable-patched pairs from the Big-Vul dataset. Four mutation tracks are applied to both vulnerable and benign samples: comment removal, unreachable code insertion, variable renaming, loop rewriting, and a combined setting that applies all transformations. Using frozen microsoft/codebert-base embeddings with six downstream classifiers, we evaluate the robustness of vulnerability predictions and introduce a train-only defence that projects clean and mutated representations closer together while preserving vulnerability-class information. Under combined mutations, the baseline Vulnerable Flip Rate (VFR) ranges from 35.55% to 42.15%. The proposed defence reduces the mean common-set VFR from 22.60% to 11.32% and improves mean mutated accuracy from 55.33% to 57.10%. For Logistic Regression, VFR decreases by 17.29 percentage points, although benign-to-vulnerable flips increase by 11.95 percentage points. These results indicate that the defence improves robustness to semantics-preserving code transformations but introduces a false-positive trade-off and does not fully eliminate prediction instability.

</details>


### [3] [License Compliance in Open Source Cybersecurity Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21218)
*Ahmed Shah,Selman Selman,Ibrahim Abualhaol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究初步分析了200多个开源网络安全项目，识别其常用许可证类型与编程语言，并发现宽松许可项目中被限制性许可代码"污染"的案例，以及大量代码缺失版权归属信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者常将开源软件包集成进商业网络安全产品，集成前需确认许可证是否为宽松型以支持商业友好的继承与再分发。但开源包可能被静默混入限制性许可代码，导致许可证信息失真，进而给商业衍生作品带来销售或保密上的法律障碍，可能造成高昂修复成本、声誉损失及法律费用。

Method: 对200多个开源网络安全项目进行初步实证分析，统计最常用的许可证类型与编程语言，并检索宽松许可项目中可能混入限制性许可材料（即商业不友好代码）的证据。

Result: 在宽松许可的开源项目中确实识别出限制性许可证污染案例，同时发现高比例的代码缺失版权归属信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果可为管理者与开发者揭示污染如何发生，帮助开源社区通过在代码中包含许可证与版权信息更好地保护知识产权，并使创业者理解开源网络安全领域的许可与污染状况及其对软件架构决策的影响。

Abstract: Developers of cybersecurity software often include and rely upon open source software packages in their commercial software products. Before open source code is absorbed into a proprietary product, developers must check the package license to see if the project is permissively licensed, thereby allowing for commercial-friendly inheritance and redistribution. However, there is a risk that the open source package license could be inaccurate due to being silently contaminated with restrictively licensed open source code that may prohibit the sale or confidentiality of commercial derivative work. Contamination of commercial products could lead to expensive remediation costs, damage to the company's reputation, and costly legal fees. In this article, we report on our preliminary analysis of more than 200 open source cybersecurity projects to identify the most frequently used license types and languages and to look for evidence of permissively licensed open source projects that are likely contaminated by restrictive licensed material (i.e., containing commercial-unfriendly code). Our analysis identified restrictive license contamination cases occurring in permissively licensed open source projects. Furthermore, we found a high proportion of code that lacked copyright attribution. We expect that the results of this study will: i) provide managers and developers with an understanding of how contamination can occur, ii) provide open source communities with an understanding on how they can better protect their intellectual property by including licenses and copyright information in their code, and iii) provide entrepreneurs with an understanding of the open source cybersecurity domain in terms of licensing and contamination and how they affect decisions about cybersecurity software architectures.

</details>


### [4] [What Stops a Small Language Model From Driving a Database Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21341)
*Cevheri Bozoglan,Yusuf Gundogdu,Abdullah Kaya,Koray Sirin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对39个本地开源权重模型与1个托管对照模型在生产级SQL客户端agent模式下进行11天、8,199次运行的评测：2,100次模型归因损失中75.7%来自至少调用过一次工具的运行，其中"传输"类（用了工具但未产出交付物）最大（36.2%），"能力"类（完全未调用工具）最小（17.3%）；拒绝码日志缺失掩盖了5个服务端缺陷，修复5处服务端代码（不改模型、提示词与采样设置）使6个模型在30格中变动6–21格；另报告了本地模型基准中因无上下文上限导致的混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界普遍假设小型开放权重模型缺乏推理能力，因而无法胜任agent式数据库工作。作者用一个生产系统直接检验这一假设，并试图厘清agent失败的真实归因（模型能力 vs 系统/工具链缺陷）。

Method: 以开源SQL客户端的agent模式为被测系统，本地部署39个开放权重模型并设1个托管对照，覆盖6类任务面，历时11天共8,199次运行，记录110,711条账本事件与14,008次工具调用拒绝。归因分析采用按模型聚类的重采样（clustered resampling）而非按运行的样本重采样，以规避样本相关性带来的假显著。关键干预：在生产账本仅记录拒绝码、不记录模型参数的基础上，额外捕获模型arguments，并据此定位与修复服务端缺陷，同时保持模型、提示词、采样设置不变。

Result: 2,100次模型归因的agent模式损失中1,590次（75.7%）来自至少调用过一次工具的运行；该多数结论在99.7%的聚类重采样中成立，并在22个损失≥20的模型中有15个成立，说明它是对模型稳健而非对运行稳健的效应。"传输"类占36.2%（最大），"能力"类占17.3%（最小），但该排序按模型聚类仅在74.5%的重采样中成立，故仅作为本语料性质报告。传输失败可分解为少数机械性参数形态。捕获arguments后暴露5个服务端缺陷（其一为某工具要求某字段、其组合的兄弟工具禁止该字段，却因字段缺失而判该运行失败）。5处服务端改动（不触及模型、提示词、采样设置）使6个模型在30格中变动6–21格。另报告一个影响已发表本地模型基准的混淆：无上下文上限时，一个7.1 GB模型被允许使用其全部262,144 token窗口，在64 GB机器上占用51 GB，产生的运行在常规日志中与"模型超时"无法区分。

Conclusion: 小型开放权重模型在agent式数据库任务上的失败并非常规意义上的能力不足，其损失多数发生在已成功调用工具的运行中，且相当部分可归因于服务端与工具链缺陷而非模型本身；修复少量服务端逻辑即可在不改变模型与解码设置的条件下显著改善结果门类。作者据此质疑"小模型做不了agent工作"的通行叙事，并警示本地模型基准中上下文上限缺失会与超时混淆。语料、评分器与可复现全部图表/数值的验证器均已开源。

Abstract: Small open-weight language models are assumed to fail at agentic database work because they lack the reasoning capacity for it. We test that against a production system. Over eleven days we drove the agent mode of an open-source SQL client with 39 open-weight models served locally and one hosted control, across six task surfaces: 8,199 runs, 110,711 ledger events, 14,008 refused tool calls. Of the 2,100 model-attributed agent-mode losses, 1,590, or 75.7%, came from runs that had invoked at least one tool. That majority is what survives resampling models rather than runs: it holds in 99.7% of clustered resamples and in 15 of the 22 models with at least twenty losses. Within it, transport, a run that used the tools and never got a deliverable through, is the largest class at 36.2% and capability, a run that invoked no tool at all, the smallest at 17.3%; we report that ordering as a property of this corpus rather than a general finding, since clustered by model it holds in only 74.5% of resamples. Transport failures decompose into a few mechanical argument shapes. Production ledgers record refusal codes and never the model's arguments, so these were invisible for ten days; capturing them exposed five server defects, one of which demanded a field on one tool, forbade it on the sibling that composed it, then failed the run for its absence. Five server changes, touching no model, prompt or sampling setting, moved six models by 6 to 21 cells out of 30. We also report a confound we believe affects published local-model benchmarks, ours included: with no context cap, one 7.1 GB model was admitted at its full 262,144-token window and held 51 GB on a 64 GB machine, producing runs indistinguishable in any ordinary log from a model timing out. The corpus, the scorer and a verifier that regenerates every figure are released.

</details>


### [5] [Cross-Platform vs Native Mobile Development: An Empirical Study of Software Quality Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21544)
*Alexandru Ilovan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对同一植物管理应用的五种实现（原生 iOS、原生 Android、Flutter、React Native、Kotlin Multiplatform，共 8 个可执行变体）进行基准比较，依据 ISO/IEC 25010 评估时间行为、实现规模、源码组织与渲染响应性，结论是各方案的优劣取决于质量属性、工作负载、平台与测量边界，不存在普适的最优技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨平台移动框架宣称可实现代码复用、缩短交付周期、降低实现工作量，但其相对原生开发的权衡难以客观评估。现有比较多依赖简化应用、不一致的功能集或狭窄的指标集合，缺乏可比性与全面性，因此需要一个受控、共享基准的实证对比研究。

Method: 采用同一植物管理应用的 5 种实现（原生 iOS、原生 Android、Flutter、React Native、Kotlin Multiplatform），跨平台方案同时面向 Android 与 iOS，共 8 个可执行变体。各实现共享相同的领域模型、后端服务、功能需求与基准契约，遵循 ISO/IEC 25010 质量模型，考察时间行为、实现规模（footprint）、源码组织与可观测渲染响应性。数据集包含每个变体 2000 次完整运行，覆盖已认证与缓存检索、图像传输与解码、列表渲染与滚动、本地同步、媒体上传等场景；后端准备与框架特定的 UI 驱动与主客户端工作流分开分析。

Result: 在两个操作系统上，原生实现的非 UI 客户端耗时小计最低；Kotlin Multiplatform 是最接近原生表现的跨平台方案；操作级与 UI 封装级的排名随任务而变化。跨平台方案的自编移动端源码总量少于两个原生应用之和，源码清单显示各方案在文件规模、组织方式与依赖使用上呈现不同模式。

Conclusion: 研究并未识别出普遍更优的技术，而是表明每种方案的优势与代价取决于所关注的质量属性、工作负载、目标平台与测量边界。

Abstract: Cross-platform mobile frameworks promise code reuse, shorter delivery cycles, and lower implementation effort, but their trade-offs relative to native development remain difficult to assess objectively. Many comparisons rely on simplified applications, inconsistent feature sets, or a narrow set of metrics. This paper compares five implementations of the same plant-management application: native iOS, native Android, Flutter, React Native, and Kotlin Multiplatform. The shared approaches target both Android and iOS, yielding eight executable variants. Guided by ISO/IEC 25010, the study examines time behavior, implementation footprint, source-code organization, and observable rendering responsiveness.
  All implementations share the same domain, backend services, functional requirements, and benchmark contract. The supplied dataset contains 2,000 completed runs per variant and covers authenticated and cached retrieval, image transfer and decoding, list rendering and scrolling, local synchronization, and media upload. Backend preparation and framework-specific UI drivers are analyzed separately from the primary client workflow.
  Native records the lowest non-UI client subtotal on both operating systems, with Kotlin Multiplatform the closest cross-platform implementation, while operation and UI-wrapper rankings vary by task. The shared approaches contain less authored mobile source than the native applications combined, and the source inventory shows different patterns of file size, organization, and dependency use. Rather than identifying a universally superior technology, the study shows that each approach's advantages and costs depend on the quality attribute, workload, platform, and measurement boundary.

</details>


### [6] [GameLogicBench: Evaluating Coding Agents on Runtime Game Logic with Tick-Level State Assertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21562)
*Xinyu Che,Yunfei Ge,Shihao Li,Yanchen Liu,Hang Yan,Xinping Lei,Yanghai Wang,Zixuan Dong,Yifan Yao,Qianqian Xie,Letian Zhu,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 GameLogicBench：72 个 Godot 玩法逻辑任务，自动化评测器逐 tick 校验规则，含 403 场景/1451 用例，并验证评测器对不同正确实现的接受度与对变异体的拒绝度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体需在大型软件项目中修改与测试代码，游戏开发要求其实现玩法规则，且游戏即使运行中违反规则也可能以“有效状态”结束。现有基准多为回放固定样例、评分视频或让另一模型裁判，缺少在评测器选定场景中贯穿执行、逐时检查规则且判分完全可复现的基准。

Method: 构建 GameLogicBench：72 个 Godot 项目玩法逻辑任务；自动化评测器在每个模拟 tick 检查游戏规则；403 个手工设计场景经带种子参数变化生成 1451 个测试用例；为区分行为与实现，评测器须接受同一任务的不同正确实现，同时拒绝“变异体”（移除某项必要能力的实现）；任务覆盖孤立机制、多系统交互与仓库级特性；并用变异体验证评测器。

Result: 在 20 种语言模型与脚手架组合中，最佳运行解决 52.78% 任务；Claude Code 下 12 个模型随任务范围从孤立机制扩展到交互系统、再到仓库级特性，解决数下降；仓库级任务中智能体更频繁检查代码、工具调用更多；多数失败提交可运行但某项必需玩法行为实现错误；未用变异体验证的评测器版本会让错误提交通过；开放网络时智能体从公开仓库复制代码。

Conclusion: 可靠评测不仅取决于测试拒绝什么，也取决于智能体可访问哪些外部代码；需以变异体验证评测器，并考虑网络访问对评估的污染。

Abstract: Coding agents can modify and test code across large software projects. Game development is a domain where agents must implement gameplay rules. A game can end in a valid state even after violating its rules during the run. Current game-development benchmarks replay fixed examples, score videos, or ask another model to judge the result. However, no existing benchmark checks game rules throughout execution across varied evaluator-selected scenarios while ensuring exactly reproducible verdicts. We introduce GameLogicBench, a benchmark of 72 gameplay-logic tasks in Godot projects. An automated evaluator checks each game's rules at every simulation tick. Across 403 hand-designed scenarios, seeded parameter variations produce 1,451 test cases. To ensure that the evaluator measures behavior rather than implementation choice, it must accept different correct implementations for each task while rejecting mutants, implementations with one required capability removed. The tasks span isolated mechanics, multi-system interactions, and repository-scale features. Across 20 combinations of language models and scaffolds, the best observed run solves 52.78% of tasks. Under Claude Code, all twelve models solve fewer tasks as task scope expands from isolated mechanics, through interacting systems, to repository-scale features. Agents inspect code more often and make more tool calls on repository-scale tasks than on isolated-mechanic tasks. Most unsuccessful submissions are runnable, but implement some required game behavior incorrectly. We compared versions of our benchmark evaluator built with and without validation using mutants. Without this validation, incorrect agent submissions passed. A separate analysis finds agents copying code from public repositories when network access is open. Reliable evaluation thus depends both on what the tests reject and on what external code agents can access.

</details>


### [7] [Labelling Bug-Fixing Commits with Local Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21616)
*Philip König,Georg Goldenits,Caroline König,Sebastian Raubitzek,Fabian Obermann,Dennis Toth,David Schmidt,Edgar Weippl,Kevin Mallinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究能否仅凭提交内容（提交信息+代码差异）用开源权重语言模型标注缺陷修复提交。在人工验证的Java、Python、JavaScript语料上，关键词基线召回不足半数，而开源权重模型能召回绝大多数，逐仓库显著优于基线，且模型规模与效果无一致正相关；同时指出无负例的评测语料无法支持考虑精确率的比较，并发布标注流水线与多语言银标准语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺陷预测依赖"哪些提交修复了缺陷"这一标签，但现有标签获取途径均引入噪声：复用基准存在已记录的数据质量问题，问题跟踪器链接有偏且报告常被错误分类，提交信息关键词匹配只是粗糙启发式。因此需要一种不依赖问题跟踪器、可复现、低成本、可用于专有代码的标注方法。

Method: 以人工验证与整理的缺陷修复数据集（覆盖Java、Python、JavaScript）为基准，将关键词基线与一组成员开源权重（可本地运行）的语言模型进行对比；对每个模型输入提交信息与代码差异进行提示，按仓库逐一比较，并进行统计显著性检验；同时分析语料中负例对精确率评估的必要性。

Result: 在人工验证语料上，关键词基线只能召回不到一半的修复，开源权重模型则召回绝大多数，并在每个仓库上都以统计显著优势超过基线；模型规模更大并不稳定带来更好效果。此外，缺少负例的评测语料无法支撑对这类分类器进行考虑精确率的比较。

Conclusion: 仅凭提交内容即可用本地开源权重语言模型实现较高质量的缺陷修复标注，克服了对问题跟踪器和关键词启发式的依赖；研究同时强调评测语料需包含负例。作者发布了标注流水线及按推荐配置生成的多语言标注语料，作为可复现的银标准资源，用于构建当前项目特定的数据集。

Abstract: Defect prediction depends on knowing which commits fix bugs, yet the labels that encode this are produced by routes that each introduce noise. Reused benchmarks carry documented data-quality problems, issue-tracker links are biased and the underlying reports are frequently mistyped, and matching keywords in commit messages is a coarse heuristic. This paper examines whether commits can be labelled as bug fixes from their content alone, using open-weight language models that run locally and therefore keep the process reproducible, inexpensive at corpus scale, usable on proprietary code, and independent of any issue tracker. Against datasets of manually validated and curated bug fixes spanning Java, Python, and JavaScript, we compare a keyword baseline with a set of open-weight models of varying size, prompting each with the commit message and the code diff. On the manually validated corpus the keyword baseline recovers fewer than half of the fixes, whereas the open-weight models recover the large majority and outperform it repository by repository with statistical significance, and larger models do not consistently outperform smaller ones. We further show that evaluation corpora without negative examples cannot support a precision-aware comparison of such classifiers. We release the labelling pipeline together with a labelled, multi-language corpus produced by the recommended configuration, as a reproducible silver-standard resource for building current, project-specific datasets.

</details>


### [8] [From Smarter to Hungrier: the Role of Energy Efficiency in Software-defined Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21643)
*Ella Peltonen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文探讨软件定义汽车发展对可持续性和环境伦理的威胁，强调需要在引入高耗能软硬件与可持续性之间取得平衡，并呼吁开发测量、管理和优化能耗的新工具与设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代个人车辆中的自动化功能与高级驾驶辅助系统带来了安全与舒适应用，但车辆从机械实体向软件定义设备演进，导致传感器复杂度、算力和网络能力提升，进而运行更复杂算法，使车辆更智能的同时也需要更多能耗。因此，作者关注这一发展对可持续性及环境伦理构成的威胁。

Method: 本文属于讨论/立场性研究，通过分析软件定义车辆发展趋势，讨论引入更多高资源消耗软硬件组件与其对社会和可持续性影响之间的平衡关系。

Result: 论文指出软件定义汽车的智能化发展会显著增加能耗与资源需求，对可持续性和环境伦理形成威胁，并强调现有工具不足以应对该问题。

Conclusion: 作者强调需要新型工具来测量、管理和优化能耗及其他可持续性指标，并提供设计指导与可操作建议，以在推进软件定义汽车的同时维护环境伦理与可持续性。

Abstract: Automated features and advanced driving support systems have enabled a range of life-saving and comfort applications in modern personal vehicles. Development from mostly mechanical entities towards truly software-defined devices also means that added sensor complexity, processing power, and networking capabilities provide a platform for running even more complex algorithms and applications. As vehicles become smarter, they require even more energy to enable advanced machine learning- and artificial intelligence-based functionalities. This paper discusses the implications for sustainability and the threats to environmental ethics posed by the development of software-defined vehicles. We discuss the balance between introducing more resource-hungry software and hardware components and their effect on society and sustainability. We underscore the need for novel tools to measure, manage, and optimise energy consumption and other sustainability indicators, with design guidance and actionable recommendations.

</details>


### [9] [Supporting Industrial Test-Failure Analysis with LLM-Based Systems: An Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21843)
*Eric Jansson,Per Strandberg,Thomas Sörensen,Eduard Paul Enoiu,Wasif Afzal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于工具增强的大语言模型系统，用于Westermo公司夜间测试失败的根因分析（RCA），比较了单智能体与编排式多智能体配置，并由六位从业者评估场景报告、结合120次重复执行的运行指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夜间测试产生异构的测试数据与日志，从业者目前需跨多个来源手工排查，效率低且依赖人工经验，因此需要探索工具增强的LLM/RCA工作流来支持根因分析。

Method: 在Westermo实现单智能体与编排式多智能体两种RCA工作流，均可访问测试元数据与日志；以两个真实失败场景开展探索性工业案例研究；六位从业者通过问卷与焦点小组评估报告，并从120次重复执行中采集运行测量指标（成本、时长、一致性等）。

Result: 在感知正确性、推理质量、修复现实性、清晰度、有用性与信任度方面，两种配置在场景间均未表现出一致的从业者感知质量优势；单智能体生成报告更快、成本更低；120次重复执行提供了成本、时长与一致性的运行数据。

Conclusion: 单智能体系统在成本与速度上更优，是当前场景下更实用的基线方案；多智能体架构的潜在收益需在更复杂场景中进一步评估。

Abstract: This study examines tool-augmented Large Language Model (LLM) systems for supporting Root Cause Analysis (RCA) of nightly test failures at Westermo Network Technologies AB. Nightly test executions produce heterogeneous test data and logs that practitioners currently inspect manually across multiple sources. We implemented an RCA workflow in single-agent and orchestrated multi-agent configurations, both with access to test metadata and logs. An exploratory industrial case study used two real failure scenarios. Six practitioners evaluated the scenario reports through a survey and focus group, and operational measurements were collected from 120 repeated executions. The evaluation covered practitioner-perceived correctness, reasoning quality, fix realism, clarity, usefulness, and trust, as well as cost, duration, and consistency. Neither configuration showed a consistent practitioner-perceived quality advantage across the two scenarios. The single agent system generated reports faster and at lower cost, making it the more practical baseline in this context. The potential benefits of agent architectures require further evaluation in more complex scenarios.

</details>


### [10] [How Researchers Use and Verify AI Coding Assistants: Tasks and Validation Practices in Scientific Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22049)
*Gabrielle O'Brien,Reed Milewicz,Nasir Eisty*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 基于2025年527份研究者自由文本调查，梳理生成式AI在科研编程中的五类主要用途及非正式评估方式，发现评估依赖个人判断，编程经验影响信任倾向而非评估策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI已进入科研编程实践，但缺乏关于研究者将哪些任务交由AI处理、以及如何判断生成代码正确性的实证证据。

Method: 对2025年调查中527份自由文本回答进行编码，样本多为美国大学写代码的研究者；每条回答描述一个具体任务、AI使用方式与结果评估行为，并将任务类型与评估策略同编程经验、研究领域和信心评分进行关联分析。

Result: 用途集中于五类任务：数据处理、可视化、调试、数学/科学计算、统计分析。评估方式非正式且个体化：过半仅描述运行生成代码，自动化测试与他人复核罕见。用途与评估策略随编程经验变化很小，但信心有差异——经验较少者更信任AI而非自己，经验丰富者相反。评估信心与所报策略无关，其最强相关因素是工具信心与自我信心。

Conclusion: 科研代码中AI贡献的验证主要依赖个人判断，缺乏共享的测试与评审基础设施；界面设计可支持与任务相匹配的评估，而非将评估负担完全留给用户。

Abstract: Generative AI has entered research programming, yet there is little evidence about which tasks researchers hand to it or how they decide whether its code is correct. We draw on 527 free-text responses to a 2025 survey of researchers who write code, most of them at U.S. universities. In each response, a researcher recounts a single task from their own work, the way they used an AI tool for it, and what they did to assess the result. We coded the task and the evaluation strategies reported, and related both to programming experience, research area, and confidence ratings. Use was concentrated in five tasks: data handling, visualization, debugging, mathematical/scientific computing, and statistical analysis. Evaluation was informal and individual. Over half of accounts described running the generated code, while automated tests and review by another person were rare. Use cases and evaluation strategies varied little with programming experience, but confidence did: Less experienced programmers trusted the AI more than themselves, and experienced programmers the reverse. Evaluation confidence was not associated with the strategies reported. Its strongest correlates were confidence in the tool and in oneself. Validating AI contributions to scientific code rested largely on individual judgment, outside shared infrastructure for testing or review. Interfaces could support task-appropriate evaluation rather than leave it to the user.

</details>

### [11] [RBS-Attention: Radius-Bounded Sparse Prefill for Long-Context Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20971)
*Chuxu Song,Jiuqi Wei,Zhencan Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练稀疏预填充方法RBS-Attention，通过对质心基分支与半径救援分支双路选择解决"均值稀释"问题，在H100上实现20.65倍注意力加速和5.97倍端到端首token加速，同时保持接近稠密注意力的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理的主要瓶颈在于prefill阶段：生成开始前，稠密自注意力需处理整个prompt。稀疏块选择虽能降低开销，但单个块的质心（均值）可能掩盖块内个别高度相关的token，导致真正相关的块被低估而漏选。作者将这一失效模式命名为"均值稀释"（mean dilution），需要一种选择机制来补救被均值掩盖的关键块。

Method: RBS-Attention是一种免训练的稀疏prefill方法，含两条互补的选择分支：1）质心基分支，捕捉块的平均相关性；2）救援分支，利用最大key块半径及其依赖于prompt、层、头的分布，识别有被低估风险的块。两条分支各自独立阈值化，再将其mask组合，从而控制救援块的贡献，同时保持常规的块稀疏FlashAttention执行。

Result: 在H100 GPU上，RBS-Attention在Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8、128K上下文下实现20.65倍独立prefill注意力加速、11.92倍vLLM prefill注意力加速、5.97倍端到端首token时间（TTFT）加速。在稠密Qwen3-32B上，其RULER总精度为88.65，稠密注意力为89.52；并通过LongBench-v2、InfiniteBench和Video-MME提供额外质量评估。支持性实验测量了实际保留率、在匹配密度下比较选择器，并刻画了块大小、阈值与内存行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，半径自适应的双分支选择是长上下文prefill的一种有效方案，能在显著加速prefill注意力的同时，维持与稠密注意力接近的生成质量。

Abstract: Long-context large language model inference is increasingly limited by prefill, where dense self-attention processes the entire prompt before generation begins. Sparse block selection can reduce this cost, but a block centroid may hide a highly relevant token among many irrelevant ones. We call this failure mode mean dilution and propose RBS-Attention, a training-free sparse-prefill method with two complementary selection branches. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescue branch uses the maximum key-block radius and its prompt-, layer-, and head-dependent distribution to identify blocks at risk of underestimation. Independently thresholding the two branches and combining their masks controls the contribution of rescue blocks while preserving regular block-sparse FlashAttention execution. On H100 GPUs, RBS-Attention achieves 20.65$\times$ standalone prefill-attention speedup, 11.92$\times$ vLLM prefill-attention speedup, and 5.97$\times$ end-to-end time-to-first-token speedup at 128K on Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8. On the dense Qwen3-32B model, it obtains 88.65 overall RULER accuracy versus 89.52 for dense attention; LongBench-v2, InfiniteBench, and Video-MME provide additional quality evaluation. Supporting experiments measure actual retention, compare selectors at matched density, and characterize block-size, threshold, and memory behavior. Together, these results support radius-adaptive dual-branch selection as an effective approach to long-context prefill.

</details>


### [12] [Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20974)
*Despoina Kosmopoulou,Anastasios Tsetsilas,Efthymios Georgiou,Giannis Karamanolakis,Swastik Roy,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Attention-Aware Routing (AAR)，用滑动窗口注意力权重提取的时序与频谱特征增强 MoE 路由器，仅训练路由参数即可提升推理性能并揭示路由-注意力耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 路由仅依赖 token 隐状态等有限上下文信息进行专家选择与加权，缺乏对模型上下文状态的显式利用。

Method: 将滑动窗口内注意力权重所表征的上下文摘要（与隐状态解耦）提取为时序与频谱特征，用于增强路由器；冻结基础 Transformer，仅训练路由参数，从而隔离路由这一变量。在 OLMoE 上验证。

Result: GSM8K 相比仅路由 SFT 基线提升 +3.37 pp；路由与注意力构成耦合回路，第 l 层路由变化经残差流传播放大第 l+1 层注意力汇（attention sink）；减少长发散生成，错误答案变短而正确答案长度不变；具有强深度敏感性，全层无差别应用会损害事实检索，而深层引入可保持数学推理增益。

Conclusion: AAR 揭示跨深度的检索-推理张力，使层选择性 AAR 成为探测不同层注意力所携带路由相关信息的受控手段。

Abstract: In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, isolating routing as the sole variable. AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline on OLMoE. Beyond performance, we show that routing and attention form a coupled circuit: routing changes at layer l propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks at layer l+1, reshaping attention without any direct update to the attention mechanism itself. Further, AAR reduces long diverging generation, with incorrect answers getting shorter, while correct answers remain unchanged in length. Finally, AAR is strongly depth-sensitive: applying it indiscriminately across layers can degrade factual retrieval, whereas mathematical reasoning gains persist when it is introduced deeper in the network. This sensitivity exposes a retrieval--reasoning tension across depth and makes layer-selective AAR a controlled probe of the routing-relevant information carried by attention at different layers.

</details>


### [13] [CaLR: Causal Latent Revision for Robust Diffusion Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20981)
*Wei Cai,Jian Zhao,Yuchen Yuan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CaLR 将推理重构为受约束的潜空间优化，结合因果拓扑矩阵与隐式微分实现梯度引导的“思维修正”，兼顾自回归模型的因果结构与扩散模型的并行生成优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归（AR）模型存在局部贪心问题，而扩散语言模型（DLM）缺乏推理所需的严格因果结构。作者希望融合二者优势、克服各自缺陷。

Method: 提出 Causal Latent Revision（CaLR）框架，将推理形式化为受约束的潜变量优化；从专家模型采用因果拓扑矩阵（CTM），并利用隐式微分执行梯度引导的“思维修正”，以强制逻辑一致性，从而在并行生成过程中对中间步骤进行动态自我纠正。

Result: 在复杂基准上，CaLR 达到 DLM 的 SOTA 表现，超越强 AR 基线，并在诸多数独等受约束任务中展现出更优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CaLR 通过因果约束与潜空间优化的结合，有效整合了 AR 与 DLM 的优点，验证了动态自我纠错机制在推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) models suffer from local greediness, while diffusion language models (DLMs) often lack the strict causal structure required for reasoning. To combine the advantages and overcome the drawbacks of the dual, we propose Causal Latent Revision (CaLR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as constrained latent optimization. By adopting a causal topology matrix (CTM) from an expert model and implicit differentiation, CaLR performs gradient-guided ``thought revision" to enforce logical consistency, enabling dynamic self-correction of intermediate steps during parallel generation. Empirically, CaLR achieves SOTA DLM performance on complex benchmarks, surpassing strong AR baselines and demonstrating superior robustness in constrained tasks like Sudoku.

</details>


### [14] [LoRA Enhanced Contrastive Learning with SAS Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21061)
*Dan Zimmerman,Frank E. Bobe,Amelia L. McCormack,Matthew Cook,Gregory D. Vetaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将DINOv3 ViT模型适配到水下合成孔径声呐自动目标识别任务，采用三阶段参数高效框架，发现仅LoRA适配阶段有效，后续精炼阶段无增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成孔径声呐自动目标识别对海军能力有重要价值，但深度学习受限于目标图像稀缺、背景杂波强以及需要人工参与评估。

Method: 三阶段参数高效框架：Stage 1冻结ViT主干并用LoRA桥接自然图像预训练与水下声学传播差异；Stage 2用难负样本挖掘增强对岩石、沉积物等声学伪目标的决策边界；Stage 3用监督对比学习分离目标与杂波表征。在海上SAS数据上采用任务级地理划分评估，在85%测试召回率下比较各方案，并重复三次随机种子。

Result: LoRA贡献主要效果，将AUPRC从0.300提升至0.679±0.027（同一冻结主干）；Rank 4仅训练0.26%权重即达此结果。两个精炼阶段均未超过匹配对照组：难负样本挖掘相对等规模随机课程AUPRC变化为-0.0045±0.0119，SupCon相对前一阶段变化为+0.0002±0.0096。

Conclusion: 这些零结果表明挖掘发生在编码器已拟合的数据上，且监督阶段已施加了大部分目标-杂波几何结构。单一高效适配阶段已足够，堆叠精炼并不必要。

Abstract: Automatic target recognition (ATR) with synthetic aperture sonar (SAS) supports advanced naval capabilities, but deep learning is constrained by scarce target imagery, background clutter, and human-in-the-loop assessment. We adapt DINOv3 Vision Transformer (ViT) models to underwater SAS ATR using a three-stage parameter-efficient framework. Stage 1 uses Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) while freezing the ViT backbone, bridging the gap between natural-image pretraining and underwater acoustic propagation. Stage 2 uses hard-negative mining to strengthen the decision boundary against acoustic mimics, including rocks and sediment formations resembling man-made targets. Stage 3 uses Supervised Contrastive Learning (SupCon) to separate target and clutter representations. We evaluate at-sea SAS data using a mission-level geographic split, compare all arms at 85 percent test recall, and repeat each comparison over three random seeds. LoRA accounts for the primary effect, increasing area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) from 0.300 to 0.679 +/- 0.027 using the same frozen backbone. Rank 4 achieves this result while training only 0.26 percent of weights. Neither refinement stage exceeds its matched control: hard-negative mining changes AUPRC by -0.0045 +/- 0.0119 versus an equal-size random curriculum, and SupCon changes AUPRC by +0.0002 +/- 0.0096 versus the preceding stage. These null results indicate that mining occurred on data the encoder had already fit and that supervised stages had already imposed most target-clutter geometry. One efficient adaptation stage is sufficient; stacked refinement is not.

</details>


### [15] [Detecting Hallucination in LLMs: Tracing the Topological Signatures of Impaired Context Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21096)
*Amir Jalilifard,Anderson Rocha,Eric Wong,Marcos Medeiros Raimundo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析注意力图中的信息流拓扑结构，利用Forman-Ricci曲率识别信息瓶颈，提出一种单遍（single-pass）幻觉检测方法，在多个LLM和基准上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分大语言模型（LLM）的幻觉响应与非幻觉响应是重要问题，现有基于注意力的方法仍有改进空间。作者希望从注意力图的信息流拓扑结构这一新视角来刻画幻觉，从而更有效地检测幻觉。

Method: 分析注意力图的Forman-Ricci曲率以识别表征信息瓶颈的结构模式；提出一种同时捕捉与幻觉响应相关的注意力头的半局部（semi-local）与全局（global）信息流特征的方法，为单遍（single-pass）检测方案。

Result: 在多个LLM和公开基准上广泛评估。单遍方法在两个幻觉检测基准上一致优于现有基于注意力和多响应（multi-response）的基线，并在多种LLM架构上取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 因果生成过程中token间上下文共享受损与LLM幻觉密切相关。幻觉响应一致表现为对自注意力的过度依赖、从早期token的上下文检索弥散，或信息过度挤压（information over-squashing），尤其在最后一个transformer层。

Abstract: In this work, we examine the topology of information flow patterns within attention graphs to effectively distinguish hallucinated from non-hallucinated responses. We analyze the Forman-Ricci curvature to identify structural patterns indicating information bottlenecks in attention graphs. We then introduce a method that captures both semi-local and global information-flow characteristics of attention heads associated with hallucinated responses. We evaluate our approach extensively across several LLMs and established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed single-pass approach provides consistent improvements over existing attention-based and multi-response baselines across two hallucination-detection benchmarks, while achieving competitive performance across diverse LLM architectures. Further analysis reveals that impaired context sharing among tokens during causal generation is strongly associated with hallucination occurrences in LLMs. In particular, hallucinated responses are consistently characterized by an over-reliance on self-attention, diffused context retrieval from earlier tokens, or information over-squashing, especially in the final transformer layer.

</details>


### [16] [AutoRecLab: Describe the Experiment, Get the Code!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21863)
*Moritz Baumgart,Philipp Meister,Justus Krell,Michael Schmidt,Bela Gipp,Joeran Beel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoRecLab 是一个基于 Python 的自主推荐系统实验平台，可将自然语言研究想法自动转化为可执行实验代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统研究高度依赖实证评估，但将实验设计转化为可执行代码仍依赖人工，过程繁琐且易出错，亟需自动化手段降低门槛与错误率。

Method: 提出 AutoRecLab：从自然语言提示出发，推导显式实验需求，构建并验证原型，再迭代扩展为完整实验；工作流结合 RAG 文档检索、静态类型校验与执行引导的树搜索。

Result: 在演示中自主实现了显式反馈到隐式反馈转换研究；在 6 种算法、3 个数据集的基线对比中，9 次运行有 8 次成功，使用 GPT-5.4-mini 时平均每次运行成本约 1 美元。

Conclusion: AutoRecLab 表明自然语言驱动的自主实验流程在推荐系统研究中具备可行性与较低成本，有望减少人工编码负担并提升实验可复现性。

Abstract: Empirical evaluation is central to recommender-systems (RecSys) research, but turning experimental designs into executable code remains a manual and error-prone task. We present AutoRecLab, a Python-based autonomous RecSys lab that automates RecSys experiments from natural-language prompts. Given a research idea, AutoRecLab derives explicit experiment requirements, builds and validates a prototype, and iteratively expands it into the requested full experiment. The workflow combines retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for documentation lookup, static type verification, and execution-steered tree search. In our demonstration, AutoRecLab autonomously implements an explicit-to-implicit feedback conversion study. In a baseline comparison across six algorithms and three datasets, 8 of 9 runs succeed at an average cost of approx- imately $1 per run with GPT-5.4-mini.

</details>


### [17] [Decoupling Internal Representational Changes and Causal Importance in Fine-Tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21113)
*Lingfang Li,Procheta Sen,Shubham Das,Danushka Bollegala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨微调如何改变LLM内部表征（注意力模式与逐层激活），并考察这些变化是否与EAP识别的任务关键组件相关，发现EAP组件集中于特定层、其层级分布与表征变化最大的层基本无关，且跨任务组件重叠不必然带来迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调已成为将LLM适配下游任务的通行方法，但其如何重塑模型内部机制仍缺乏理解。本文旨在揭示微调对内部表征的影响，并检验这些影响是否与驱动任务表现的EAP识别组件（注意力头与logit级激活）存在关联。

Method: 分析微调前后LLM的内部表征，包括注意力模式与逐层激活；利用EAP（基于激活的归因/路径方法）识别任务关键组件（注意力头、logit级激活）；比较EAP组件的层级分布与微调中表征变化最大的层；并考察不同任务间EAP组件的重叠及其与跨任务性能迁移的关系。

Result: EAP识别的组件集中在特定层，表明模型在内部化任务特定行为时存在一定程度的功�能局部化；但这些组件的层级分布与微调中发生最显著表征变化的层基本不相关；跨任务的EAP组件重叠并不转化为跨任务性能迁移（当任务性质不同，如分类与生成任务时）；更突出的是，当一个任务上的微调与另一任务在EAP识别组件上高度重叠时，会导致后者性能下降。

Conclusion: 微调带来的内部表征变化与EAP识别的任务关键组件在层级上并不对齐，且组件重叠不能预测正向迁移，甚至可能导致负迁移，提示EAP局部分析与表征层面的变化需区分看待，并对基于组件共享的多任务/持续微调策略提出警示。

Abstract: Fine-tuning has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting LLMs to a variety of downstream tasks. However, how it reshapes their internal mechanisms remains poorly understood. To address this, we investigate how fine-tuning alters internal representations in LLMs, including attention patterns and layer-wise activations, and examine whether these changes are linked to task-relevant components identified by EAP (e.g., attention heads and logit-level activations) that drive task performance. We find that EAP-identified components are concentrated within specific layers, indicating a degree of functional localisation in how models internalise task-specific behavior. Notably, the distribution of these components across layers is largely uncorrelated with the layers undergoing the most substantial representational changes during fine-tuning. Furthermore, we observe that overlap in EAP-identified components across tasks does not translate into cross-task performance transfer if the tasks are different in nature (e.g. classification vs. generative tasks). More specifically, fine-tuning on one task can lead to a degradation of performance on another when the two tasks exhibit a high degree of overlap in their EAP-identified components.

</details>


### [18] [TinyCeNN-LM: Quality-Gated Conversion of Pretrained Attention with CeNN-Inspired Cellular-Recurrent Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21139)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TinyCeNN-LM，一种质量门控的注意力后训练转换框架，用类 CeNN 的细胞递归层替换预训练语言模型中的注意力，并以表示保真度与 NLL 阈值决定接受或回退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型中的注意力替换本质上是兼容性问题：看似合理的替代结构可能改变后续层所期望的表示。因此需要一种保守、可控、可验证的转换机制，而非宣称普遍的注意力替换或加速。

Method: 采用质量门控后训练转换（quality-gated post-training conversion）框架，引入受 CeNN 启发的细胞递归层，具备有界局部处理、紧凑递归记忆、路由、融合以及接受或回滚验证机制。研究三种实现：Integrated Memory、MemoryFusion 和 PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32。严格 PDelta3 转换仅在表示与 NLL 标准同时通过固定阈值时接受该层。

Result: 在 SmolLM2-135M 上，第 0-2 层被接受，累计 ΔNLL=+0.01209；第 3 层因表示保真度不达标被拒绝，尽管 NLL 可接受。在 Qwen3.5-0.8B 上，全注意力层 3、7、11 被接受，最终 ΔNLL=+0.02073。Integrated Memory 将困惑度变化控制在 -0.07% 至 +0.93% 之间，同时将总缓存最多减少 6.01%。抽样 200 项下游合理性检查显示，转换后的 Qwen 版本整体准确率为 28.5%–32.0%。

Conclusion: 结果支持保守的、质量门控的结构转换，而非普遍性的注意力替换或加速；转换的可行性依赖于逐层验证的表示与 NLL 双重标准。

Abstract: Replacing attention in a pretrained language model is a compatibility problem: a plausible substitute may alter representations expected by later layers. TinyCeNN-LM introduces a \emph{quality-gated post-training conversion} framework using CeNN-inspired cellular-recurrent layers with bounded local processing, compact recurrent memory, routing, fusion, and accept-or-rollback validation. Three implementations are studied: Integrated Memory, MemoryFusion, and PDelta3-GDN2-CLVR+Local32. Strict PDelta3 conversion accepts a layer only when representation and NLL criteria pass fixed thresholds. On SmolLM2-135M, layers 0-2 are accepted with cumulative $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.01209$, while layer 3 is rejected despite acceptable NLL because representation fidelity fails. On Qwen3.5-0.8B, full-attention layers 3, 7, and 11 are accepted with final $Δ\mathrm{NLL}=+0.02073$. Integrated Memory keeps perplexity within $-0.07\%$ to $+0.93\%$ while reducing total cache by up to $6.01\%$. A sampled 200-item downstream sanity check gives $28.5\%$--$32.0\%$ overall accuracy for converted Qwen releases. The results support conservative, quality-gated structural conversion rather than universal attention replacement or speedup.

</details>


### [19] [Clinician-Grounded Quality Assurance for AI-Assisted Psychiatric Intake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21149)
*King Shi,Amanda Li,Jonathan Ivey,Synthia Qia Wang,Guan Gui,Hyunseo Kim,Peter Zandi,Jason Straub,Jacob Taylor,Ananya Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 InterviewPlayground，一套以临床医生为基准、基于记忆增强患者模拟器的开放式 AI 问诊评估平台，用于对 AI 辅助精神科接诊系统进行常规质量保证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 AI 辅助精神科接诊系统投入临床使用前，卫生系统需要可常规执行的评估手段，以对照其临床质量标准。由于临床医生问诊风格各异，评估须满足三点：支持跨问诊方式比较、尽量减轻医生负担、并测量对部署方具有临床相关性的表现指标。

Method: 构建以记忆增强患者模拟器为核心的医生锚定评估平台 InterviewPlayground；使用专家撰写的临床情景（vignettes）创建可交互患者，搭建模拟接诊平台，并设计面向接诊任务的评估模态。开展 6 名临床医生的 25 分钟试点评估，与基于 GPT 的 LLM 接诊问诊者进行对比。

Result: LLM 问诊者更完整地还原了患者情景中嵌入的临床相关条目（88.0% vs. 38.9%），但产生更多非基于问诊的临床推断（56.8% vs. 27.8%），且对已识别的安全顾虑进行刻画的比例更低（33.3% vs. 66.7%）。

Conclusion: 该平台为 AI 辅助精神科接诊任务的实际部署质量保证奠定了基础，可在统一框架下比较不同问诊风格并揭示 LLM 在信息收集、推断依据与安全顾虑处理上的差异。

Abstract: Before patients can use AI-assisted psychiatric intake systems, health systems need practical ways to routinely evaluate these tools against their clinical standards for quality assurance. Because clinicians may use different intake styles, evaluation for this task must (1) support comparison across interviewing approaches, (2) minimize clinician burden, and (3) measure clinically relevant performance for health systems deploying these technologies. We present a clinician-grounded evaluation platform built around a memory-augmented patient simulator for open-ended AI interviewing, InterviewPlayground. We created interactive patients using InterviewPlayground with our expert-authored vignettes, constructed a simulated intake platform for the interviews, and designed evaluation modalities relevant to intake. In a pilot of 6 clinicians in a 25-minute assessment compared to a GPT-based LLM intake interviewer, the LLM recovered more of the clinically relevant items embedded in the patient vignettes (88.0% vs. 38.9%), but made more clinical inferences not based on the interview (56.8% vs. 27.8%), and characterized identified safety concerns less often (33.3% vs. 66.7%), setting the stage for deployed quality assurance for this task.

</details>


### [20] [Efficient Benchmarking in Production: A Study of an Evolving LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21267)
*Yining She,Lei Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对生产环境分析类LLM智能体的重复评测，比较随机抽样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集与IRT自适应测试，发现多维2PL自适应测试分数保真度最佳（仅用38.5%题量即达1.03pp MAE），但最终因运维简便部署难度分层固定子集，并验证其跨智能体族与跨校准窗口的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境LLM智能体持续迭代需反复评测，但完整基准重跑成本高昂，因此需要高效的循环评测方法。

Method: 基于574次生产基准历史运行（按时间划分为校准期与留出期），对比四类方法：随机抽样、历史缓存、固定代表性子集、IRT自适应测试（含多维2PL模型）。

Result: 多维2PL自适应测试整体分数保真度最优，执行38.5%题量（200题）时MAE为1.03pp；难度分层固定子集无需重新校准即可迁移至五个其他智能体族，且校准窗口短至一天仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: 综合分数保真度与运维简便性，最终部署难度分层固定子集，并据此提出生产智能体循环评测的实践建议。

Abstract: Production LLM agents are evaluated repeatedly as they evolve, but full agent benchmarks are costly to rerun. We study efficient recurring evaluation for a production analytics agent serving tens of thousands of monthly active users and report first-hand deployment experience. Using 574 historical runs of the production benchmark, split chronologically into calibration and held-out periods, we compare random sampling, historical caching, fixed representative subsets, and IRT-based adaptive testing. The results show that multidimensional 2PL adaptive testing achieves the best overall score fidelity: executing 200 questions, 38.5% of a full run, yields 1.03 pp of MAE. We nevertheless deployed difficulty-stratified fixed subsets because of their operational simplicity, and show they transfer without recalibration to five other agent families and remain stable across calibration windows as short as one day. Drawing on this deployment experience, we report practical recommendations for recurring production-agent evaluation.

</details>


### [21] [Can Agents Design Better Chips with a Higher Level Abstraction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21157)
*Zijian Ding,Yang Zou,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探索LLM智能体是否应通过更高抽象层次设计芯片，提出AHRR工作流（Agent-based HLS设计结合Post-HLS RTL精炼），在11个任务基准上相比直接RTL设计获得2.6倍几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体芯片设计方法大多直接在RTL层面操作，未充分利用更高层次抽象带来的设计知识与优化潜力，因此作者想探究智能体能否借助更高抽象层次设计出更好的芯片。

Method: 对比四种流程：Direct RTL Design、Agent-based HLS Design、Post-Compiler HLS Refinement、Post-HLS RTL Refinement，并将Agent-based HLS Design与Post-HLS RTL Refinement组合为AHRR；以FPGA作为端到端评估平台，在包含11个任务的多样化基准套件上进行评测。

Result: AHRR在基准套件上相比Direct RTL Design取得2.6倍几何平均加速；案例研究表明HLS将设计知识蒸馏为智能体可利用的抽象，而RTL精炼则恢复低层次优化机会。

Conclusion: AHRR是一种有前景的智能体芯片设计工作流，结合高层抽象与低层精炼可提升设计质量，相关代码与评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly being explored for chip design, but most existing approaches operate directly at RTL. We ask whether agents can design better chips by leveraging higher-level abstractions. We compare Direct RTL Design, Agent-based HLS Design, Post-Compiler HLS Refinement, and Post-HLS RTL Refinement, and combine Agent-based HLS Design with Post-HLS RTL Refinement as Agent-based HLS with RTL Refinement (AHRR). We use FPGAs as a practical, easy-to-deploy platform for end-to-end evaluation, but note that the design-flow tradeoffs we study are largely independent of the target technology. Across a diverse 11-tasks benchmark suite, AHRR achieves a 2.6$\times$ geometric-mean speedup over Direct RTL Design across our benchmark suite. Case studies show that HLS distills design knowledge into abstractions that agents can leverage, while RTL refinement recovers lower-level optimization opportunities. Together, these results make AHRR a promising workflow for agentic chip design. The code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/ZijD/AHRR.

</details>


### [22] [GameASG-Bench: Benchmarking Autonomous Software Generation for Game Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21293)
*Xiuhui Zhang,Yi Chen,Shusheng Xu,Fan Li,Huan Wang,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GameASG-Bench 基准，将行为可测试性纳入自主软件生成任务，通过 L1 静态检查与 L2 浏览器执行检查评估 47 个游戏生成任务，揭示高平均检查通过率掩盖的任务级合规差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主软件生成能将人类需求转化为可执行应用，但应用可交付并不代表其交互组件满足规定的行为需求，现有评估缺乏对行为合规性的系统检验。

Method: 在生成前声明评估接口规范（固定合法起始场景、玩家级动作、稳定快照、拒绝行为与不变量），保留私有实现自由；采用两层检查：L1 静态源码级合规检查，L2 浏览器执行检查结合语义观察、真实输入与运行时证据；构建覆盖 12 个主要品类、2D 与 3D 交互的 47 个浏览器原生游戏生成任务，每项任务配可执行检查与独立验证的参考实现。

Result: 在九种智能体栈中，最高平均 L2 检查通过率为 93.2%，但要求全部 L1 及相关 L2 前置与核心需求检查通过的最高严格任务成功率仅 55.3%（26/47）。对 DeepSeek-V4-Flash，完整工具访问与更大名义轮次预算提升严格任务成功数，而严格任务成功率对推理投入并非单调。两种测试框架均各获 18 个严格任务成功，但仅 10 个任务在两者下同时成功。

Conclusion: 高平均检查通过率会掩盖任务级合规差距，行为可测试性应作为生成任务的内在组成部分，以严格任务成功率为核心评价指标。

Abstract: Autonomous software generation (ASG) aims to turn human requirements into executable applications, but delivering these applications does not necessarily establish that their interacting components satisfy the specified behavioral requirements. We introduce GameASG-Bench, a benchmark that makes behavioral testability part of the generation task for game development. Our design declares an evaluation interface specification before generation, fixing legal starting scenarios, player-level actions, stable snapshots, rejection behavior, and invariants while leaving private implementations open. Concretely, we include: (i) static L1 checks that assess source-level compliance; and (ii) browser-executed L2 checks that combine semantic observations with real input and runtime evidence. We implement this protocol as 47 browser-native game-generation tasks spanning 12 primary genres and both 2D and 3D interaction, each with executable checks and an independently verified reference implementation. Our experiments answer four key questions about end-to-end agent performance, tool access and nominal turn budget, reasoning effort, and harness choice. Across nine agent stacks, the highest observed mean L2 check pass rate is 93.2%, yet the highest observed strict task success rate, requiring all L1 and applicable L2 prerequisite and core requirement checks, is only 55.3% (26/47 tasks). For DeepSeek-V4-Flash, full tool access and larger nominal turn budgets yield more strict task successes, while the strict task success rate is not monotonic in reasoning effort. Both tested harnesses achieve 18 strict task successes, but only ten tasks succeed under both. These results expose task-level compliance gaps that high average check pass rates actually obscure.

</details>


### [23] [SpecOpt: Contact-Diff Reasoning for Agentic Molecule Optimization Toward Binding Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21165)
*Thao Nguyen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"特异性优化"（SpecOpt）这一分子设计任务，并开发了一套由对接与残基感知接触分析驱动、大语言模型生成结构修饰的智能体框架，在不显著改变分子相似性和成药性的前提下提升既有药物对靶点相对脱靶蛋白的结合偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脱靶蛋白结合是小分子药物不良反应的主要来源，但多数基于结构的分子设计方法专注于从头生成选择性化合物，而非改善已有、表征充分的药物的选择性。这一空白是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为SpecOpt：对既有化合物进行受约束的结构修饰，以增强其对预期靶点相对于已知脱靶蛋白的结合偏好，同时保持结构同一性与成药性。为系统评估，构建了基于ChEMBL的基准数据集，通过策展的药物机制注释确定预期靶点、通过实测活性确定脱靶蛋白。随后开发智能体框架：将化合物分别对接至预期靶点与脱靶蛋白，通过残基感知的原子-蛋白接触比较对接构象，并将差异相互作用信息输入大语言模型以提出针对性的结构修饰方案。候选分子仅在满足分子相似性、ADMET及靶点-脱靶对接选择性标准时才被保留。消融实验以29个化合物为对象进行。

Result: 在915个化合物上，该智能体使84.8%的化合物改善了靶点-脱靶结合差距，平均差距由-0.72 kcal/mol提升至+0.47 kcal/mol，同时与起始化合物的平均Tanimoto相似度保持在0.72。消融实验表明，残基特异性接触信息是关键优化信号：将残基身份替换为二元接触指示量后，全部29个消融化合物的改进效果消失。

Conclusion: 结果确立了SpecOpt作为一个独立的分子设计问题，并证明残基感知的差异相互作用是提升既有化合物特异性的有效信号。

Abstract: Off-target protein binding is a major source of adverse effects for small-molecule drugs, yet most structure-based molecular design methods focus on generating selective compounds de novo rather than improving the selectivity of existing, well- characterized drugs. We introduce specificity optimization (SpecOpt), a molecular design task that seeks constrained structural modifications to an existing compound that increase its binding preference for an intended target over known off-targets while preserving its structural identity and drug-like properties. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct a ChEMBL-derived benchmark from compound-target interaction data, identifying intended targets through curated drug-mechanism annotations and off- targets through measured activities. We then develop an agentic framework that docks each compound against its intended target and off-targets, compares the resulting poses through residue-aware atom-protein contacts, and provides these differential interactions to a large language model to propose targeted structural modifications. Candidates are retained only if they satisfy molecular similarity, ADMET, and target-off-target docking selectivity criteria. On 915 compounds, the agent improves the target- off-target binding gap for 84.8% of compounds, shifting the mean gap from -0.72 to +0.47 kcal/mol while maintaining a mean Tanimoto similarity of 0.72 to the starting compounds. Ablation studies identify residue-specific contact information as the critical optimization signal: replacing residue identities with binary contact indicators eliminates improvement on all 29 ablation compounds. These results establish SpecOpt as a distinct molecular design problem and demonstrate residue-aware differential interactions as an effective signal for improving the specificity of existing compounds.

</details>

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus and related benchmarks evaluate whether AI models can solve novel reasoning tasks, but often leave unclear whether success reflects inference of the intended underlying rule or reliance on shortcuts. We address this gap by studying test-time task embeddings in Vision ARC (VARC), a model in which a pre-trained backbone is complemented by a trainable embedding representing the transformation rule. In the original VARC, test-time training (TTT) is jointly applied to the backbone and task embedding. Here we introduce a novel two-step TTT protocol: first finetune only the task embedding (Embed-TTT), then freeze it and finetune the backbone. Across ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and two controlled datasets with known rules, Embed-TTT consistently yields improved task embeddings, ones that align better with underlying task rules, improve embedding-based retrieval, and enable accurate linear probing of known rules. Qualitatively, Embed-TTT identifies more semantically meaningful relations between test and train tasks on ARC-AGI-1. We also show that optimizing only task embeddings (less than 0.01% of model parameters) already solves a non-trivial fraction of ARC-AGI-1, ConceptARC, and Mini-ARC tasks, while the full two-step pipeline improves final performance. Finally, we show that Embed-TTT recovers the underlying geometric structure of parametric rules and learns compositional capabilities that enable rule-wise interpolation, but not extrapolation. These findings support a clearer separation between rule induction and rule execution in ARC-like evaluations, motivating benchmarks that better distinguish in-distribution from out-of-distribution rules.

</details>

Method: 基于专业观察与对标准、文献的目的性综合（purposive synthesis），以设计科学（design science）界定方法贡献，以情境化方法工程（situational method engineering）指导裁剪与复用。框架先确立目标与义务，再在七个风险域（含使命与价值实现）进行风险评估，据此导出部署前保证、运行时控制与证据要求，进而支撑能力资格认定、差距评估与逻辑架构；并以 Agent Operating Envelope 规定允许动作与升级条件，以 Risk-Aligned Independence Levels（RAIL）概括被授权的自主程度。最后用虚构零售银行应用示例。

Result: 形成一套结构化的用例操作化框架，产出可追溯的基础，用以判定组织必须实现什么、已具备何种支持能力、以及尚存哪些未解决问题；案例示例展示了方法的可操作性。但论文为提案性质，尚缺实证结果。

Conclusion: AI-GRACE 提供了连接治理与实施的用例级操作化路径，其价值有待实证检验：需评估其是否能改善部署决策、提升效率并促进方法复用。

Abstract: Organizations deploying agentic artificial intelligence must determine more than whether a model is trustworthy; they must establish what to validate, control, and observe for a use case to deliver its intended outcome while meeting applicable obligations. This paper proposes AI-GRACE (Agentic Intelligence-Governance, Risk, Assurance, Controls, and Evidence) as a use-case operationalization framework connecting organizational governance with technical implementation. The proposal draws on professional observations and a purposive synthesis of standards and literature, using design science to frame the method contribution and situational method engineering to guide contextual tailoring and reuse. The framework establishes objectives and obligations and then assesses risks in seven proposed domains, including mission and value realization. It derives requirements for assurance before deployment, runtime controls, and evidence, which guide capability qualification, gap assessment, and a logical architecture. An Agent Operating Envelope specifies permitted actions and escalation conditions, while Risk-Aligned Independence Levels (RAIL) summarize the authorized independence. A fictional retail banking application illustrates the method. The contribution is a traceable basis for deciding what an organization must implement, what it already supports, and what remains unresolved. Empirical evaluation must establish whether it improves deployment decisions, efficiency, and reuse.

</details>


### [26] [Information-Gain Rewards over Diversity-Pruned Tests: GT-Anchored Verifier Co-Training for Reliable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21208)
*Ana Nunez,Peyman Najafirad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoVer：单策略GRPO框架，用信息增益奖励与三阶段多样性筛选同时解决自博弈代码生成中的"宽松性坍缩"和"集中偏差"，在5个基准上显著提升pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自博弈方法将同一语言模型同时训练为代码生成器和测试作者，可突破固定测试集的限制，但存在两个耦合缺陷：(1) 宽松性坍缩——平凡、无区分度的测试即可最大化通过率奖励；(2) 集中偏差——i.i.d.采样的测试聚集在众数输入上，放大估计器方差。因此需要一种能同时抑制这两种失效模式的训练框架。

Method: 提出CoVer，一个单策略GRPO框架：(1) 信息增益（IG）奖励——以自生成测试的通过/失败向量与基于真实答案的连续正确性信号 y∈[0,1]^m 之间的互信息为打分，并用二者协方差符号作为门控，仅对具有正向区分度的测试给予奖励；(2) 三阶段多样性感知筛选——从候选池中经无效性过滤、输入字符串过滤、执行画像过滤，剪枝为行为上非冗余的测试套件，在固定执行预算下提升IG估计器的有效样本量。

Result: 在LiveBench、MBPP、LiveCodeBench、CodeContests、CodeForces五个基准上，相较Qwen2.5-Instruct骨干，CoVer将单次pass@1在7B上提升+5.8分、14B上提升+7.1分，并在两个规模下均取得所有对比方法中最高的宏平均。作为CodeT排序流水线中的即插即用骨干，CoVer-7B额外贡献+3.5分。

Conclusion: CoVer通过信息增益奖励与多样性筛选的联合设计，有效缓解了自博弈代码生成中的宽松性坍缩与集中偏差，验证了协同训练对生成与测试选择的双重收益。

Abstract: Self-play methods that co-train a single language model as both coder and test author promise to move code-generation RL beyond fixed test suites, but they suffer from two coupled pathologies: permissiveness collapse, where pass-rate rewards are maximised by trivial, non-discriminative tests, and concentration bias, where i.i.d. sampled tests cluster on modal inputs and inflate estimator variance. We introduce CoVer (Co-trained Coder and Verifier), a single-policy GRPO framework that addresses both failure modes. First, an information-gain (IG) reward scores each self-generated test by the mutual information between its pass/fail vector and a graded, ground-truth-anchored correctness signal y [0, 1] m, gated by the sign of their covariance so that only positively discriminative tests receive reward. Second, a three-stage diversity-aware selection step prunes a candidate pool to a behaviourally non-redundant suite (invalidity, input-string, execution-profile filtering), raising the effective sample size of the IG estimator at fixed execution budget. On five benchmarks (LiveBench, MBPP, LiveCodeBench, CodeContests, Code-Forces), CoVer raises one-shot pass@1 by +5.8 points at 7B and +7.1 points at 14B over the Qwen2.5-Instruct backbone, and achieves the highest macro-average among all compared methods at both scales. As a drop-in backbone inside the CodeT ranking pipeline, CoVer-7B adds +3.5 points, demonstrating the dual benefit of co-training for both generation and selection.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感认知诊断方法假设认知诊断主干已充分解释能力、题目和概念效应，剩余误差主要归因于情感。但真实教育数据中存在题目校准偏差、系统性概念偏差、个性化学生-概念偏差以及潜在学生-题目匹配等稳定认知残差，若无显式建模路径，这些残差会泄漏进情感表征，产生情感污染。因此需要解耦能力残差与情感表征。

Method: 提出能力-残差解耦框架：首先通过学生、题目、概念、学生-概念以及低秩学生-题目成分捕获未建模的认知残差；随后用情感模块调制猜测/失误效应；并设计受Q矩阵约束的概念残差注意力机制，自适应聚合仅与题目相关的概念残差。

Result: 在ASSIST2017、ASSIST2012、ASSIST2009和Junyi四个数据集上，结合六种认知诊断主干进行实验，报告的对比中响应预测均有提升，且在情感标签可用时情感对齐普遍改善。消融实验、泄漏探测、主成分分析可视化、长尾分析和案例研究进一步表明：能力残差吸收了稳定的认知偏差，减少了情感分支中的认知污染，并提升了认知诊断模型的鲁棒性与预测精度。

Conclusion: 显式建模并解耦认知残差可有效缓解情感污染问题，在提升认知诊断预测精度的同时改善情感对齐，为情感认知诊断提供了一种更稳健的建模路径。

Abstract: Cognitive diagnosis infers students' concept mastery from response logs. However, students' responses are not determined by mastery alone: non-cognitive factors such as emotion, engagement, and fatigue can also affect performance. Affective cognitive diagnosis therefore extends conventional cognitive diagnosis by incorporating affective states. Existing methods often assume that the cognitive diagnosis backbone has already explained ability, item, and concept effects, so the remaining errors can be attributed mainly to affect. We argue that this assumption can be insufficient in real educational data: item calibration bias, systematic concept bias, personalized student-concept deviations, and latent student-item matching can form stable cognitive residuals. Without an explicit modeling pathway, these residuals may leak into affective representations, producing affect contamination. To address this problem, we propose an ability-residual decoupled framework for affective cognitive diagnosis. The model first captures unmodeled cognitive residuals through student, item, concept, student-concept, and low-rank student-item components, and then uses an affective module to modulate guess/slip effects. A Q-matrix-constrained concept residual attention mechanism adaptively aggregates only item-relevant concept residuals. Experiments on ASSIST2017, ASSIST2012, ASSIST2009, and Junyi with six cognitive diagnosis backbones show response-prediction gains across the reported comparisons and generally improved affect alignment when affect labels are available. Ablation studies, leakage probes, principal component analysis visualization, long-tail analysis, and case studies further indicate that ability residuals absorb stable cognitive bias, reduce cognitive contamination in the affective branch, and enhance the robustness and predictive accuracy of cognitive diagnosis models.

</details>

Abstract: Perceptual planning tasks require two key capabilities: accurately perceiving uncertain scenes and planning valid action sequences following logical rules. Conventional methods convert perception into discrete symbolic facts and then plan, discarding perceptual uncertainty and severing task-level feedback to perception. We introduce a generic, fully differentiable neuro-soft-symbolic framework that connects visual perception and task planning within a single computational graph. The framework maintains a continuous soft symbolic state, lifts domain rules into a differentiable soft-$T_P$ transition operator, and optimizes action logits over a short planning horizon. Gradients from the planning objective can also update the perception parameters, allowing task-relevant perceptual representations to be refined during planning. On Blocksworld, our method solves 40/40 LatPlan-40 tasks and 596/600 PlanBench-600 tasks, compared with 33/40 for LatPlan and 587/600 for the reasoning-model baseline, while requiring substantially less computation and time. In the perceptual-uncertainty ablation, our method improves the success rate from 59\% with frozen perception to 83\%. We further conduct task-and-motion simulations on Blocksworld scenes, providing an execution-level validation of the compatibility between decoded task plans and downstream robotic motion execution.

</details>

*Lance Ying,Jinzhou Wu,Yingshan Susan Wang,Shivam Aarya,Luca M. Schulze Buschoff,Harry Chen,Katherine M. Collins,Andrea de Varda,Shuhao Fu,Sean Dae Houlihan,Akshay K. Jagadish,Guangyuan Jiang,Samuel Kiegeland,Tetsu Kurumisawa,Rongzhi Liu,Ryan Liu,Ningshan Ma,Kathryn McGregor,Younes Strittmatter,Polina Tsvilodub,Jacob Hoover Vigly,Sarah Wu,Enjie Xu,Yiling Yun,Kelsey Allen,Tyler Brooke-Wilson,Brian Christian,Evelina Fedorenko,Michael C. Frank,Michael Franke,Tao Gao,Samuel J. Gershman,Robert D. Hawkins,Jennifer Hu,Julian Jara-Ettinger,Max Kleiman-Weiner,Sydney Levine,Tal Linzen,Hongjing Lu,Timothy O'Donnell,Desmond C. Ong,Steven T. Piantadosi,Rebecca Saxe,Eric Schulz,Tianmin Shu,Felix A. Sosa,Ilia Sucholutsky,Tan Zhi-Xuan,Tomer Ullman,Fei Xu,Ilker Yildirim,Jian-Qiao Zhu,Thomas L. Griffiths,Tobias Gerstenberg,Kevin Smith,Joshua B. Tenenbaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CogGym，一个基于认知科学的可扩展统一框架，用于在匹配实验试次上系统比较模型与人类行为，并据此评估50个大语言模型在258个常识推理实验中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与认知科学共同关注理解与建模人类智能。随着AI能力提升，需要明确模型在哪些方面接近人类、哪些方面系统性偏离。但人类可执行任务极为广泛多样，难以进行可扩展且严谨的人机对比。

Method: CogGym采用半自动、人在回路(human-in-the-loop)的流水线，将多样实验范式统一标准化为与任务无关的实验标记语言(Experiment Markup Language, EML)，实现可复现、忠实的大规模比较。首发版本整理并标准化了来自100篇论文的258个认知实验，聚焦人类常识推理，并评估50个大语言模型与人类反应的拟合程度。

Result: 发现明显的规模趋势：更大、更新的AI模型更能复现人类判断。但模型在常识推理任务上的提升显著慢于数学、编程等形式推理基准的增益；模型—人类拟合仍远低于人类分半信度(R²=0.93文本、0.95图像、0.92视频)，最佳模型仅为R²=0.59文本、0.58图像、0.43视频。

Conclusion: CogGym旨在成为持续更新的“活”评估框架，不断纳入新的认知科学实验，刻画模型行为与人类行为的相似与系统性差异，以及这些模式随模型和实验演进而发生的变化。

Abstract: Understanding and modeling human intelligence are parallel goals shared by artificial intelligence (AI) and cognitive science. As AI systems grow increasingly capable, in what ways do model responses resemble human responses, and where do they systematically diverge? The sheer breadth and diversity of the tasks humans can perform and think about pose a challenge for scalable and rigorous comparison between humans and models. We introduce CogGym, a scalable, unified framework grounded in cognitive science for systematically comparing model and human behavior on matched experimental trials. CogGym uses a semi-automated, human-in-the-loop pipeline to standardize diverse experimental paradigms into a task-agnostic Experiment Markup Language (EML), enabling reproducible and faithful comparison at scale. For initial release, we curate and standardize 258 cognitive experiments from 100 papers that focuses on human commonsense reasoning, and evaluate 50 large language models against human responses. We find a clear scaling trend where larger and more recent AI models better reproduce human judgments. Yet AI models' improvement on such common reasoning tasks is considerably slower than the gains observed on formal-reasoning benchmarks like math and coding, and model--human fit remains well below human splithalf reliability ($R^2 = 0.93$ on text, $0.95$ on image, and $0.92$ on video) with the best models achieving $R^2 = 0.59$ on text, $0.58$ on image, and $0.43$ on video experiments. We intend for CogGym to provide a living evaluation framework that continually incorporates new cognitive science experiments to characterize where model behavior resembles human behavior, where it systematically diverges, and how those patterns change as models and experiments evolve.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宏单元布局方法主要优化手工设计的代理目标（如估算线长），且通常采用一次性数值优化，难以在同一循环中融入视觉布局上下文、设计经验知识以及下游物理设计反馈。因此作者希望将宏布局从黑盒优化转变为闭环推理问题，以提升端到端布局质量。

Conclusion: 在物理设计反馈下对空间结构进行推理，可以超越代理目标优化，改善端到端布局质量。该工作表明闭环推理与多验证器引导有望成为宏单元布局的新范式，并提供可复现的开放基准推动后续研究。

Abstract: Automated macro placement remains a fundamental challenge in VLSI physical design. Despite decades of research, existing approaches predominantly optimize hand-crafted proxy objectives, such as estimated wirelength, and typically produce placements through one-shot numerical optimization, limiting their ability to incorporate visual layout context, codified design expertise, and downstream physical-design feedback in a unified loop. We present PlaceReasoner-Beta, a verifier-guided multi-agent framework that reformulates macro placement as a closed-loop reasoning problem rather than black-box optimization. A vision-language model (VLM) planner generates candidate placements from the floorplan image, macro specifications, and connectivity structure; a geometric verifier enforces physical legality and expert placement principles; a physical verifier refines candidates using early implementation feedback; and a post-route optimizer further improves promising layouts using final PPA. To enable reproducible evaluation, we introduce PlaceReasoner-Bench, a fully open end-to-end benchmark built from open RTL designs, EDA tools, and technology libraries. It comprises 8 designs at two aspect ratios, yielding 16 tasks with fixed floorplans and I/O assignments, so methods differ only in macro positions and orientations and are evaluated using routed PPA and DRC rather than pre-route proxies. Across the benchmark, PlaceReasoner-Beta achieves the best timing among DRC-clean methods on all square tasks, reducing post-route TNS by 61.2% at 1:1 and 53.0% at 2:1 relative to the classical baseline field. It also shortens routed wirelength on most designs despite never explicitly optimizing it, demonstrating that reasoning over spatial structure under physical-design feedback can improve end-to-end layout quality beyond proxy-objective optimization.

</details>


### [31] [LEGIT: Credentialing Protocol for Trustworthy AI Agent Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21325)
*Steve Drew,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LEGIT 凭证化协议，将认证、声誉与市场分配机制连接起来，解决智能体市场中买家难以判断哪个智能体最适合其任务的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体市场（agentic marketplaces）中，具备不同能力的 AI 智能体自主为买家完成专门任务。核心痛点是：买家难以确定哪个智能体在其任务上表现最佳；已报告的基准分数在任务、软件与预算之间难以验证或比较。

Method: 提出 LEGIT 凭证化协议，连接认证（certification）、声誉（reputation）与市场分配（marketplace allocation）。认证通过签名记录，将测得的每解决任务的质量与成本绑定到智能体配置、任务领域、评估预算与证据上。声誉将过往任务结果记录链接到同一身份，并受所报告反馈可靠性的约束。买家与智能体可验证凭证记录，并查看可选的视觉画像。此外，在设定的 Sybil 攻击模型下，量化声誉操纵所需的押金与费用。

Result: 评估揭示了任务成功率相近的智能体配置之间存在成本差异，并表明比较结果依赖于评估预算。互补分析给出了在给定 Sybil 攻击模型下实施声誉操纵所需的押金与费用。

Conclusion: 结果支持将性能测量绑定到被测配置与资源限制上；LEGIT 通过认证与声誉结合，为智能体市场的可信比较与分配提供机制保障。

Abstract: Agentic marketplaces are emerging where AI agents with varying capabilities autonomously complete specialized tasks for buyers. A major challenge of such marketplaces is that buyers cannot easily determine which agent will perform best on their tasks. Reported benchmark scores may be difficult to verify or compare across tasks, software, and budgets. We introduce LEGIT, a credentialing protocol connecting certification, reputation, and proposed marketplace allocation. Certification binds measured quality and cost per solved task to an agent configuration, task domain, evaluation budget, and evidence through a signed record. Reputation links records of past task outcomes to the same identity, subject to the reliability of the reported feedback. Buyers and agents can verify credential records and inspect optional visual profiles. Evaluations reveal cost differences between agent configurations with similar observed task success, and show that comparisons depend on the evaluation budget. These results support binding performance measurements to the tested configuration and resource limits. A complementary analysis quantifies the deposits and fees required for reputation manipulation under a stated Sybil attack model.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究在隔离、无网络且安全受限环境下部署离线大语言模型，作为航空图像分析人员的决策支持工具。作者提出一种模块化检索增强架构，支持文本与图像输入，并通过巴西空军四名图像分析人员的试点研究进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中作战依赖复杂规则、既定程序和时效性强的分析，且常处于连通性受限和严格安全约束之下。分析人员需将书面条令与图像结合，却往往无法访问外部计算资源。因此需要一种能够在离线、隔离环境中使用、且答案可溯源至原始来源的决策支持工具。

Method: 提出一种适用于无互联网连接的模块化检索增强（RAG）架构，支持来自技术手册的文本和图像输入。开展包含四名巴西空军图像分析人员的试点研究，包括：(i) 基于其电子目标识别条令的条令知识测评，比较人类与系统的表现；(ii) 测量在无 AI 辅助下人工编制侦察目标报告（REMIR）的认知负荷。

Result: 人工任务负荷较高，尤其体现在脑力需求（6.0/7）和努力程度（5.0/7）。所提系统在测评中达到与人类相同分数（8/10），耗时 7.1 分钟，而人类平均耗时 26.5 分钟。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来 AI 辅助评估建立了基线，并描述了用于系统比较人工流程与 AI 辅助流程的未来评估协议。

Abstract: Air operations rely on complex rules, established procedures, and time-critical analysis under limited connectivity and strict security constraints. In such environments, analysts must combine written doctrine with images, often without access to external computing resources. This paper studies offline large language models as decision support tools, deployed in isolated and restricted environments to give analysts access to doctrinal knowledge that remains traceable to its original sources through natural language interaction. We describe a modular retrieval-augmented architecture suitable for operation without Internet connectivity, supporting both text and image input from technical manuals. As a first step toward evaluating this architecture, we report a pilot study with four image analysts of the Brazilian Air Force, combining (i) a doctrinal knowledge assessment based on their electronic-target identification doctrine, comparing human and proposed system performance on the same test, and (ii) a measurement of the cognitive workload involved in manually producing a reconnaissance target report (Relatório de Missão de Reconhecimento - REMIR) without AI assistance. The results show a demanding manual task, especially in terms of mental demand (6.0/7) and effort (5.0/7), while the proposed system matches the human score (8/10) and completes the assessment in 7.1 minutes (compared to a human average of 26.5 minutes), establishing a baseline for future AI-assisted evaluation. Finally, we describe a future evaluation protocol to systematically compare manual and AI-assisted workflows.

</details>


### [33] [DENSE: Distilling Agent Trajectories into Evidence-Grounded Shortcut Trees for Self-Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21423)
*Siyuan Liu,Fan Yu,Dongyu Ru,Yizhu Liu,Yifan Yang,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Yixin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 DENSE 与 REFIT：从无结果标签的执行轨迹中蒸馏可复用反馈，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上严格通过率提升 7.12–15.64 pp 且重跑 token 减少 19.0–43.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线智能体部署产生大量执行轨迹，但任务级验证与专家标注的规模化成本很高；作者希望在没有事后结果标签的情况下，利用轨迹中关于局部进展、恢复与未完成需求的证据，蒸馏出可复用的反馈。

Method: DENSE（Distilling Evidence from Nested Subtask Executions）将证据组织为证据锚定的嵌套捷径树，压缩冗余尝试、利用恢复证据跨层级调和问题，并总结已完成分支、展开未解决分支，从而把可复用进展与剩余义务关联起来。评估采用 REFIT，一种源配对协议：在事后结果盲设下比较来自共享初始轨迹的反馈，并重置环境与模型上下文以对同一任务进行全新尝试。

Result: 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，DENSE 在四种接收者模型中取得所测试非特权反馈方法里最高的严格通过率；相较初始执行，严格通过率提升 7.12–15.64 个百分点，重跑时观察到的接收者 token 减少 19.0–43.6%。GPT-5.5 消融支持将嵌套子任务分析与捷径构建、问题调和相结合。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于证据的轨迹复用可实现智能体自我改进，减少对外部监督的依赖。

Abstract: Online agent deployments produce abundant execution traces, while task-specific verification and expert annotation are costly to scale. We study how to distill these traces into reusable feedback without post-hoc outcome labels, drawing on their evidence of local progress, recovery, and unfinished requirements. We introduce DENSE (Distilling Evidence from Nested Subtask Executions), which organizes this evidence into evidence-grounded nested shortcut trees. DENSE compresses redundant attempts, reconciles issues across levels using recovery evidence, and summarizes completed branches while expanding unresolved ones, linking reusable progress to remaining obligations. We introduce REFIT, a source-paired protocol comparing feedback from shared initial trajectories under post-hoc outcome blindness, with environments and model contexts reset for fresh attempts at the same tasks. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, DENSE achieves the highest strict pass rate among tested non-privileged feedback methods across four recipient models. Relative to initial executions, strict pass rate improves by 7.12-15.64 pp, with 19.0-43.6% fewer observed recipient tokens in reruns. GPT-5.5 ablations support combining nested subtask analysis with shortcut construction and issue reconciliation. These findings point toward agent self-refinement through evidence-grounded trajectory reuse with less reliance on external supervision.

</details>


### [34] [GVPO++: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21432)
*Kaichen Zhang,Yuzhong Hong,Junwei Bao,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dingqian Hong,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GVPO（Group Variance Policy Optimization），通过将KL约束奖励最大化的解析解引入梯度加权，摆脱对重要性采样的依赖，保证唯一最优解且支持灵活采样分布，并可自然扩展到在线策略蒸馏（OPD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法（如GRPO）依赖重要性采样，导致训练不稳定，限制实际部署，需要一种兼具理论保证与实用稳定性的新方法。

Method: GVPO将KL约束奖励最大化的解析解融入梯度加权方案；其梯度等价于隐式奖励中心距离与实际奖励中心距离之间的均方误差；无需重要性采样，采样分布灵活，并可扩展至OPD及一族广义OPD目标。

Result: GVPO具备两项优势：(1) 保证唯一最优解，且精确对应KL约束奖励最大化目标；(2) 支持灵活采样分布、无需重要性采样。同时可自然推广至OPD并优化多种扩展OPD目标。

Conclusion: GVPO将理论保证与实践适应性统一，为可靠、通用的LLM后训练与在线策略蒸馏建立了新范式。

Abstract: Post-training plays a pivotal role in enhancing the reasoning capabilities and task-specific expertise of large language models (LLMs). Despite recent advances in post-training methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), their practical deployment remains impeded by training instability arising from the reliance on importance sampling.
  We introduce Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO), a novel post-training method that integrates the analytical solution of KL-constrained reward maximization into its gradient weighting scheme. This formulation provides an intuitive interpretation: GVPO's gradient corresponds to the mean squared error between the central distance of implicit rewards and that of actual rewards. GVPO offers two key advantages: (1) it guarantees a unique optimal solution, exactly to the KL-constrained reward maximization objective, and (2) it enables flexible sampling distributions without requiring importance sampling.
  Beyond general post-training, we show that GVPO naturally extends to on-policy distillation (OPD). Furthermore, GVPO enables the optimization of a broad family of extended OPD objectives, providing a principled foundation for diverse objective design. By unifying theoretical guarantees with practical adaptability, GVPO establishes a new paradigm for reliable and versatile LLM post-training and on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [35] [Risk-Aware Occupancy for Safety-Oriented End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21470)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,Yiren Zhao,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知占用（risk-aware occupancy）这一稠密表示，将全局场景占用、地图交通约束与未来动态智能体占用统一编码为BEV图，并设计E2E网络ROIDrive将其注入规划查询，同时构建RiskOcc4D-nuScenes数据集；在nuScenes上相对碰撞率分别降低52.9%（UniAD指标）和35.0%（ST-P3指标）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶系统常用稀疏表示（对象、车道线等离散元素）刻画环境感知，但在拥挤、遮挡场景下面对非结构化障碍物、不确定区域与复杂交互时存在安全隐患，需要一种显式且统一的规划相关风险表示。

Method: 提出稠密表示"风险感知占用"，将全局场景占用、地图导出的交通约束和未来动态智能体占用联合编码进统一的BEV图，在时空维度捕获规划所需的规划相关风险证据；设计端到端网络ROIDrive，通过独立分支预测风险感知占用并注入规划查询以生成安全导向轨迹；构建基于nuScenes与Occ3D-nuScenes的RiskOcc4D-nuScenes数据集以量化安全问题。

Result: 在nuScenes上，风险感知占用相比基线实现相对开环碰撞减少：UniAD指标下52.9%，ST-P3指标下35.0%。

Conclusion: 以稠密、显式、统一的风险感知占用替代稀疏表示，并结合专用E2E网络与量化数据集，可有效提升端到端驾驶规划的安全性，显著降低碰撞风险。

Abstract: Sparse representation formulates the environment perception for the end-to-end driving system as a set of discrete elements like objects and lane lines. This formulation meets safety risks in crowded, occluded scenes dealing with unstructured obstacles, uncertain regions, and intricate interactions. In this paper, we propose a dense representation, risk-aware occupancy, to characterize planning-relevant risks in an explicit and uniform manner. It jointly encodes global scene occupancy, map-derived traffic constraints, and future dynamic agent occupancy into a unified BEV map. The unified BEV map captures the risk evidence for trajectory planning in both spatial and temporal dimensions. We design an E2E network, ROIDrive, to realize risk-aware occupancy. It predicts risk-aware occupancy with an independent branch and injects it into planning queries for safety-oriented trajectory generation. In addition, to quantify the safety problem, we introduce RiskOcc4D-nuScenes built upon nuscenes and occ3d-nuscenes. Our risk-aware occupancy yields relative open-loop collision reductions of 52.9% under the UniAD metric and 35.0% under the ST-P3 metric on nuScenes.

</details>


### [36] [Driving on Registers, Reasoning on Risk: Risk-Aware Occupancy for Register-Based End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21486)
*Jiaxing Chen,Hengduo Zou,YuKai Qin,Yiren Zhao,Lidong Yu,Bolin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RRDrive，以风险感知占据（risk-aware occupancy）作为稠密、时间对齐、可由轨迹查询的场景表示，提升多模态轨迹预测的生成与排序质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
