Motivation: 现有多模态轨迹预测受限于稀疏场景表示：证据不完整导致候选轨迹生成质量低，且几何相似轨迹间排序不可靠。在register基线中，差/劣候选占19.74%，而oracle最优候选平均仅排在第33.9位。

Method: 引入风险感知占据作为稠密、时间对齐且可被轨迹查询的表示：其全局结构指导高质量多模态生成，候选条件化的风险查询（candidate-conditioned risk queries）支持细粒度选择；并构建带自动风险标注的RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM数据集。此外开发外部RiskOcc预测器，直接从传感器输入估计风险感知占据。

Result: 选定轨迹PDMS达0.951，较基线0.937相对提升1.5%；候选平均PDMS提升7.7%。困难场景中候选PDMS提升30.2%，优质候选间Spearman相关性由0.26提升至0.67（提升0.41）。外部RiskOcc预测器取得有竞争力的表现。

Conclusion: 风险感知占据表示能有效提升多模态轨迹生成的覆盖率与排序可靠性，且可由感知模块从传感器直接估计，验证了该表示的可行性与向非oracle场景推广的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal trajectory prediction improves behavioral coverage in end-to-end autonomous driving, but existing methods remain limited by sparse scene representations. Incomplete evidence leads to low-quality candidate generation and unreliable ranking among geometrically similar trajectories. On a register-based baseline, bad and poor candidates constitute 19.74% of the candidate set, while the oracle-best candidate ranks only 33.9th on average. We propose RRDrive, which introduces risk-aware occupancy as a dense, temporally aligned, and trajectory-queryable representation. Its global structure guides high-quality multimodal generation, while candidate-conditioned risk queries support fine-grained selection. We further construct RiskOcc4D-NAVSIM with automatic risk annotations. RRDrive achieves a selected-trajectory PDMS of 0.951, representing a 1.5% relative improvement over the baseline (0.937), and improves the average candidate PDMS by 7.7%. In challenging scenes, it improves candidate PDMS by 30.2% and increases the Spearman correlation among good candidates by 0.41, from 0.26 to 0.67. To move beyond this oracle setting, we further develop an external RiskOcc predictor, a perception module that estimates risk-aware occupancy directly from sensor inputs. The competitive performance validates the representation's feasibility.

</details>


### [37] [LogicTrack: Auditing Reasoning Trajectories of Large Language Models with Formal Logic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21492)
*Jingyu Hu,Shu Yang,Weiru Liu,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LogicTrack：神经符号框架，将推理步骤自动形式化并用定理证明器验证，提出SBR奖励进行逐步打分与回溯搜索，并构造回溯轨迹SFT数据，提升推理链可验证性与答案正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT优化方法多依赖结果导向反馈，中间推理步骤的逻辑有效性基本未被验证，导致LLM可能以逻辑有缺陷的中间链得出正确最终答案。

Method: 提出LogicTrack神经符号框架：将每个推理步骤自动形式化为符号表示，交由自动定理证明器验证；引入Solver-Based Backtracking Reward（SBR）逐步量化逻辑可靠性，并据此在推理时引导回溯树搜索；进一步用其轨迹中的回溯过程构造SFT数据，使微调模型内化逐步审计能力。

Result: 在8个推理基准、7个LLM上的大量实验表明，LogicTrack有效提升了推理链的可验证性以及最终答案通过率。

Conclusion: LogicTrack通过神经符号验证与逐步回溯奖励，增强了CoT整体质量与可信度，对高风险领域的推理应用具有价值。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has been shown to improve the performance of large language models (LLMs), yet existing optimization methods largely rely on outcome-based feedback, leaving the logical validity of intermediate reasoning steps largely unverified. To address the gap whereby LLMs arrive at correct final answers through logically flawed intermediate reasoning chains, we propose LogicTrack, a neuro-symbolic framework that audits reasoning trajectories by auto-formalizing each reasoning step into symbolic representations and verifying it with automated theorem provers. LogicTrack introduces Solver-Based Backtracking Reward (SBR), a step-wise scoring mechanism that quantifies logical soundness and guides backtracking tree search at inference time. We further extend LogicTrack to construct supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data with backtracking traces from its trajectories, enabling fine-tuned models to internalize step-wise auditing as an intrinsic capability. Extensive experiments across 8 reasoning benchmarks and 7 LLMs demonstrate that LogicTrack effectively improves both the verifiability of reasoning chains and final answer pass rate, thereby enhancing overall CoT quality and trustworthiness in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [38] [PolyBridgeBench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs for Physics-Grounded Bridge Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21493)
*Zicheng Zhao,Dongyin Chen,Rui Xu,Yinghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PolyBridgeBench，一个可执行的多模态桥梁设计基准，用于评估MLLM能否生成完整承重结构并在仿真失败后修复，实验揭示确定性合法性与动态成功间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型虽在视觉理解与结构化生成上表现良好，但这些能力无法确立工程设计在执行时是否可行。已有基准评估空间推理、结构有效性或物理落地构建，但均未考察MLLM能否合成完整承重结构，以及在仿真执行暴露失败后进行修复的能力。

Method: 构建PolyBridgeBench可执行基准：模型接收视觉场景与结构化工程约束，生成完整的节点—构件—材料拓扑；采用确定性合法性检查作为动态物理仿真执行的关卡门槛；执行失败后返回失败推演的时间视觉证据，并在固定交互预算下评估修复能力。分别测量确定性有效性、动态功能成功率与失败后恢复三类指标。

Result: 在189个关卡上对六种代表性MLLM进行实验，结果显示：确定性有效性与动态成功之间存在显著差距；模型对材料预算高度敏感；在主要严格预算设定下，失败后恢复能力有限。

Conclusion: 该基准通过分离测量确定性有效性、动态功能成功与失败后恢复，能够定位设计失败的具体阶段，揭示当前MLLM从"结构合法"到"执行可行"及"失败可修复"之间仍存在明显能力缺口。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models, or MLLMs, perform well at visual understanding and structured generation, yet these capabilities do not establish whether an engineering design will work when executed. Existing benchmarks assess spatial reasoning, structural validity, or physics-grounded construction, but they do not determine whether MLLMs can synthesize complete load-bearing structures and repair them after simulator execution exposes a failure. We introduce PolyBridgeBench, an executable benchmark for multimodal bridge design. A model receives a visual scene and structured engineering constraints and generates a complete node--member--material topology. Deterministic legality checks gate execution in a native dynamic physics simulation. Following an execution failure, the benchmark returns temporal visual evidence from the failed rollout and evaluates repair under a fixed interaction budget. Separate measurements of deterministic validity, dynamic functional success, and post-failure recovery identify the stage at which design fails. Experiments with six representative MLLMs across 189 levels expose a substantial gap between deterministic validity and dynamic success, pronounced sensitivity to material budgets, and limited post-failure recovery under the primary strict-budget setting.

</details>


### [39] [The Communication Bottleneck: A Round-Trip Study of Tree-Structured Expression Serialization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21509)
*Xavier Suau,Alex Ferrando de las Morenas,Luca Zappella,Samy Bengio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作提出往返（round-trip）协议，量化大模型将树结构算术表达式序列化为自然语言后信息保留程度，发现信道有损且不对称，失败主要源于生成端且由树结构驱动，并证明该信道可通过少量微调显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当语言模型通过思维链或自由文本中间结果推理时，需要将结构化信息序列化为自然语言；但这一"瓶颈"究竟能保留多少树结构的组合性内容，缺乏精确、可量化的实证答案。

Method: 提出往返协议：生成器把过程化生成的算术表达式转化为应用题（word problem），独立的抽取器仅凭应用题还原表达式，并以符号等价性作为精确评判标准。对16个模型的全部两两组合进行评估，得到通信矩阵，其边际分布可分离生成质量与抽取质量；同时进行约3600条共享算子与树形的微调实验，以及新算子／新词表的不相交领域实验。

Result: （1）信道有损且不对称，互换生成端与抽取端可使准确率变化最高达60.4个百分点，最佳组合准确率92.9%，且来自两端使用不同模型而非同一模型；（2）至少73.6%的往返失败源自生成端，难度由树结构（算子数量、深度、右分支）而非模型家族决定；（3）信道可训练，约3600条微调样本使所有开源模型超过未训练的Gemini-3.1-Pro（同语义下的上界），在引入新算子与新词表的不相交领域也普遍提升，表明增益并非匹配语义的假象，但与前沿模型仍有差距。

Conclusion: 树结构表达式的序列化是模型以自然语言传递层次化结构时的主要限制因素；该限制既已被诊断，也可通过少量针对性微调得到缓解。

Abstract: When language models reason in chain-of-thought or exchange free-text intermediates, they serialize structured information into natural language. How much tree-structured compositional content survives this bottleneck? We propose a round-trip protocol that answers this question empirically for tree-structured expressions. A generator converts a procedurally generated arithmetic expression into a word problem, a separate extractor recovers the expression from the word problem alone, and symbolic equivalence provides an exact oracle. Evaluating all pairwise combinations of sixteen models yields a communication matrix whose marginals separate generation quality from extraction quality. Three main findings emerge. First, the channel is lossy and asymmetric: swapping which model generates and which extracts shifts accuracy by up to 60.4 points, and the best pair reaches 92.9% by combining different models on each end rather than the same model on both. Second, at least 73.6% of round-trip failures originate at generation, and difficulty is driven by tree structure (operator count, depth, right-branching) rather than model family. Third, the channel is trainable: ~3600 fine-tuning examples that share the evaluation's operators and tree shapes lift every open-weight model above untrained Gemini-3.1-Pro, an upper bound under matched semantics. A disjoint-domain regime with new operators and vocabulary also raises every open-weight model, confirming the gain is not an artifact of matched semantics, though a gap to the frontier remains. Together these results identify tree-structured expression serialization as a primary limiting factor when models communicate hierarchical structure through natural language.

</details>


### [40] [Learning-to-Optimize as the Missing Architectural Layer of AI-Native Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21519)
*Giambattista Amati,Federica Mangiatordi,Pierpaolo Salvo,Emiliano Pallotti,Simone Angelini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将 Learning-to-Optimize (L2O) 定位为 AI 原生通信网络中"优化与智能之间缺失的架构层"，把优化算法重新定义为离线知识生成器，为神经代理模型提供监督信号，并提出包含优化、知识生成、代理学习、运行时推理四阶段的通用工作流，最后以 NR-V2X 中继选择问题（MILP 求解器生成解训练 GNN 实现近实时近最优决策）作为实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来 AI 原生通信网络需要自主资源管理、自适应控制与零接触运维，AI 已成为其基础设计原则。现有 AI 原生架构虽在网络功能中广泛嵌入智能，但缺乏对"优化知识如何被系统性生成、迁移与利用"的架构级指导，即缺少连接优化与 AI 原生智能的中间抽象层。

Method: 论文提出以 L2O 作为架构抽象的范式：不再将优化仅视为在线决策引擎，而是将优化算法重新定义为离线知识生成器，产出高质量监督信息以训练神经代理模型，使模型继承优化专长同时支持低时延运行时推理。具体给出通用四阶段 L2O 工作流——优化、知识生成、代理学习、运行时推理；并以 NR-V2X 中继选择问题为案例，用 MILP 求解器产生的最优解训练图神经网络（GNN），实现实时近似最优决策。

Result: 在 NR-V2X 中继选择案例中，由 MILP 求解器生成的优化解成功训练出 GNN，该 GNN 能在运行时实时复现近最优决策，验证了所提四阶段 L2O 工作流的可行性。

Conclusion: 不同于主要聚焦算法加速的现有 L2O 方法，该框架将 L2O 确立为可跨异构通信与计算系统复用的架构抽象，并将 Learning-to-Optimize 定位为未来 AI 原生网络的关键架构使能者。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) is becoming a fundamental design principle of future AI-native communication networks, enabling autonomous resource management, adaptive control, and zero-touch network operation. While current AI-native architectures increasingly embed intelligence across network functions, they provide little guidance on how optimisation knowledge should be systematically generated, transferred, and exploited by AI models. This paper argues that the Learning-to-Optimize (L2O) represents the missing architectural layer between optimisation and AI-native intelligence. Rather than viewing optimisation merely as an online decision engine, the proposed paradigm redefines optimisation algorithms as offline knowledge generators that produce high-quality supervisory information for neural surrogate models. The resulting models inherit optimisation expertise while enabling low-latency runtime inference suitable for dynamic network environments. A generic four-stage L2O workflow is introduced, comprising optimisation, knowledge generation, surrogate learning, and runtime inference. Unlike existing Learning-to-Optimize approaches, which primarily focus on algorithm acceleration, the proposed framework establishes L2O as an architectural abstraction applicable across heterogeneous communication and computing systems. The proposed paradigm is illustrated by an NR-V2X relay-selection problem, in which optimisation-generated solutions from a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solver are used to train a Graph Neural Network that can reproduce near-optimal decisions in real time. The presented perspective positions Learning-to-Optimize as a key architectural enabler for future AI-native networks.

</details>


### [41] [Dual-Interest Sequential Product Recommendation With Multi-Granular SSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21548)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSRec，一种双兴趣跨SSM模型，通过长短期兴趣解耦与残差交叉融合，在序列推荐中兼顾效率与效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer序列推荐成本高；SSM方法虽高效但多将物品编码为单一静态上下文角色，忽视物品多义性（同一物品在不同用户上下文中具不同语义角色），且难以捕捉不同时间粒度上的动态行为。

Method: DSRec包含两条分支：长期兴趣分支通过历史聚合捕捉稳定偏好；短期兴趣分支由点击时间间隔调制，强调局部会话意图。二者分别由全序列Mamba（长期）和时间调制SSM（短期，依据时间间隔动态调整状态演化）编码，并采用残差交叉融合机制在保持语义独立的同时交换上下文信息，实现跨粒度对齐。

Result: 在公开基准数据集上的实验表明，DSRec优于其他最先进方法。

Conclusion: 显式解耦物品在不同时间粒度下的语义角色，并结合SSM的高效长程建模与跨粒度融合，可有效提升序列推荐性能，为物品多义性建模提供新思路。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation aims to predict the next item a user will interact with based on their historical behavior. Advances in Transformers have significantly improved sequential recommendation but are still limited by cost efficiency. Although State Space Models (SSMs) have recently enabled efficient long-range modeling, most existing methods encode each item with a single static contextual role, overlooking the phenomenon of item polysemy. In fact, the same item often plays different semantic roles depending on user context, and existing methods are limited in capturing dynamic behavior across different temporal granularities. In this work, we propose DSRec, a novel dual-interest cross-SSM model that explicitly disentangles item roles across long-term and short-term semantic context. Sequential items are encoded into long-term interest embeddings that capture stable preferences via historical aggregation, and a short-term interest branch that emphasizes local session intent modulated by inter-click time intervals. These interest embeddings are processed through distinct SSM encoders: a full-sequence Mamba for long-term modeling, and a time-modulated SSM that dynamically adjusts state evolution based on temporal gaps. To enable effective cross-granularity alignment, we adopt a residual cross-fusion mechanism that exchanges contextual information between the two branches while preserving semantic independence. Experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that DSRec outperforms other state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Accuracy: Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss for Structured Fraudulent Job Posting Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21599)
*Syed Ali Ahmed,Malaika Raza,Muhammad Shoaib Siddiqui,Muhammad Rafi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出质心引导对比损失（CGCL），将分类与密集聚类统一，通过质心驱动的top-k推拉机制重塑潜空间表征，在EMSCAD数据集上实现SOTA的虚假招聘广告检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假招聘广告检测方法难以同时兼顾高准确率与具有意义、能刻画虚假帖子细微差异的潜空间表征结构。

Method: 提出Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss（CGCL），将分类与密集聚类统一，通过质心驱动的top-k推拉机制一致性地重塑潜空间表征，兼顾类间可分性与类内紧凑性。

Result: 在公开基准数据集EMSCAD上进行大量实验，结果表明该方法取得了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: CGCL的两个组成部分互补，使模型能建立准确的决策边界并保持高聚类紧凑度，有效捕捉类可分性与潜空间结构；代码已开源。

Abstract: Fraudulent job posting detection aims to identify job advertisements that are corrupted either through fake content, misleading information, or negative intent, disrupting the online eco-system of job-seekers and employers. Existing studies in this domain lack effective methods to simultaneously achieve high accuracy and meaningful structure of latent-space representations that capture subtleties among fake posts. To this end, we propose Centroid-Guided Contrastive Loss (CGCL), a loss function which unifies classification with densely formulated clustering to consistently reshape latent-space through a centroid-driven top-$k$ push-and-pull mechanism. The complementary nature of CGCL enables the model to enforce accurate decision boundaries and maintain high clustering compactness, effectively capturing both class separability and latent structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance of our method on EMSCAD, a public benchmark dataset. The code associated with this work is available at: https://github.com/ali-ahmed925/CGCL_code

</details>


### [43] [Reducing Barriers to Academic Support: Evaluating a Course-Specific RAG System for Addressing Help-Seeking Disparities in Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21600)
*Andy Gray,Jake Hobbs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究设计并评估了课程专属RAG系统Beacon，以降低学生求助门槛。混合方法结果显示，学生认为其回答更贴合课程内容、比通用生成式AI更可信，可作为咨询教师前的第一支持渠道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术支持获取不均等：部分学生因焦虑、怕被评判、期望不明或信心不足而不愿求助。计算机教育中编程任务累积性强、认知负荷高，该问题尤为突出。通用生成式AI虽被广泛使用，但其回答可能不准确、缺乏情境化或与模块期望不符。

Method: 采用设计型研究（DBR）方法迭代开发Beacon——一个课程专属的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，提供私密、即时、与模块对齐的学术支持；回答以经审核的教学材料为依据，旨在降低求助障碍并促进自主学习。采用混合方法评估，结合问卷与半结构化访谈，对象为某高校的学生与教职员工。

Result: 学生认为Beacon的回答与模块内容高度一致，比无限制的生成式AI工具更可信；尤其认可其使用伪代码和脚手架式讲解而非直接给出答案。参与者对未经核实的AI生成回答仍持谨慎态度，但将其视为咨询讲师或官方资源之前的有价值的第一支持点。

Conclusion: 精心设计的课程专属AI系统可占据自主学习与正式支持之间的中间地带，从而降低学术支持获取障碍。教育AI并非取代教育者，其最大价值在于拓宽获取指导的渠道，同时保留讲师的教学角色。

Abstract: Access to academic support is a key determinant of student success, yet students experience it unequally: some readily seek help from lecturers or tutors, while others hesitate due to anxiety, fear of judgement, uncertainty about expectations, or low confidence in their understanding. This may be especially evident in computing education, where programming tasks are cumulative and cognitively demanding. Although students increasingly turn to general-purpose generative AI tools, these can produce responses that are inaccurate, insufficiently contextualised, or misaligned with module expectations. This study presents and evaluates Beacon, a course-specific Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system providing private, immediate, module-aligned academic support. Grounding responses in approved teaching materials, Beacon was designed to lower barriers to help-seeking while encouraging independent learning. Using a design-based research approach, Beacon was developed iteratively and evaluated via mixed methods, combining questionnaires and semi-structured interviews with students and staff at a Higher Education institution. Students described Beacon's responses as closely aligned with module content and more trustworthy than unrestricted generative AI tools, valuing its use of pseudocode and scaffolded explanations over direct solutions. Although participants remained cautious about trusting AI-generated responses without verification, they viewed the system as a valuable first point of support before consulting lecturers or official resources. The findings suggest that carefully designed course-specific AI systems may reduce barriers to academic support by occupying an intermediary space between independent study and formal support. Rather than replacing educators, educational AI may be most valuable when it broadens access to guidance while preserving the pedagogical role of lecturers.

</details>


### [44] [Calibrating Teacher--Student Discrepancy for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21619)
*Qiangqiang He,Jin Li,MingCai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cal-OPD通过正负特权干预估计教师自身偏差区域，仅保留超出该区域的师生差异作为优化信号（约原始信号的52–65%），在数学推理基准上一致优于标准OPD及其变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准on-policy蒸馏所学的token级师生差异并非纯粹反映师生能力差距，还混入了教师自身的偏差（teacher self-deviation）。在privileged OPD中特权信息导致教师侧似然漂移更大，学生被诱导学习更多教师自身偏差，问题被进一步放大。

Method: 提出Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (Cal-OPD)：通过正向与负向特权干预（positive and negative privileged interventions）估计教师的自身偏差区域，并据此校准原始师生差异，仅保留落在该区域之外的分量作为蒸馏优化信号。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，Cal-OPD仅保留约52–65%的原始师生差异作为优化信号，却在不同模型规模上一致超过标准OPD及其变体。

Conclusion: 去除教师自身偏差成分可使on-policy蒸馏的优化信号更准确地刻画真实能力差距，从而以更少但更纯净的信号获得更优的推理性能，且方法具备跨模型规模的一致性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves reasoning models by learning the token-level discrepancy between a stronger teacher and an on-policy student. However, this discrepancy does not purely reflect the capability gap between the teacher and the student: it also contains deviations arising from the teacher itself, which are consequently mixed into the observed teacher--student discrepancy and indiscriminately learned by standard OPD during training. This issue is further exacerbated by privileged OPD, where privileged information induces larger teacher-side likelihood shifts, thereby encouraging the student to learn more of the teacher's own deviation. We introduce \textbf{Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (Cal-OPD)}, which estimates the teacher's self-deviation region through positive and negative privileged interventions and calibrates the original teacher--student discrepancy by retaining only the component that lies beyond this region. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that, while retaining only about 52--65\% of the original teacher--student discrepancy as the optimization signal, Cal-OPD consistently outperforms standard OPD and its variants across model scales.

</details>


### [45] [One Prompt Does Not Fit All: Self-Meta-Evolve for Personalized Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21626)
*Hongliang Li,Lu Wang,Yong Xu,Hanyang Chen,Zhitao Hou,Xiaoting Qin,Song Ge,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将企业信息抽取重新定义为面向用户的提示自适应问题，提出双层元演化框架Self-Meta-Evolve，为每位用户维护专属提示并借助交互反馈持续优化，同时发布基于O*NET的人物画像驱动基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化方法依赖单一提示并针对全局目标优化，忽视了真实工作场景中用户需求的高度异质性；而企业信息抽取中同一文档往往需要针对不同用户进行不同重组。

Method: 提出Self-Meta-Evolve分层框架：为每位用户维护专用提示，通过双循环过程持续精化——内循环基于人物画像条件反馈编辑结构化提示，外循环通过蒸馏成功的编辑模式来演化元提示本身；并构建含292个模拟企业用户的人物画像驱动IE基准及基于O*NET职业分类的可复现人物画像生成流程。

Result: 在该基准上，Self-Meta-Evolve成功率达74.58%，比最强提示优化基线高出13.56个百分点，仅两次迭代即达52.54%；二十位真实从业者参与的双盲人工研究显示，其自适应提示在71%的成对比较中胜过静态基线。

Conclusion: 面向用户的提示自适应是企业信息抽取的更合理范式，双层元演化框架在自动基准与真实人工评估中均显著优于静态全局提示优化方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for enterprise information extraction (IE), where the same document must be reorganized differently for each user. Existing prompt optimization methods, however, rely on a single prompt optimized against a global objective, which is misaligned with the inherent user heterogeneity of real workplaces. We formulate enterprise IE as per-user prompt adaptation under interaction feedback and propose Self-Meta-Evolve, a hierarchical framework that maintains a dedicated prompt for each user and continuously refines it through a dual-loop process: an inner loop that edits structured prompts based on persona-conditioned feedback, and an outer loop that evolves the meta-prompt itself by distilling successful editing patterns. To enable scalable training and evaluation, we release a persona-driven IE benchmark of 292 simulated enterprise users, paired with a reproducible persona-generation pipeline grounded in O*NET occupational taxonomies. On this benchmark, Self-Meta-Evolve achieves a 74.58% success rate, outperforming the strongest prompt-optimization baseline by 13.56 absolute points, and reaches 52.54\% within only two iterations. A double-blind human study with twenty real professionals further confirms that prompts adapted by our framework win against static baselines in 71% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [46] [Accelerating Dense LLMs via L0-regularized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21672)
*Zhenyu Zhang,Jiudong Yang,Zhaowen Tao,Meng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出L0-MoE，一种基于L0正则化的轻量级MoE方法，可在几乎不损失性能的前提下加速稠密LLM，最高获得2.5倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM性能强但推理慢、成本高；现有加速方法会造成明显性能下降，而传统MoE模型需要大量计算资源。

Method: 采用L0正则化的轻量级MoE，引入聚类混淆矩阵进行领域感知的数据集构建，并使用动态批处理实现高效训练。

Result: 相比稠密模型实现最高2.5倍加速，同时保持有竞争力的性能，优于现有LLM加速基线方法。

Conclusion: L0-MoE是一种在保持性能的同时有效加速稠密LLM的轻量级方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance but suffer from slow and costly inference. Existing acceleration methods often lead to noticeable performance degradation, while Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models require extensive computational resources. In this paper, we propose L0-MoE, a lightweight MoE approach using L0-regularization to accelerate dense LLMs nearly without performance loss. Our method introduces a cluster confusion matrix for domain-aware dataset curation and applies dynamic batching for efficient training. Experiments show that L0-MoE achieves up to 2.5x speedup over dense models while maintaining competitive performance, outperforming existing LLM acceleration baselines.

</details>


### [47] [GUARD: Natural Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models via Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21677)
*Zeyu Yan,Guanghao Zhou,Minghui Qiu,Ming Gao,Cen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GUARD框架，通过引导答案-推理蒸馏将不安全披露转化为"自然遗忘轨迹"，并引入NFRS指标评估替代质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大推理模型（LRM）的思维链（CoT）使得受保护事实或不安全推理事先出现在中间推理步骤中，而现有遗忘方法只压制目标内容或重定向内部表征，未规定遗忘后轨迹应如何延续，导致幻觉性替代、边界畸形或重复输出。

Method: GUARD将模型生成的不安全披露转化为安全退出轨迹，用引导令牌（guidance tokens）对齐冻结的LRM，再将引导行为蒸馏进模型参数；同时提出自然遗忘推理分数（NFRS）度量结构稳定性、流畅性与无支撑替代内容。

Result: 在R-TOFU及基于STAR-1衍生的有害意图设定上，GUARD在两个主流蒸馏LRM上显著减少不安全与隐私披露，同时保持推理效用。

Conclusion: LRM遗忘应学习"自然遗忘轨迹"而非仅压制目标内容，GUARD与NFRS为遗忘替代质量提供了新的方法与评估视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in large reasoning models (LRMs) have made machine unlearning more challenging, as protected facts or unsafe rationales may surface in intermediate chain-of-thought (CoT) traces before the final answer is produced. Existing unlearning objectives typically suppress the target content or redirect internal representations, but they never specify how the post-forgetting trajectory should continue, which can lead to hallucinated substitutes, malformed boundaries, or repetitive outputs. We argue that LRM unlearning should instead learn a natural forgetting trajectory: a coherent non-disclosing CoT followed by a stable refusal-style answer that replace the original disclosure. To this end, we propose Guided Answer-Reasoning Distillation (GUARD), which converts model-generated unsafe disclosures into safe-exit trajectories, aligns a frozen LRM via guidance tokens, and distills the guided behavior into model parameters.To address the lack of metrics for replacement quality beyond leakage, we further introduce Natural Forgetting Reasoning Score (NFRS), which captures structural stability, fluency, and unsupported substitutes in forgotten outputs. Extensive experiments on R-TOFU and a STAR-1-derived harmful-intent setting show that GUARD substantially reduces unsafe and privacy disclosures across two widely adopted distilled LRMs while preserving reasoning utility. Codes are available at https://github.com/zeyu-Yan/GUARD

</details>


### [48] [Listen Before You Speak: Response Planning from Listener Facial Reactions for Conversational Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21683)
*Yunji Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReACT-TTS 两阶段框架，利用对话中听者一秒的前置面部序列来规划下一句话的情绪与韵律，再进行语音合成，并在 MELD 双人对话协议上通过实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话语音不仅依赖文本内容，还取决于对话上下文以及听者在说话人开口前的即时反应行为。现有方法多仅依赖文本条件，忽略了听者前置行为这一互补线索，因此需要引入听者前置面部动态来指导回应规划。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段以一秒的听者前置面部序列作为条件，规划下一句的情绪与韵律；第二阶段与 Grad-TTS 骨干连接，实现端到端语音生成。实验在严格的 MELD 双人对话协议上进行，比较 Temporal（时序视觉条件）与 Text-only 条件，并开展消融实验（视觉变体、早晚期差异建模、正确听者反应与循环错配对比），同时进行由 20 位语音研究者参与的语境适当性主观评测。

Result: 在十个随机种子上，Temporal 条件的平均宏 F1 与 VAD 一致性均高于 Text-only，而准确率基本不变；消融显示时序建模在视觉变体中表现最佳，显式的早晚期差异并非必要，正确的听者反应平均优于循环错配。主观评测中 76% 判断偏好 Temporal，9% 偏好 Text-only，15% 无偏好。

Conclusion: 结果表明听者前置动态可作为对话回应规划的互补线索，支持在语音合成中引入听者前置行为信息；代码已开源。

Abstract: Conversational speech depends on dialogue context and the listener's immediately preceding behavior. We propose ReACT-TTS, a two-stage framework that uses a one-second pre-response listener facial sequence to plan the next utterance's emotion and prosody before speech realization. On a strict dyadic MELD protocol, Temporal conditioning yields higher mean macro-F1 and VAD concordance than Text-only across ten seeds, while accuracy remains essentially unchanged. Ablations show that temporal modeling performs best among the visual variants and that an explicit early-to-late difference is unnecessary; correct listener reactions also outperform cyclic mismatches on average. In a contextual-appropriateness study with 20 speech researchers, 76% of judgments prefer Temporal, 9% Text-only, and 15% report no preference. We further connect the predicted response style to a Grad-TTS backbone for end-to-end speech realization. Overall, the results support pre-response listener dynamics as complementary cues for conversational response planning. The source code is available at https://github.com/CYJ1/ReACT-TTS_public.

</details>


### [49] [World Modeling in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21748)
*Pierre Beckmann,Matthieu Queloz,Andre Freitas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 行为失败可能掩盖Transformer已学到的忠实环境表征；通过机制分析与因果干预，可将失败溯源至叠加的路口特征干扰，并据此提出机制指标比较不同模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究常以行为失败（如导航错误）推断Transformer缺乏世界模型，但行为表现与内部表征可能不一致，需要机制层面的证据来判定模型是否真正建模了环境，并解释失败来源。

Method: 以在曼哈顿随机游走上训练的Transformer（TaxiGPT）为对象，采用机制分析与因果干预，考察其对路口、街道、自身位置、目标罗盘的表征；分析路口特征叠加导致的干扰；提出"affordance packing"（将合法移动相同的路口表征分组）以限制错误后果；并提出机制性指标用于跨模型比较。

Result: 模型确实表征了路口与街道、追踪自身位置并利用目标罗盘导航；其失败源于叠加路口特征之间的干扰，破坏内部地图中的定位。Affordance packing可减轻这些错误的后果；所提机制指标显示世界建模能力在不同训练阶段依次涌现。

Conclusion: 研究主张从"模型是否拥有世界模型"转向机制性地研究其世界建模过程，即表征环境并据此指导行为的相互作用能力。

Abstract: Behavioral failures can make a transformer appear to lack a world model even when it has learned faithful representations of its environment. We demonstrate this in TaxiGPT, a transformer trained on random walks through Manhattan whose failures have been interpreted as evidence of an incoherent internal map. Through mechanistic analysis and causal interventions, we show that the model represents intersections and streets, tracks its position, and uses a goal compass to navigate. We trace its failures to interference between superposed intersection features, which disrupts localization within the internal map. Affordance packing, which groups representations of intersections with the same legal moves, helps limit the consequences of these errors. Finally, we propose mechanistic indicators that we use to compare models and show that world-modeling capacities emerge at different stages of training. Our findings motivate a shift from asking whether a model has a world model to mechanistically studying its world modeling: the interacting capacities through which it represents its environment and uses those representations to guide behavior.

</details>


### [50] [ECG Mirage: Revealing and Mitigating the Underutilisation of ECGs in Vision-Language Models for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21755)
*Jinning Liang,Mingcheng Zhu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出"ECG Mirage（心电图海市蜃楼）"这一多模态临床预测的失效模式，并利用视觉提示调优作为缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急诊科决策依赖病史、生命体征、化验和心电图等异质临床信息。视觉-语言模型（VLM）可联合处理这些模态，但预测性能强并不意味着模型真正利用了正确患者的心电图信息，存在"表面多模态能力"的隐患。

Method: 将ECG Mirage细分为两种形式：ECG忽视（ECG几乎无预测增益）与ECG混淆（匹配ECG优于无图像输入，但不优于不匹配ECG）。在MDS-ED数据集上，固定临床文本和预测目标，对比匹配ECG、结局不一致的错配ECG和无图像输入三种输入的预测结果，评估四个VLM。随后在冻结VLM主干的前提下，先用监督学习、再用条件直接偏好优化（cDPO）训练四个受限视觉提示。

Result: 四个VLM在ICU入住和临床恶化两个任务上，匹配ECG均未带来一致优势（即存在ECG Mirage）。经过训练后的模型在ICU入住预测上达到70.6%的平衡准确率，临床恶化预测达67.5%，并将匹配与错配之间的性能差距分别扩大到约16.5和5.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究识别了多模态临床预测中的ECG Mirage现象，并提出视觉提示调优作为一种高效、低成本的缓解策略，使模型真正依赖患者特异性的ECG信息。

Abstract: Emergency department (ED) decision-making relies on heterogeneous clinical information, including patient history, vital signs, laboratory results, and electrocardiograms (ECGs). Vision--language models (VLMs) can jointly process these modalities, but strong predictive performance does not necessarily imply meaningful use of the correct patient's ECG. We term this failure mode ECG Mirage: apparent multimodal capability without useful dependence on patient-specific ECG information. We distinguish two forms: ECG neglect, where ECGs provide little predictive benefit, and ECG confusion, where matched ECGs outperform no-image inputs but not mismatched ECGs. To evaluate these behaviours, we compare predictions obtained with matched ECGs, outcome-discordant mismatched ECGs, and no-image inputs while holding the clinical text and prediction targets fixed. Across four VLMs on MDS-ED, matched ECGs provide no consistent advantage for either ICU admission or clinical deterioration prediction. We then train four restricted visual prompts using supervised learning followed by conditional direct preference optimisation, while keeping the VLM backbone frozen. The resulting models achieve balanced accuracies of 70.6% for ICU admission and 67.5% for deterioration and increase the matched-versus-mismatched performance gap to approximately 16.5 and 5.5 percentage points, respectively. Overall, our study identifies ECG Mirage in multimodal clinical prediction and introduces visual prompt tuning as an efficient mitigation strategy.

</details>


### [51] [LLM-Generated Feature Pools for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21801)
*Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Corinne Ancourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一个简单统计流水线在无监督单变量时间序列异常检测中，关键在于候选统计特征池的构建；用多模态LLM按域生成候选池并与手工池合并选择，可将VUS-PR提升至0.588。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格选择协议下，简单统计方法在单变量时间序列异常检测中能达到何种水平，并定位性能瓶颈究竟来自特征池、评分模型还是特征子集搜索。

Method: 在滑动窗口上提取少量统计量构成候选池，用直推式鲁棒（MAD）模型对窗口打分，并借助留出调优集按域选择特征子集；通过三种选择策略、事后oracle、聚合网格与候选池消融定位关键因素；进而用多模态LLM结合域内上下文示例窗口按域生成候选池，并在生成池与手工池的并集上做选择。

Result: 在TSB-AD-U上达到每序列VUS-PR 0.529，高于公开榜单最佳神经方法（0.45）与统计方法（0.44），并接近最强预训练基础模型（差距0.06）；消融显示选择策略与oracle带来0.031波动、聚合网格0.096，而更换候选池带来0.226；生成池在匹配选择下与手工池相当且覆盖不同域，二者并集选择在全部十二组生成器-随机种子对上均优于生成池，将流水线提升至0.588。

Conclusion: 候选池设定性能上限，特征子集搜索仅为二阶因素；简单统计流水线配合LLM生成的多域候选池，在严格无（或弱）监督协议下即可匹配榜单最优结果，表明统计特征空间的设计比复杂模型与搜索更关键。

Abstract: We study how far a simple statistical pipeline can go on univariate time series anomaly detection under a strict selection protocol. The method extracts a small pool of statistics over sliding windows, scores each window with a transductive robust (MAD) model, and selects a feature subset per domain on a held-out tuning split. On TSB-AD-U it reaches $0.529$ per-series VUS-PR, above the best neural ($0.45$) and statistical ($0.44$) entries on the public leaderboard and within $0.06$ of the strongest pretrained foundation model, several of which use more supervision than ours. Ablations locate the cause: across three selection strategies and a hindsight oracle the score moves by $0.031$, and across the aggregation grid by $0.096$, while changing the candidate pool moves it by $0.226$. The candidate pool sets the ceiling; the search over it is second-order. We therefore generate a pool per domain by prompting a multimodal LLM with in-context example windows from that domain. The generated pools match the hand-crafted one under matched selection, and the two cover different domains: selecting over their union improves on the generated pool in all twelve generator-seed pairs and lifts the pipeline to $0.588$, matching the performance of the best entry on the leaderboard.

</details>


### [52] [MIST: Multimodal Survival Prediction with Genomic-Guided Histology Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21811)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Richard R. Chen,Jana Lipkova,Benedikt Wiestler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIST：以基因组特征为查询token，对基础模型衍生的病理切片上下文token进行注意力查询，实现紧凑高效的多模态生存预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生存模型可融合全切片图像（WSI）与基因组信息的互补预后价值，但面临外部队列分布漂移（cohort shift）与计算复杂度高的双重挑战；此外，常规方法仅在最终阶段合并各自独立编码的模态，融合不够充分。

Method: MIST（Multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention）：将基因组特征表示为token，令其在生存预测前查询由基础模型导出的紧凑病理上下文token，从而在早期即用组织学上下文丰富分子信息，而非仅在末段融合。训练目标为离散时间生存预测，并结合基因组特征掩码（genomic feature masking）、WSI dropout与配对WSI-基因组对比对齐。

Result: 在结肠、肾脏、肺及胶质母细胞瘤四个队列的外部评估中，MIST在主要比较项上的外部C-index优于标准融合基线。

Conclusion: 基因组引导的组织学注意力是一种紧凑且有效的多模态肿瘤预后预测策略。代码已开源：https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST

Abstract: Multimodal survival models can combine complementary prognostic information from whole-slide images and genomic profiles, but effective fusion remains challenging amid external cohort shift and computational complexity. To address these challenges, we propose MIST, multimodal survival prediction with genomic-guided histology attention. MIST represents genomic features as tokens and allows them to query compact foundation-model-derived histology context tokens before survival prediction. This design enriches molecular information with histology context rather than merging separately encoded modalities only at the final stage. Training combines discrete-time survival prediction with genomic feature masking, WSI dropout, and paired WSI-genomics contrastive alignment. Across four external evaluations in colon, renal, lung, and glioblastoma cohorts, MIST improves external C-index over standard fusion baselines in the primary comparisons. These results support genomic-guided histology attention as a compact and effective strategy for multimodal oncology outcome prediction. Our code is available at https://github.com/samiyavuuz/MIST .

</details>


### [53] [EnterpriseVal: Quantifying the Efficacy, Reliability and Value of Generative AI in the Enterprise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21841)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EnterpriseVal——一个用例级的企业大模型评估系统，用于把"模型能做什么"的公开基准测试转化为"该工作流在本地数据与管控下是否值得部署扩展"的决策依据，并给出全球银行三流程试点证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型在专业交付物上已接近人类水平，但多数企业生成式AI项目未产生可衡量商业效果，大量智能体项目预计被取消。作者认为这本质是测量问题：公开基准回答的是"模型能做什么"，而部署决策需要回答"该工作流在这里、用我们的数据、在我们的管控下是否适配、可靠、安全且值得扩展"。

Method: EnterpriseVal 由五部分组成：(i) 用例与冻结的社会技术配置的形式化规格，涵盖模型、提示词、检索、工具、护栏与人工监督，并以自主性等级与后果等级共同确定所需评估强度；(ii) 覆盖保真度、效用、效率、可靠性、保证与监督的指标目录；(iii) 通过预测驱动推断（prediction-powered inference）将盲评专家判断与经校准的LLM-as-judge评分相结合的评分协议；(iv) 两级阈值门，以可执行算法形式给出，将带置信界的指标向量映射为 REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE 决策；(v) 价值-风险模型，其中审核者捕获率为实测参数。

Result: 在全球银行三个工作流的试点中：信贷备忘录撰写任务上，最优模型的人工评分引用精确率达88%（门槛70%），幻觉率1.6%（门槛5%）；流程改造任务中，分析师精修工作量从估计的每份文档27.4小时降至2.9小时。

Conclusion: 论文区分了已确立结果、试点证据、所提系统与开放假设，并明确了完成全面验证所需的实验。

Abstract: Frontier language models now produce professional deliverables that expert graders judge to match human work on a substantial share of economically valuable tasks, yet most enterprise GenAI initiatives fail to show a measurable business effect and a large fraction of agentic projects are expected to be cancelled. We argue that this is substantially a measurement problem: public benchmarks answer "what can the model do?", whereas a deployment decision requires "is this workflow fit, reliable, safe and worth scaling - here, on our data, under our controls?". We present EnterpriseVal, a use-case-level evaluation system that closes this gap. It comprises (i) a formal specification of the use case and of the frozen socio-technical configuration under test, model, prompts, retrieval, tools, guardrails and human oversight, with an autonomy level and consequence tier that jointly set the required evaluation intensity; (ii) a metric catalogue spanning fidelity, utility, efficiency, reliability, assurance and oversight; (iii) a grading protocol that scales blinded expert judgement with calibrated LLM-as-judge scoring through prediction-powered inference; (iv) a two-tier threshold gate, stated as an executable algorithm, that maps metric vectors with confidence bounds to REJECT/CONDITIONAL/SCALE decisions; and (v) a value-and-risk model in which the reviewer catch rate is a measured parameter. We report a pilot across three workflows in a global bank. In credit-memo drafting, human-graded citation precision reached 88% and hallucination rate 1.6% for the best model against gates of 70% and 5%; in procedure transformation, analyst refinement effort fell from an estimated 27.4 to 2.9 hours per document. We separate established results, documented pilot evidence, the proposed system and open hypotheses, and specify the experiments required for full validation

</details>


### [54] [What Should We Ask Next? Retrieval-Aware Question Learning under Partial Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21924)
*Lyucheng Qian,John Yuehan Zhang,Pingyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAVEL proposes an online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification question policy, optimizing with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing interactive retrieval systems train question selection by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, but question value actually depends on the elicited response and its downstream effect on retrieval. Discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide only weak supervision.

Method: RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy via retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop.

Result: On Interactive-PEDES, RAVEL yields progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. It reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, providing more useful retrieval evidence and the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

Conclusion: Retrieval-aware online RL with downstream rank feedback is more effective than offline imitation for interactive retrieval under partial evidence.

Abstract: Interactive retrieval under partial evidence is a sequential information-acquisition problem: an agent must decide which question will create the most useful evidence for the next retrieval update. Existing systems train this decision by imitating an offline ordering of candidate QA pairs, although question value is determined by the response it elicits and its downstream effect on retrieval. We establish that candidate discriminativeness and perceived usefulness provide weak supervision for this objective, then introduce RAVEL, a retrieval-aware online reinforcement learning framework for interactive person re-identification. RAVEL initializes from supervised question generation, observes the current Top-4 candidates directly, and optimizes the question policy with rank feedback from the full question-answer-retrieval loop. Experiments on Interactive-PEDES show that RAVEL delivers progressively stronger retrieval performance across five interaction rounds. Further analysis shows that RAVEL reallocates the questioning budget toward localized open-ended attributes, which provide more useful retrieval evidence and yield the largest gains on initially difficult queries.

</details>


### [55] [AutoViewMem: Self-Configuring Orthogonal Views for Conversational Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21940)
*Zijie Cao,Xijun Qu,Zhicheng Gu,Xiaoshu Chen,Duanyang Yuan,Yanning Hou,Sihang Zhou,Jianxing Gong,Jian Huang,Yang Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoViewMem，一种数据驱动的长期对话记忆框架，在写入阶段将记忆组织为自配置、低重叠的语义视图，从而把语义解耦从检索阶段前移到写入阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体记忆系统多采用固定粒度或静态模式，当偏好、事件、约束、时序更新等异构信息混杂于同一表示时会产生语义干扰，导致 top-K 检索对噪声敏感、相关证据排序靠后，难以维持长程交互的一致性与个性化。

Method: AutoViewMem 从交互轨迹中发现候选视图，选择紧凑且互补的视图集合，并以此指导写入时的结构化抽取，生成可溯源（provenance-grounded）的记忆；再用离线整合提升记忆的紧凑性与一致性，使标准 top-K 相似度检索即可获取聚焦证据，无需显式路由或迭代检索。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 与 PersonaMem 基准上，以 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 为骨干模型，AutoViewMem 在长程问答与个性化任务上优于强记忆基线，同时保持简单的推理流程。

Conclusion: 该工作表明“表示优先”的设计思路能有效缓解记忆的语义干扰，在不增加检索复杂度的情况下提升长程对话智能体的记忆质量与一致性。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for large language model (LLM) agents to maintain consistency and personalization over extended interactions. Existing memory systems typically rely on fixed granularities or static schemas, but these designs struggle when heterogeneous information, such as preferences, events, constraints, and temporal updates, is embedded in a single mixed representation. The resulting semantic interference makes top-K retrieval sensitive to noise and often leaves relevant evidence poorly ranked. We present AutoViewMem, a data-driven framework that organizes long-term conversational memory into self-configuring, low-overlap semantic views before indexing. AutoViewMem discovers candidate views from interaction traces, selects a compact complementary view set, and uses these views to guide write-time structured extraction of provenance-grounded memories. This representation-first design moves semantic disentanglement from retrieval time to write time, allowing standard top-K similarity search to retrieve focused evidence without explicit routing or iterative retrieval. We further apply offline consolidation to improve memory compactness and consistency. Experiments on the LoCoMo and PersonaMem benchmarks, under both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B backbones, show that AutoViewMem improves long-horizon question answering and personalization over strong memory baselines while preserving a simple inference pipeline.

</details>


### [56] [Learning Cardiac Features: ECG Biometrics Across Time and~Exercise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21962)
*Luca Thiebaud,Paul Chauchat,Mustapha Ouladsine,Stéphane Delliaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在运动应激与跨会话条件下评估心电生物特征识别，采用孪生ResNet与多导联晚期融合，在自建运动试验数据集与公开基准上验证了心电身份识别的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电信号蕴含个体特异性模式，可用于身份识别，并有助于保护心脏敏感数据及作为自监督学习的预训练任务。但现有研究多局限于单会话静息状态，对时间与生理变化下的鲁棒性缺乏验证，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 采用孪生ResNet（Siamese ResNet）并结合多导联晚期融合策略；训练数据来自心肺运动试验提取的大规模心电数据集；评估采用运动与时间感知协议，并在公开基准数据集上进行测试。

Result: 在生理与时间双重变化条件下，会话内静息到峰值运动的等错误率（EER）为1.7%；在CYBHi数据集上取得3.9%的最优性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持存在一种对生理和时间漂移具有鲁棒性的内在心脏特征签名，说明心电生物特征识别在真实动态条件下具备可行性与应用潜力。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) carry subject-specific patterns enabling reliable individual discrimination, forming the basis of ECG biometrics. Beyond authentication, this paradigm holds significant potential to secure sensitive cardiac data and to serve as a pretext task in self-supervised learning. Yet, most studies remain confined to singlesession, resting data, leaving robustness to temporal and physiological variations largely untested. We address this gap by evaluating ECG biometrics under realistic conditions involving exercise-induced stress and cross-session variability. A Siamese ResNet with late multi-lead fusion strategy is trained on a large ECG dataset extracted from cardiopulmonary exercise tests and evaluated with a exercise-and time-aware protocol, as well as on public benchmarks. This first extensive assessment of ECG biometrics under combined physiological and temporal variability achieves an intra-session rest-to-peak EER of 1.7% and stateof-the-art 3.9% on the CYBHi dataset. Findings support the presence of an intrinsic cardiac signature resilient to physiological and temporal drift.

</details>


### [57] [A Lie Detector Test for Language Models: Reading Knowledge a Model Won't Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21996)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PIR方法，通过读取模型内部状态识别其"知道但隐藏"的答案，可区分"不愿答"与"不能答"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可能持有未报告的知识：在能力评估中"放水(sandbag)"，或输出与内部知识相悖的答案。仅凭输出无法判断模型是隐藏答案还是根本没有该知识。需要一种无参考的方法检测模型的"内隐识别"。

Method: 借鉴取证学的隐藏信息测试(Concealed Information Test)——向受试者呈现真实细节与干扰项，测量其对识别项更强的反应。方法PIR(Probe of Internal Recognition)在模型内部做同样的事：给出问题及候选答案，从模型内部状态读出其识别为正确的候选。PIR无需诚实的参考模型，也无需标注真值语料(reference-free)。

Result: 在5个家族(Gemma、Qwen、Llama、Mistral、Phi)的8个模型上，PIR以0.70–0.87的平衡准确率恢复被识别的答案，显著高于未知项基线0.28–0.40和随机率0.25。在各类隐藏形式下(提示性欺骗、训练出的sandbagging、外部密码锁和circuit-broken检查点)识别率保持0.85–0.93。模型隐藏已知答案时识别率仍高；当遗忘学习(unlearning)移除该知识时，识别率降至模型从未知晓该问题的水平。

Conclusion: PIR能区分"不愿回答"与"不能回答"的模型，支持sandbagging审计与遗忘学习验证。该信号具因果性，提供超越黑箱行为线索的信息，并从选择题扩展到自由生成。

Abstract: Large language models can hold knowledge they do not report. A model may sandbag on a capability evaluation, or answer against what it internally knows, and its outputs alone cannot tell whether it is hiding an answer or simply does not have one. We borrow the Concealed Information Test, a forensic method that identifies guilty knowledge by presenting a suspect with the true detail among plausible decoys and measuring a stronger response to the item they recognize. Our method, Probe of Internal Recognition (PIR), does the same inside a model. It presents a question with its candidate answers and reads, from the model's internal states, which candidate the model recognizes as correct. PIR is reference-free, needing no honest reference model and no labeled truth corpus. Across eight models from five families (Gemma, Qwen, Llama, Mistral, and Phi), PIR recovers the recognized answer at 0.70 to 0.87 balanced accuracy, well above the 0.28 to 0.40 unknown-item baseline and the 0.25 chance rate. It stays readable across every form of concealment we test, from prompted deception and trained sandbagging to external password-locked and circuit-broken checkpoints, with recognition between 0.85 and 0.93. When the model hides a known answer, recognition stays high. When unlearning removes the knowledge, recognition drops to the level of a question the model never knew. PIR therefore separates a model that will not answer from one that cannot, which supports sandbagging audits and unlearning verification. The signal is causal, adds information beyond black-box behavioral cues, and extends from multiple-choice questions to free-form generation.

</details>


### [58] [CodeMidas: Scaling Agentic Coding RL Environments from Code Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22068)
*Bowen Ye,Lei Li,Shicheng Li,Zihao Yue,Linghao Zhang,Hanglong Lv,Yuanxin Liu,Wenhan Ma,Hao Tian,Rang Li,Jinhao Dong,Yikai Zhao,Xiangwei Deng,Hailin Zhang,Liang Zhao,Qi Liu,Lingpeng Kong,Tong Yang,Fuli Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CodeMidas，一个仅以源码为任务输入、通过 agentic 流水线将已有代码库中已实现的功能转化为可执行 RL 环境的框架，构建了 5,545 个训练任务的数据集，并用 GRPO 训练 MiMo-V2.5，在五类基准上均取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练具备编程能力的智能体需要大量带可靠验证器的多样化任务。开源代码库是这类任务的丰富来源，但现有方法依赖 issue、commit 等开发过程产物，只能抽取有限类型的任务，难以规模化构建 RL 环境。因此作者希望直接以源代码作为唯一任务特有输入，扩大可抽取任务的范围。

Method: 提出 CodeMidas 这一 agentic 流水线，将现有代码库中的已实现功能转为可执行 RL 环境。流水线在环境构建的每个阶段都分配智能体算力：由智能体探索已实现功能以形成行为规格（behavioral specification）；基于原始代码的实际执行构造测试；再通过执行检查与重复解 rollout 来验证和筛选候选任务。

Result: 最终数据集包含来自 3,185 个开源代码库的 5,545 个训练任务，覆盖 23 种编程语言和 15 个技术领域。用 GRPO 训练 MiMo-V2.5 后，在五个不同基准上均获提升：问题修复（DeepSWE +11.7%）、整程序构建（ProgramBench +17%）、终端任务（Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%）。消融实验表明增加高质量训练任务数量可提升性能；轨迹分析显示 RL 训练后的智能体表现出更多代码库探索和更多样化自验证等更优行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，源代码可作为可扩展的基础来构建 RL 环境，从而在多样化软件任务上提升编程智能体的能力。

Abstract: Training capable coding agents via reinforcement learning (RL) requires diverse tasks with reliable verifiers. Open-source codebases offer a rich source of such tasks, while existing methods typically rely on development artifacts such as issues and commits, limiting the range of tasks that can be extracted. To better scale RL environments, we present CodeMidas, an agentic pipeline that turns implemented functionality in existing codebases into executable RL environments using source code as its only task-specific input. CodeMidas allocates agentic compute to every stage of environment construction: agents explore implemented functionality to formulate behavioral specifications, construct tests grounded in execution of the original code, and validate and filter candidate tasks through execution checks and repeated solution rollouts. The resulting dataset has 5,545 training tasks from 3,185 open-source codebases spanning 23 programming languages and 15 technical domains. Training MiMo-V2.5 on these tasks with GRPO improves performance on all five diverse benchmarks, covering issue repair (DeepSWE + 11.7%), whole-program construction (ProgramBench +17%), and terminal work (Terminal-Bench v2.1 +8.5%). Ablations show that increasing the number of high-quality training tasks improves performance. Trajectory analysis shows the RL-trained agent demonstrates better behaviors like increasing codebase exploration and more diverse self-verification. These results establish source code as a scalable foundation for constructing RL environments that improve coding agents across diverse software tasks.

</details>


### [59] [Designer-RSI: Evolving Procedural Memory from User Traffic for Agentic Graphic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22086)
*Hongyang Du,Lan Yan,Christian Flores,Asim Kadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种持续自适应框架：冻结的前沿模型通过230余个工具操作专业设计软件，外部自然语言技能记忆（程序性记忆）从经验中积累并精炼可复用设计流程；通过"扩展"（新增技能）与"深化"（修订已有技能）两种机制配合匹配重放门控，在无权重更新、无人工标注条件下，将技能库从76项增至139项，GenEval2执行成功率由72.7%提升至99.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业平面设计是长周期智能体任务，其结构化、可编辑产物源于大量相互依赖的动作，但结果缺乏可靠的程序化判定标准（no reliable programmatic oracle），难以用传统奖励信号进行优化。因此需要一种在噪声、不可验证反馈下实现持续自适应的途径。

Method: 框架包含：1）冻结前沿模型，通过230余个工具操作专业设计软件；2）外部程序性记忆，以自然语言形式存储技能；3）扩展机制：为反复出现但未被覆盖的子任务获取新流程；4）深化机制：依据已有技能自身的成功与失败执行记录进行修订；5）匹配重放门控（matched replay gate），仅接纳能修复失败且不使已观察成功发生回退的变更。

Result: 五轮实验，基于1,406份真实用户需求与1,869条自动评分轨迹，在无权重更新、无人工标签条件下，技能库由76项文档派生技能增至139项；Claude-Sonnet-4上GenEval2执行成功率由72.7%升至99.3%，生成质量提升11.99分；在四个专业设计基准上对无技能智能体的胜率分别为61.8%（Claude-Sonnet-4）与67.6%（Claude-Opus-4.6）。消融实验显示：在用户流量基准的200份留出需求上，单独扩展或深化分别达到49.4%／48.6%胜率，二者结合达58.5%（p = 0.025）。

Conclusion: 程序性记忆为智能体在噪声大、不可验证反馈下的持续自适应提供了一条切实可行的路径，且扩展与深化两种机制在组合使用时效果显著优于任一单独机制。

Abstract: Professional graphic design is a long-horizon agentic task in which structured, editable artifacts emerge from many interdependent actions, yet outcomes admit no reliable programmatic oracle. We introduce a continual adaptation framework in which a frozen frontier model operates professional design software through more than 230 tools, while an external procedural memory of natural-language skills accumulates and refines reusable design procedures from experience. The memory widens by acquiring procedures for recurring uncovered subtasks and deepens by revising existing procedures against their own successful and failed executions, while a matched replay gate admits only changes that repair failures without regressing observed successes. Five rounds over 1,406 real user briefs and 1,869 automatically graded trajectories, with no weight updates and no human labels, grow the bank from 76 documentation-derived skills to 139 and raise GenEval2 execution success on Claude-Sonnet-4 from 72.7% to 99.3% (+11.99 points in generation quality), with 61.8% and 67.6% win rates against the no-skill agent across four specialized design benchmarks on Claude-Sonnet-4 and Claude-Opus-4.6. We further show the two mechanisms are effective in combination: on 200 held-out briefs from user-traffic benchmark, widening or deepening alone reaches a 49.4% / 48.6% win rate over the no-skill agent, while their combination reaches 58.5% (p = 0.025). Procedural memory offers a practical route to continual adaptation of agents under noisy, unverifiable feedback.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [60] [Verify, Don't Trust: Agentic Model Development for Video Discovery Retrieval at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21257)
*Hao Fu,Baiting Zhu,Minglei Chen,Yinjie Huang,Shuai Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EvoPilot——一种人工把关的长周期在线自动研究方法，通过角色化智能体、版本化领域技能与类型化适配器执行实验轮次，配合持久化记录与确定性校验，在37天检索系统研究活动中定位并修复评估缺陷，取得可复现的离线与在线收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短周期自动研究（分钟级、自包含程序）难以迁移到真实在线场景：在线研究跨越异步系统、小时级变体与数周活动，并可能影响产品。一个已完成的运行仍可能因代码变更无效（no-op）、数据窗口泄漏、评估器语义漂移或实验组与对照组流经不同服务漏斗而得出无效结论。作者希望建立能保证长周期在线研究结论有效性的方法。

Method: EvoPilot采用人工把关（human-gated）的长周期在线自动研究流程：每轮由角色分工的智能体执行，依托版本化领域技能（domain skill）与类型化适配器（typed adapter）；通过持久化记录保留实验与失败信息；用确定性检查（deterministic checks）强制执行已记录的经验教训。研究以驱动Video Deep Dive（VDD）的检索系统为对象，开展37天活动，覆盖七个方向，使用每小时刷新的数亿级视频索引。验证手段包括人工把关的核验、后验重放（replay）与变异测试（mutation tests）。

Result: - 早期人工实验未证实交互头（interaction head）带来收益；一次原始自动研究尝试重访该方向，却错误地将离线命中率下降22个百分点归因于交互头。EvoPilot的人工把关验证将该下降追溯为既有评估缺陷（输出深度为3,000与600）；修复后，匹配比较测得的离线提升为3.20个百分点。
- 后验重放与变异测试能够拒绝无效比较、同时接受有效对照；持久化状态恢复了一次被中断的轮次；产物复用节省约5 GPU小时。
- 另有一项为期七天的随机在线评估，估计VDD切片上Good Search Result Rate for Retention（GSRR）相对提升0.66%。

Conclusion: EvoPilot表明，借助人工把关、版本化技能与适配器、持久化状态和确定性校验，长周期在线自动研究可以稳定地排除无效结论（无效变更、数据泄漏、评估漂移、漏斗不一致），在真实产品检索系统上获得可复现的离线与在线收益，同时提升实验资源利用效率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can propose, implement, and evaluate model changes. Autoresearch loops demonstrate this capability through minutes-scale iterations on a self-contained program. Online autoresearch instead spans asynchronous systems, hours-long variants, and weeks-long campaigns that can influence a product. A completed run can still support an invalid conclusion when a code change is a no-op, data windows leak, evaluator semantics drift, or the two arms traverse different serving funnels. We present EvoPilot, a human-gated method for long-horizon online autoresearch. Role-specific agents execute each round through a versioned domain skill and typed adapter. Durable records preserve experiments and failures; deterministic checks enforce recorded lessons.
  We study a 37-day campaign for the retrieval system that powers Video Deep Dive (VDD), an online experience for discovering follow-on videos after a user opens a seed video. The campaign covered seven directions and used an hourly refreshed index of hundreds of millions of videos. Earlier manual experiments had not established a benefit from an interaction head. A primitive autoresearch attempt revisited the direction but incorrectly attributed an offline hit-rate decline of 22 percentage points to the head. We then introduced EvoPilot. Its human-gated verification traced the drop to a pre-existing evaluation defect that produced output depths of 3,000 and 600. After repair, a matched comparison measured an offline improvement of 3.20 percentage points. Post-study replay and mutation tests rejected invalid comparisons while admitting valid counterparts. Durable state recovered an interrupted round, and artifact reuse avoided approximately five GPU-hours. Separately, a seven-day randomized online evaluation estimated a 0.66% relative increase in the VDD slice of Good Search Result Rate for Retention (GSRR).

</details>


### [61] [Hybrid GPU-CPU Retrieval for Personalized Search at Ultra-Large Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21281)
*Hao Fu,Jichao Sun,Baiting Zhu,Qiaoling Liu,Yan Shi,Cheng Lu,Liu Liu,Yubo Wang,Xin Yao,Xiangyu Niu,Xu Dong,Wenhan Lyu,Chiyao Shen,Yinjie Huang,Minglei Chen,Shuai Ding,Li Fan,Xiao Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种混合GPU-CPU协同服务系统，通过编排而非新模型类别解决超大规模个性化检索中"深度与规模"的矛盾：GPU路径负责十亿级文档池的深度融合与交互预排序，CPU路径负责约二十倍规模的轻量个性化检索，两路可独立或同时运行并去重后进入下游排序。生产A/B测试验证了相关性、参与度提升及经济性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在万亿级用户生成内容的嵌入检索场景中，存在"个性化-规模悖论"：将全部服务库存放入GPU显存资源开销过大，而CPU算力又无法在延迟敏感路径上执行同样交互密集的模型。因此需要一种能同时满足深度个性化与广覆盖、并在固定延迟和资源预算下运行的系统。

Method: 采用混合GPU-CPU协同服务架构：一条高深度GPU路径在约十亿文档规模的精选在线池上融合检索与交互预排序；一条高广度CPU路径以轻量个性化打分检索约二十倍大的独立在线库存。每条请求可运行其中一条或两条路径，候选结果去重后进入共享的下游排序。

Result: 系统已部署上线。全系统A/B测试相较传统纯CPU配置提升了模型评分相关性与实质性参与度；分路径实验显示各路径在其部署范围内均具正向价值。检索日志表明两路径贡献结构上不同的候选，服务测量刻画了各路径延迟，匹配容量规划量化了"深度给GPU、广度给CPU"的经济合理性。

Conclusion: 结果验证了一种实用、可独立演进的深度-广度架构，适用于超大规模个性化搜索，其核心思想是通过编排协调异构算力资源，而非依赖新模型类别。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval on user-generated content at the trillion-document scale exposes a sharp conflict between two production demands: deep, expressive personalization for queries with rich user intent, and broad coverage of a massive inventory under fixed latency and resource budgets. We characterize this as the personalization-scale paradox: hosting the full serving inventory in GPU memory is too resource intensive, while CPU compute cannot execute the same interaction-heavy model on the latency-critical path.
  We present a hybrid GPU-CPU co-serving system that resolves the paradox through orchestration rather than a new model class. A high-depth GPU pathway fuses retrieval and interaction pre-ranking over a curated online pool on the order of a billion documents, while a high-breadth CPU pathway searches an independently selected online inventory roughly twenty times larger with lightweight personalized scoring. Either or both pathways can run per request; candidates are deduplicated before shared downstream ranking.
  The system is deployed in production. A full-system A/B test against the legacy CPU-only configuration improves model-scored relevance and substantive engagement, while separate pathway experiments show positive value at their own deployment scopes. Retrieval logs show that the pathways contribute structurally distinct candidates, production serving measurements characterize their latency, and a matched capacity plan quantifies the economic rationale for assigning modeling depth to GPUs and inventory breadth to CPUs. Together, these results validate a practical, independently evolvable depth-breadth architecture for ultra-large-scale personalized search.

</details>


### [62] [Auto-Bidding with Disentangled Advertiser Profiles and Train-Free Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21308)
*Songyue Cai,Shan Gu,Wei Chen,Ziru Xu,Lianyu Wang,Jian Xu,Xiaofeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ADAPT自动竞价框架，通过解耦广告主画像与免训练自适应实现个性化竞价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于画像的方法在推荐等领域有效，但用于自动竞价受限：纯画像提取困难、公私信息同时建模难、画像更新与冷启动自适应困难。

Method: 两阶段训练+免训练自适应：(1) 基于广告主记忆库对比学习提取纯静态与动态画像；(2) 将动态画像解耦为公共画像与私有画像，与静态画像联合条件化竞价策略；(3) 训练后无需重训即可为新广告主构建画像、为已有广告主更新画像。

Result: 大规模自动竞价基准实验表明ADAPT持续取得优越性能，消融研究验证各模块有效性。

Conclusion: ADAPT有效解决了画像提取、公私信息解耦和免训练自适应的挑战，是可行的个性化自动竞价方案；源码将开源。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is a key component of modern advertising systems that provides a personalized bidding strategy for each advertiser. By characterizing each individual, profile-based methods achieve personalization and have proven effective in domains such as recommendation. However, despite the diverse bidding behavior of advertisers, their application to auto-bidding remains limited. A primary reason is that constructing and leveraging advertiser profiles face several challenges: extracting pure profiles is non-trivial, modeling common and private information simultaneously is difficult, and profile updating and cold-start adaptation remain challenging. To tackle these issues, we propose \textbf{ADAPT}, an \underline{\textbf{A}}uto-bidding framework with \underline{\textbf{D}}isentangled \underline{\textbf{A}}dvertiser \underline{\textbf{P}}rofiles and \underline{\textbf{T}}raining-free adaptation. ADAPT introduces a two-stage training paradigm and supports training-free adaptation. Specifically, (i) the stage 1 extracts pure static and dynamic profiles via contrastive learning over the advertiser memory bank; (ii) the stage 2 disentangles the dynamic profile into a common profile and a private profile, and combines them with the static profile to jointly condition the bidding strategy; (iii) once trained, ADAPT constructs profiles for new advertisers and updates profiles of existing advertisers without retraining. Our experiments on a large-scale auto-bidding benchmark demonstrate that ADAPT consistently achieves superior performance, and ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each module. The source code will be released at https://github.com/YuzunoKawori/ADAPT.

</details>


### [63] [Adaptive Preference Modeling via Explicit Indirect Relational Learning for Personalized Fashion Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21475)
*Shuiying Liao,Li Li,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应偏好对比学习框架APCL，通过显式建模直接与间接关系信号，并结合多模态信息与功能视图对比学习，提升稀疏数据下个性化时尚互补推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化时尚互补推荐需在稀疏且多模态的数据条件下联合建模用户偏好与物品兼容性。现有方法多通过图传播隐式捕获高阶关系信号，或依赖直接交互数据，难以显式建模间接偏好与兼容性关系。

Method: APCL在统一推荐架构中显式建模直接与间接关系信号：通过相关性引导的自适应聚合机制构建间接的用户-物品与物品-物品关系，并将其表示为个性化视图与兼容性视图；引入功能视图对比学习策略，对齐直接/间接偏好表示及直接/间接兼容性表示，以增强跨关系上下文的一致性；同时融合多模态视觉与文本信息。

Result: 在两个基准时尚推荐数据集上，APCL持续优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式间接关系建模结合多模态信息与对比学习，可捕获更丰富的语义特征，并提升稀疏交互场景下的鲁棒性与推荐效果。

Abstract: Personalized fashion complementary recommendation requires jointly modeling user preferences and item compatibility under sparse and multimodal data conditions. Existing approaches often capture higher-order relational signals implicitly through graph propagation or rely on direct interaction data, limiting their ability to explicitly model indirect preference and compatibility relationships. To address this limitation, we propose an Adaptive Preference with Contrastive Learning framework (APCL) that explicitly models both direct and indirect relational signals within a unified recommendation architecture. Specifically, APCL constructs indirect user-item and item-item relationships through a correlation-guided adaptive aggregation mechanism and represents them as dedicated personalization and compatibility views. To improve representation learning, we further introduce a functional view contrastive learning strategy that aligns direct and indirect preference representations and direct and indirect compatibility representations, encouraging consistency across relational contexts. By integrating multimodal visual and textual information with explicit indirect relational modeling, APCL captures richer semantic characteristics while improving robustness in sparse-interaction settings. Experiments on two benchmark fashion recommendation datasets demonstrate that APCL consistently outperforms representative baseline methods.

</details>


### [64] [Predictable Failure in Multi-Hop Retrieval: Score-Distributional Confidence Scoring and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22056)
*Andre Bacellar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了多跳检索失败在结构上可预测的子群体中聚集，提出CWAR可归约性与特征机制互补性两大理论结果，并据此设计RegimeAbstain（基于检索置信分数RCS的校准弃答策略），在三个多跳基准、两种检索架构、五种失败机制下取得最佳或并列最佳的AUC-AC，显著降低置信错误答案率（CWAR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索失败并非在查询间均匀分布，而是聚集于结构上可预测的子群体。现有置信度与弃答方法未能刻画这种结构性，且LLM-judge与dense-only两种检索机制之间存在明显的AUC-AC差距，缺乏统一解释与通用解决方案。

Method: 首先证明两个理论结果：(1) CWAR可归约性——当且仅当检索特征携带关于成功与否的互信息时，置信失败缩减可实现，该条件在LLM-judge流水线中满足而在纯稠密检索中显著更弱，从而解释两机制的AUC-AC差距；(2) 特征机制互补性——没有单一ANN分数特征能在所有失败机制下取得最优预测性能，主导特征因数据集而异（MuSiQue为查询长度，HoVer为hop-1集中度），并构造见证对证明各自在一种机制中必要、在另一种中无贡献。据此实例化RegimeAbstain，计算检索置信分数RCS——由至多九个查询-ANN结构特征构成的逻辑函数，无需额外LLM调用，并用于实现校准弃答策略。定义CWAR指标，在三个多跳基准与两种检索架构、五种失败机制上进行评估，并与八个置信基线对比。

Result: 覆盖五种失败机制、CWAR范围14.5%至62.1%。RCS在全部五种条件下取得最佳或并列最佳的AUC-AC。在MuSiQue（LLM-judge）上，RCS将CWAR从39.5%降至20.6%（50%覆盖率下相对降低47.8%），ECE=0.035。仅在MuSiQue上训练的模型迁移至2WikiMultiHopQA时AUC仅损失-0.5pp，证实机制特征的领域无关结构。

Conclusion: 多跳检索失败具有可形式化的结构性：CWAR可归约性刻画了实现置信失败缩减的充要条件，特征机制互补性表明不同失败机制需不同主导特征。RegimeAbstain以无额外LLM开销的结构化RCS实现校准弃答，在多基准、多架构下持续优于现有置信基线，并具备良好的跨域迁移能力。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval failures are not uniformly distributed across queries: they cluster in structurally predictable subpopulations. We prove two results formalizing this structure. First (CWAR Reducibility): confident-failure reduction is achievable if and only if retrieval features carry mutual information about success, a condition satisfied by LLM-judge pipelines but substantially weaker in dense-only settings, explaining the AUC-AC gap between regimes. Second (Feature Regime Complementarity): no single ANN score feature achieves best predictive performance across all failure regimes; the dominant feature differs between datasets (query length on MuSiQue, hop-1 concentration on HoVer), and a constructive witness pair shows each is necessary in one regime and non-contributory in the other. We instantiate these principles in RegimeAbstain, which computes a Retrieval Confidence Score (RCS), a logistic function of up to nine query-ANN structural features, all available without any additional LLM call, and uses it to implement a calibrated abstention policy. We define the Confident-Wrong-Answer Rate (CWAR) metric and evaluate across three multi-hop benchmarks (MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, HoVer) and two retrieval architectures (LLM-judge and dense-only), covering five failure regimes with CWAR from 14.5% to 62.1%. RCS achieves best or co-best AUC-AC in all five conditions against eight confidence baselines. On MuSiQue (LLM-judge), RCS reduces CWAR from 39.5% to 20.6% at 50% coverage (47.8% relative reduction), with ECE=0.035. A model trained on MuSiQue transfers to 2WikiMultiHopQA with only -0.5pp AUC loss, confirming the domain-agnostic structure of regime features.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [65] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Stiefel-AdamW：将线性分解块 W=BA 中一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形上，把 GL(r) 规范对称性降为紧凑正交对称性，从而在保留 AdamW 逐坐标对角预条件的同时消除因子爆炸，且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性分解块（如 LoRA、低秩压缩层、自注意力 QK 乘积）中 W=BA 的分解不唯一，导致训练不稳定、可用学习率受限；但现有方法仍用忽略内在几何的欧氏优化器（如 AdamW）来训练这些块。

Method: Stiefel-AdamW 作为 AdamW 的近直接替代：将一个因子约束在 Stiefel 流形、另一个保持欧氏；矩估计在环境欧氏空间进行，几何仅通过切空间投影与流形回缩引入，从而保留 AdamW 的逐坐标对角预条件。

Result: 在 GPT2、ViT、Mistral 7B 的 LoRA 式微调，以及 GPT2 在 OpenWebText 上的完整预训练中，相较强基线取得一致提升，且相对 AdamW 几乎无额外计算成本。

Conclusion: Stiefel-AdamW 同时具备黎曼方法的稳定性优势与标准收敛保证，实现对 AdamW 的近乎零成本替换，适用于所有出现线性分解块的场景。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees. We validate Stiefel-AdamW on LoRA-style fine-tuning of GPT2, ViT, and Mistral 7B and on full pretraining of GPT2 on OpenWebText, showing consistent improvements over strong baselines at essentially no additional cost over AdamW.

</details>


### [66] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏先验类并定义“稀疏维度”，证明在该先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为 Ω(√(k/n))，且对 TV 距离存在匹配上界，从而在依赖 n 的维度上克服维数灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式 AI 中，从 n 个样本学习 d 维分布的理论保证退化速度为 O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})，虽被证明是极小极大最优，但作者认为这些界过于悲观：仅靠光滑性假设不足以刻画实际应用中分布的结构。

Method: 引入稀疏先验类，将“稀疏维度”（Sparse Dimension）定义为所有分布空间上先验稀疏性的度量；在 k-稀疏先验下分析分布学习的贝叶斯风险，并在贝叶斯框架下证明分布学习与学习采样（learning to sample）的统计等价性。

Result: 在常见距离度量下，k-稀疏先验的分布学习达到贝叶斯风险下界 Ω(√(k/n))；在温和附加假设下，TV 距离上存在匹配上界（渐近于 n、k 忽略对数项）。该结果同样适用于学习采样。k 仍可依赖于维度 d 或内在维度。

Conclusion: 在适当的先验下学习可以克服关于 n 依赖的维数灾难，说明现有基于光滑性假设的界确实过于悲观。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [67] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个端到端BI评测基准BI-Bench，发现前沿LLM准确率不足50%；设计工具增强的BI-Agent并配合SFT+RL后训练，相比原LLM提升最高40个百分点，后训练版本再提升最高30个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BI流程需用户依次完成识别相关表、数据变换、构建连接关系等繁琐的准备工作，才能回答业务问题，这些步骤复杂且耗时，使BI应用门槛高。鉴于LLM在数据处理上展现出的强大能力，作者希望研究LLM能否端到端地直接回答BI问题，从而免去人工准备环节。

Method: 1）从公开来源采集大量真实BI项目，并从真实用户仪表盘中人工抽取（问题，真值答案）对，构建首个系统评估LLM端到端BI能力的基准BI-Bench；2）设计工具增强的BI-Agent，将BI工作流分解为搜索、连接、变换等结构化数据子任务，并编排各BI阶段的专用数据管理方法；3）开发后训练框架，从真实BI项目合成训练轨迹，对BI-Agent进行监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL）后训练。

Result: 即使前沿LLM在BI-Bench上表现也很差，准确率不足50%。工具增强的BI-Agent相比原始LLM取得最高40个百分点的准确率提升；经过后训练的BI-Agent再获得最高30个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在复杂BI工作流中结合工具增强推理与领域特定后训练至关重要，并为未来研究指明了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [68] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出端到端可训练的弹性阈值注意力（ETA），通过从查询表示预测动态上下文阈值实现稀疏解码，在保持稠密模型质量的同时获得最高2.5倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，大规模KV缓存导致严重的内存带宽瓶颈；现有稀疏注意力依赖刚性启发式规则进行选择性加载，会丢弃必要上下文，带来质量退化。

Method: ETA从查询表示直接预测动态上下文阈值，对困难检索或推理步骤分配近似稠密上下文，对常规token进行剪枝。训练时不直接删除子阈值logits，而是乘性抑制其趋近于零，形成平滑的均匀注意力概率库，从而消除初始token上的局部注意力汇聚（attention sink）。推理时对无信息KV块进行硬剪枝，并吸收粗粒度GPU块选择误纳入的token。另在Triton中实现自定义解码kernel，利用缓存的几何-概率界以O(1)时间筛选KV块；并引入离线校准算法冻结每头常量阈值以消除预测器开销。

Result: 1.45B预训练ETA模型在约85%训练稀疏度、约38%活跃解码密度下，于语言建模、常识推理和长上下文needle检索任务上媲美稠密注意力。在最长512K token序列上，相比FlashAttention-2实现最高2.5倍端到端解码加速。离线校准可额外减少27%注意力计算量。

Conclusion: ETA证明了通过可学习的动态阈值策略，可在硬件层面实现稀疏加速而不牺牲稠密模型质量，为长上下文部署提供了兼顾效率与质量的可训练稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [69] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Bio-MF——一种无潜变量、单步的MeanFlow框架，用于由EEG条件生成fNIRS信号，可在7.0 ms内完成一次生成并在混合MI-BCI解码中取得显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合MI-BCI（EEG+fNIRS）性能优于纯EEG系统，但配对采集EEG-fNIRS不便或不可得，因此需要EEG到fNIRS的跨模态生成。现有方法生成慢且常需预训练，难以满足实时MI-BCI需求；而单步生成模型虽低延迟，却因去除迭代精修导致保真度下降并引入非生理伪影。

Method: 提出Bio-MF：无潜变量的单步MeanFlow框架，直接在信号空间进行x-prediction，并将信号空间输出转换为MeanFlow速度监督，单次网络评估即可完成推理。为在异构传感器布局下保留任务相关血流动力学结构，集成时空交互4D编码、跨模态无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）以及噪声水平门控FFT正则化。

Result: 数据集1上，EEG+合成fNIRS相较纯EEG使ACC分别提升3.37（HbR）和4.15（HbO）个百分点；数据集2在未见过的64通道EEG蒙太奇下增益为2.98和2.50个百分点。在RTX PRO 6000 GPU上，生成单次fNIRS试次耗时7.0 ms，相较1000步SCDM实现857倍加速。

Conclusion: Bio-MF能够在保持任务相关生成质量的同时实现快速EEG到fNIRS合成，有效支撑下游混合MI解码，适用于实时MI-BCI场景。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage.
