However, existing DFL approaches to MVO rely on surrogate losses or constraint relaxations for tractability, creating a structural mismatch between predictive model training and the constrained MVO solved at evaluation. We propose a single-level optimization formulation that incorporates the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality conditions of the lower-level MVO into the upper-level learning problem. This formulation explicitly preserves the budget and short-sale constraints while remaining tractable for standard nonlinear optimization solvers. Rolling-window experiments on real-world ETF (Exchange Traded Funds) data across two asset universes with different correlation structures show that our method achieved the best performance on multiple investment metrics and also demonstrated performance improvement due to the proposed regularization.

</details>


### [106] [Optimal Randomized Proper Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21445)
*Zachary Chase,Idan Mehalel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了随机proper在线学习算法的最优期望错误界为 O(L(H) log T)，其中 L(H) 为函数类的 Littlestone 维度，T 为时间步长。该结果改进了 Daskalakis 与 Golowich (STOC 2022) 的 O(L(H) log^6 T)，并在最坏情况下达到最优（至多相差常数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线学习理论中长期关注的核心问题是：随机 proper 学习算法的错误界能否达到仅含单个对数因子的最优形式。此前最优结果为 O(L(H) log^6 T)，对数多项式次幂的存在促使作者探究是否存在更紧的、接近信息论下界的界。

Method: 论文针对随机 proper 在线学习算法，以 Littlestone 维度 L(H) 作为函数类复杂度度量，构造并分析新的算法与证明框架，从而将期望错误界从 O(L(H) log^6 T) 改进至 O(L(H) log T)。

Result: 证明了随机 proper 学习算法的最优期望错误界为 O(L(H) log T)，严格优于此前的 O(L(H) log^6 T)；对于最坏情况下的函数类，该界在通用常数意义下是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作基本解决了随机 proper 在线学习最优错误界的问题，确立了 O(L(H) log T) 这一（至常数因子）紧的界，推进了对 Littlestone 维度与在线学习样本复杂度关系的理解。

Abstract: We prove that the optimal expected mistake bound of online learning a function class $\mathcal{H}$ by a randomized proper learning algorithm is $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log T)$, where $\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H})$ is the Littlestone dimension of $\mathcal{H}$ and $T$ is the time horizon. Our result improves upon the previously best known bound of $O(\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{H}) \log^6 T)$ given by Daskalakis and Golowich (STOC 2022), and is optimal up to a universal constant for worst-case classes.

</details>


### [107] [Understanding LLM Quantization through Activation-Guided Compensation and Orthogonal Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21450)
*Yamato Narita,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将局部权重-激活量化误差精确分解为激活引导的权重补偿项与正交残差，给出残差界，并据此推导旋转、符号选择与通道缩放的设计准则，无需反向传播即达到与SpinQuant相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W4A4训练后量化仍很困难，原因是激活离群值降低有效量化分辨率；现有权重优化、通道缩放与正交旋转虽能缓解，但各自作用的误差成分及其相互关系不清晰，缺乏统一分析。

Method: 将局部权重-激活量化误差精确分解为激活引导的权重补偿项与正交残差，利用持续通道离群量与常规激活量对残差建立上界；基于该残差界推导随机Hadamard旋转、符号选择与通道缩放的实用准则（随机符号抑制持续离群通道间相长干涉、采样多样符号模式改进变换选择、二阶矩平衡导出L2缩放规则并松弛得到SmoothQuant式L∞缩放）。

Result: 在八个Llama与Mistral模型上，采用无反向传播配置验证准则，性能与经梯度训练的SpinQuant具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该误差分解明确了哪些误差成分可由权重补偿解决、哪些需依赖变换设计，为W4A4量化中的旋转、符号与缩放选择提供了理论依据与实用指导。

Abstract: Post-training weight-activation quantization reduces the memory and inference costs of large language models, but aggressive W4A4 quantization remains difficult because activation outliers degrade effective quantization resolution. Although weight optimization, channel-wise scaling, and orthogonal rotation mitigate this problem, the error components they address and their relationship remain unclear. Using an exact decomposition of local weight-activation quantization error into an activation-guided weight compensation term and an orthogonal residual, we bound the residual using persistent channel-wise outlier and regular activation quantities. This decomposition clarifies which error components can be addressed by weight compensation and which require transformation design. We then use the residual bounds to derive practical guidelines for applying randomized Hadamard rotation, sign selection, and channel scaling. In particular, the analysis explains how random signs suppress constructive interference among persistent outlier channels, how sampling multiple sign patterns can improve transformation selection, and how second-moment balancing leads to an $L_2$ scaling rule while a further relaxation recovers SmoothQuant-style $L_\infty$ scaling. We evaluate these guidelines through backpropagation-free configurations across eight Llama and Mistral models, obtaining performance competitive with gradient-trained SpinQuant.

</details>


### [108] [Efficient Architecture Search under Leave-One-Subject-Out Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21457)
*Heinke Hihn,Friedhelm Schwenker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PainNAS：一种基于块、控制泄漏的NAS方法，将LOSO评估中的搜索次数从N降至B（B≪N），在BioVid数据集上以更少参数与FLOPs取得相当的被试级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络广泛用于自动疼痛评估，但架构设计仍主要依赖人工；将NAS嵌入LOSO评估时，完全嵌套实现需N次独立搜索，按近线性训练成本计算复杂度达O(N²)，计算代价过高。

Method: 提出PainNAS：采用块（block）结构设计，并引入泄漏控制机制，使多个被试共享同一次NAS搜索运行，从而把搜索次数由N减少为B（B≪N）。

Result: 在BioVid Heat Pain数据集上，PainNAS取得与被试级基线相当的准确率，同时参数量和FLOPs显著降低。

Conclusion: 块级搜索共享结合泄漏控制可在不损失被试级性能的前提下大幅降低NAS在LOSO评估中的计算开销，为自动疼痛评估提供高效架构方案。

Abstract: Deep neural architectures are widely used for signal processing in automated pain assessment systems. However, architecture design has remained largely a manual task despite the potential efficiency benefits of Neural Architecture Search (NAS). Embedding NAS in a Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) evaluation is computationally demanding because a fully nested implementation requires $N$ independent architecture searches and, assuming approximately linear training cost, scales as $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$. We propose a block-based, leakage-controlled approach that shares NAS runs between subjects, reducing the number of searches from $N$ to $B$, where $B \ll N$, dubbed PainNAS. On the BioVid Heat Pain dataset, PainNAS yields comparable subject-level accuracy with substantially fewer parameters and FLOPs.

</details>


### [109] [What Must Survive? Exact Task-Information--State Frontiers for Resource-Sufficient Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21523)
*Ronald Katende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在下游任务未知时压缩系统，量化有限任务先验信息（advice）能节省多少状态，给出精确前沿 p*(K)、近似奇异值前沿、公共核下界与direct-sum律，并证明最优advice划分强NP难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统常在下游任务完全确定前就被压缩，而实际中可能提前获得有限的任务提示信息。作者希望回答：此时至少需要保留多少状态，以及有限的提前任务信息最多能节省多少状态。

Method: 对有限线性任务族，先揭示任务消息、后揭示确切任务。对advice字母表大小K，以U的划分P为决策对象，最坏情况下联合任务算子的秩作为代价，定义精确前沿p*(K)=min_{|P|≤K} max_{C∈P} rank(T_C)，b比特情形取K=min(2^b,|U|)；并推导近似奇异值前沿、公共核下界、direct-sum律，以及最优advice划分的NP难性。

Result: advance task信息通过低秩联合任务算子划分降低所需状态；最优划分问题强NP难且在任意固定正近似容差下仍难。例：良态softmax attention在九比特区分512种延续之一时，给出精确524,288→1,024坐标前沿；域分解数字孪生得到界面加局部状态律与异质区域加权划分问题；分层多任务模型三比特将状态从3136降至448坐标，进一步信息逼近不可约328坐标单任务下界。

Conclusion: 提前任务信息的最优利用等价于寻找使最大联合任务算子秩最小的划分，信息越多所需保留状态越少并趋向单任务下界；但寻找最优advice划分在计算上不可行（强NP难，且近似亦难），因此实际中需依赖近似或结构化的划分策略。

Abstract: A system may be compressed before its downstream task is fully known. We ask how much retained state is then necessary and how much can be saved by limited advance task information.
  For a finite family of linear tasks, a task message is revealed before state formation and the exact task only afterwards. For an advice alphabet of size $K$, the exact frontier is \[ p^*(K)= \min_{\substack{\Pcal\text{ partition of }\U\\|\Pcal|\le K}} \max_{C\in\Pcal}\rank(T_C), \] with the $b$-bit frontier obtained by setting $K=\min(2^b,|\U|)$. Thus advance task information reduces state through partitions whose joint task operators have low rank.
  We also give an approximate singular-value frontier, a common-core lower bound and exact direct-sum law, and strong NP-hardness of finding an optimal advice partition. The hardness persists at every fixed positive approximation tolerance.
  Three examples illustrate the result. A well-conditioned softmax attention construction gives an exact $524{,}288\to1{,}024$ coordinate frontier when nine bits resolve one of $512$ continuations. A domain-decomposed digital twin yields an interface-plus-local-state law and a weighted partition problem for heterogeneous regions.
  A hierarchical multi-task model gives a two-stage frontier in which three bits reduce the required state from $3136$ to $448$ coordinates, with further task information approaching the irreducible $328$-coordinate single-task floor.

</details>


### [110] [IncentRL: The Trade-Off Between Preference Guidance and Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21525)
*Xuening Wu,Yanlan Kang,Shenqin Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 IncentRL 框架，通过 KL 惩罚引入偏好引导，并刻画其对外部任务性能的影响，在理论保证与初步实验之间建立联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好信号加入奖励可能无意改变被优化任务，需在提升学习的同时明确并限制对外部任务目标的扭曲。

Method: 在结果分布与偏好分布间加入 KL 惩罚；针对有界塑形成本的有限折扣 MDP，推导外部价值扰动界、保持原最优策略的严格动作间隔充分条件，并刻画大权重区域；实现上采用手工距离型结果代理、固定偏好分布与分数加权系数搜索。

Result: MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8 上，两百万步训练后三次种子平均成功率在系数 0.01 时为 98%，零系数基线为 90.5%，且搜索逐渐偏向更小系数。

Conclusion: 给出了偏好型 RL 核心权衡的原则性视角，但当前实验仍为描述性，尚未将 KL 塑形与更简单替代方案分离。

Abstract: Preference-based reward shaping can guide reinforcement learning, but adding preference signals to the reward may unintentionally change the task being optimized. We address this problem with IncentRL, a framework that introduces preference guidance while explicitly characterizing its effect on external-task performance. IncentRL adds a Kullback--Leibler (KL) penalty between a specified outcome distribution and a preferred distribution. For finite discounted Markov decision processes with bounded shaping costs, we derive an external-value perturbation bound, establish a sufficient strict-action-gap condition for preserving the original optimal policy, and characterize the large-weight regime through discounted cumulative preference cost. Exact examples clarify the limits of these guarantees, including tied optima and support mismatch. We study a practical implementation using a hand-designed, distance-based outcome proxy, a fixed preference distribution, and score-weighted coefficient search. On MiniGrid DoorKey-8x8, the reported three-seed mean success rate after two million training steps reaches 98\% with coefficient 0.01, compared with 90.5\% for the reported zero-coefficient baseline, while the search progressively shifts toward smaller coefficients. Together, these results provide a principled view of the central trade-off in preference-based RL: using additional guidance to improve learning without excessively distorting the original task objective. The current experiments remain descriptive and do not yet isolate KL shaping from simpler alternatives.

</details>


### [111] [OpenMAS-GCom. A Diagnostic Benchmark for Graph-enhanced Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21527)
*Kairui Yang,Xunkai Li,Kaixiang Zhang,Minghao An,Zekai Chen,Yuxuan Ba,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenMAS-GCom基准，通过控制变量干预诊断通信结构、角色分配与信息流如何影响图增强多智能体系统（G-MAS）性能，从而解决评测归因问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有G-MAS评测仅比较最终分数，混合了模型、通信模式、角色与计算成本差异，难以将性能差异归因到具体的通信结构、角色分配和信息流，存在"评测归因问题"。

Method: 用协作单元、通信链路、共享中间信息与执行规则表示系统；对比原始系统与"仅改动单一组件、其余（任务、模型、提示、预算上限）固定"的变体。干预方式包括重连通信边、移除专家或批评者智能体、将中间消息替换为错误内容、执行中禁用worker。覆盖17种单智能体/普通多智能体/图增强配置、6个领域29个数据集，并新增400个G-MAS-Complex任务。

Result: 移除专家智能体的平均损失大于移除批评者；在原始得分相近的情况下，错误消息与worker失效导致不同程度的性能退化；在G-MAS-Complex上，达到最高准确率与最高单位token准确率的配置并不相同。

Conclusion: OpenMAS-GCom通过受控干预实现了对G-MAS各组件的可归因评测，揭示角色类型、信息正确性与执行可靠性对性能的差异化影响，并为准确率与token效率的权衡提供诊断依据。

Abstract: Graph-enhanced multi-agent systems (G-MAS) coordinate large language model agents through communication graphs and role assignments, which determine how agents exchange information and divide responsibilities. However, final-score comparisons across systems combine differences in models, communication patterns, roles, and computation costs, making performance differences difficult to attribute to specific communication structures, role assignments, and information flows. To address this evaluation attribution problem, we introduce OpenMAS-GCom, a benchmark for diagnosing how these components affect G-MAS performance through controlled interventions. We represent systems through collaboration units, communication links, shared intermediate information, and execution rules. OpenMAS-GCom compares original systems with versions modified by changing one component while keeping tasks, models, prompts, and budget limits fixed. We rewire communication edges, remove specialist or critic agents, replace intermediate messages with incorrect content, and disable workers during execution. The benchmark evaluates 17 single-agent, ordinary multi-agent, and graph-enhanced configurations on 29 datasets across six domains. We add 400 G-MAS-Complex tasks requiring agents to combine information from multiple documents, resolve conflicting records, and return specified values with source identifiers. Experiments show larger mean losses after specialist removal than after critic removal, different performance degradation under incorrect messages and worker failures despite similar original scores, and different configurations achieving the highest accuracy and accuracy per token on G-MAS-Complex.

</details>


### [112] [MACE: Memory-Agent Co-Evolution with Adaptive Memory Graphs for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21533)
*Kairui Yang,Minghao An,Xunkai Li,Ziheng Yi,Zekai Chen,Guangyuan He,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MACE，一个记忆-智能体协同演化框架。通过将协作依赖组织为功能记忆单元并建模单元间关系，结合执行反馈自适应调整记忆组织与记忆使用方式（指令/清单格式），在八个基准上以81.11%的平均分超越包括最强基线SAGE（78.97%）在内的十个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统产生大量协作轨迹，记录了智能体如何规划任务、验证中间结果与修复失败。复用这些过程需要保留动作的前提条件以及后续智能体所需的输出。然而现有方法未能有效组织这类依赖关系，且记忆的呈现格式与使用方式缺乏自适应调整，导致依赖保留与检索效果不足。

Method: 作者基于实证发现（将依赖分组为功能记忆单元可提升保留率；连接单元可提升联合检索；最优单元组合随指令/清单格式变化；基于组合与格式配对结果更新选择优于分别打分）提出MACE框架。其MemGoG结构将功能单元表示为包含条件、动作、输出的相关子图，并通过支持、冲突、修复三类关系连接。MACE Loop在记忆预算内选择任务相关单元与关系，为各智能体提供指令或清单，并记录所选单元、呈现格式、智能体输出与任务结果，用于更新单元分数与关系，从而改进检索并指导后续格式选择。

Result: 在八个基准上，MACE平均得分81.11%，优于十个基线，最强基线SAGE为78.97%。

Conclusion: 通过记忆组织与智能体记忆使用的协同演化，MACE有效提升了多智能体协作过程的复用效率与任务表现，验证了功能单元分组、关系连接及格式自适应选择的综合有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems generate collaboration traces that record how agents plan tasks, verify intermediate results, and repair failures. Reusing these procedures requires preserving an action's prerequisites and the outputs needed by subsequent agents. Our empirical studies show that grouping these dependencies into functional memory units improves their retention, while connecting units increases retrieval of the units and links jointly required by a task. The preferred combination of units also changes between instructions and checklists, even when each combination's content is fixed across formats. Updating choices from the outcomes of each combination and format pairing outperforms scoring combinations and formats separately. These findings motivate MACE, a memory-agent co-evolution framework that adapts memory organization and agent memory use through execution feedback. Its MemGoG structure represents functional units as subgraphs of related conditions, actions, and outputs, connecting them through support, conflict, and repair relations. MACE Loop selects task-relevant units and relations within a memory budget and provides each agent with instructions or checklists for its current operation. It records the selected units, presentation formats, agent outputs, and task outcomes to update unit scores and relations for retrieval and inform subsequent presentation choices. Across eight benchmarks, MACE outperforms ten baselines with an average score of 81.11%, compared with 78.97% for the strongest baseline, SAGE.

</details>


### [113] [OneBid: A Unified Auto-Bidding Foundation Model for Diverse oCPX Advertising Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21550)
*Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Yitian Li,Pengfei Lv,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OneBid，一个统一自动出价基础模型，从异构 oCPX 日志中学习可复用主干，并通过离线后训练适配具体场景，在快手线上 A/B 测试中实现整体 +2.2% ADVV 提升，ROAS 场景峰值达 +13.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动出价方法（从规则控制到强化学习、决策 Transformer 等生成式方法）与当前主流的 oCPX 范式日益不匹配：oCPX 覆盖注册、购买等异构场景，每个场景由独立模型服务，导致流程碎片化，跨场景建模未被充分探索。受 LLM 等基础模型启发，将多种 oCPX 场景统一到单一模型面临三大挑战：多目标控制、严格延迟下的可扩展容量，以及安全的离线策略改进。

Method: 基于 Decision Transformer，将单一 Return-to-Go 条件扩展为两个原子信号——用于转化价值的 Return-to-Go 和用于成本比的 Cost-to-Go，并加入对下一动作预测的值感知正则化；设计序列级 Mixture-of-Experts 架构，共享专家编码跨场景知识，稀疏路由专家以低延迟捕获场景特定模式；后训练阶段采用 Critic 引导的相对离线策略优化（CROP），学习到的 critic 对候选动作进行组内相对打分，避免 GRPO 式微调的不安全在线探索，同时约束策略偏移以降低 OOD 风险。

Result: 经线上 A/B 测试验证，并在快手完成全量部署。OneBid 在 oCPX 广告上实现整体 +2.2% 的 ADVV 增益，ROAS 场景峰值达 +13.1%。模型规模与数据呈现一致的扩展性。

Conclusion: OneBid 验证了统一自动出价基础模型的可行性：通过双目标条件化、序列级 MoE 与安全离线策略优化，可在严格延迟下统一异构 oCPX 场景并带来显著线上收益，为跨场景广告出价建模提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Auto-bidding is central to computational advertising, where strategies must maximize advertisers' conversion value under economic constraints. It has evolved from rule-based controllers to reinforcement learning and generative methods such as Decision Transformer (DT). Yet these methods increasingly mismatch the prevailing optimized cost-per-X (oCPX) paradigm, which spans heterogeneous scenarios (e.g., registration, purchase), each served by a separate model, leading to fragmented pipelines and underexploring cross-scenario modeling. Inspired by foundation models like LLMs, unifying these oCPX scenarios into one model raises three challenges: multi-objective control, scalable capacity under strict latency, and safe offline policy improvement. We present OneBid, a unified auto-bidding foundation model that learns a reusable backbone from heterogeneous oCPX logs and adapts it to scenario-specific deployments via offline post-training. Building on DT, OneBid extends single Return-to-Go conditioning to two atomic signals, Return-to-Go for conversion value and Cost-to-Go for cost ratio, plus value-aware regularization on next-action prediction. To absorb distributional heterogeneity, we design a sequence-level Mixture-of-Experts architecture, where shared experts encode cross-scenario knowledge and sparsely-routed experts capture scenario-specific patterns at low latency, yielding consistent scaling with model size and data. During post-training, we align the backbone with scenario preferences via Critic-guided Relative Offline Policy optimization (CROP): a learned critic scores candidate actions group-relatively, avoiding the unsafe online exploration of GRPO-style fine-tuning while constraining policy shift to reduce OOD risk. Validated via online A/B tests and fully deployed at Kuaishou, OneBid delivers an overall +2.2% ADVV gain on oCPX Ads, peaking at +13.1% in the ROAS scenario.

</details>


### [114] [ResNLS: An Improved Model for Stock Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.01020)
*Yuanzhe Jia,Ali Anaissi,Basem Suleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合模型ResNLS，结合ResNet与LSTM，通过建模相邻股价间依赖关系提升预测精度，ResNLS-5优于基线至少20%，并在量化回测中验证其避险与盈利效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股价预测是长期难题，现有研究大多忽视股价之间不同程度依赖关系，缺乏对该依赖性的显式建模，从而限制了预测性能。

Method: ResNLS由ResNet与LSTM组成：ResNet作为特征提取器捕捉相邻股价间的依赖，LSTM在融合这些被视为残差的依赖后分析原始时间序列数据。实验比较不同输入窗口，发现以前5个连续交易日收盘价为输入（ResNLS-5）时效果最佳；并构建量化交易框架进行回测。

Result: ResNLS-5相较其他输入配置性能最优，且相比当前最先进基线至少提升20%；回测显示基于ResNLS-5预测的交易策略能在股价下跌期成功减少亏损，并在上涨期获取收益。

Conclusion: 强调相邻股价依赖性的混合建模思路有效提升股价预测精度，ResNLS-5在预测与交易应用中同时具备风险规避与盈利潜力。

Abstract: Stock prices forecasting has always been a challenging task. Although many research projects try to address the problem, few of them pay attention to the varying degrees of dependencies between stock prices. In this paper, we introduce a hybrid model that improves the prediction of stock prices by emphasizing the dependencies between adjacent stock prices. The proposed model, ResNLS, is mainly composed of two neural architectures, ResNet and LSTM. ResNet serves as a feature extractor to identify dependencies between stock prices, while LSTM analyzes the initial time series data with the combination of dependencies, which are considered as residuals. Our experiment reveals that when the closing price data for the previous 5 consecutive trading days is used as input, the performance of the model (ResNLS-5) is optimal compared to those with other inputs. Furthermore, ResNLS-5 demonstrates at least a 20% improvement over current state-of-the-art baselines. To verify whether ResNLS-5 can help clients effectively avoid risks and earn profits in the stock market, we construct a quantitative trading framework for back testing. The result shows that the trading strategy based on ResNLS-5 predictions can successfully mitigate losses during declining stock prices and generate profits in periods of rising stock prices.

</details>


### [115] [On Repulsive and Attractive Teachers: Separating Correctness from Behavior in Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21561)
*Anton Baumann,Akmal Ashirmatov,Leo Schmidt-Traub,Frederike Lübeck,Jonas Hübotter,Thomas Kleine Buening,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究on-policy自蒸馏中特权信息导致的行为偏移问题，发现吸引式与排斥式自蒸馏各自引发相反的行为变化（如抑制探索或触发潜在思考模式），进而提出对比式自蒸馏（向正确答案教师吸引、从错误答案教师排斥），使两者共享的行为偏移相互抵消，从而在多种模型上提升推理性能且保持响应长度稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy自蒸馏通过特权信息提供密集的token级监督，但特权信息不仅改变教师"知道什么"，还会改变其"行为方式"，将正确性相关的学习信号与无意的行为偏移纠缠在一起，因此需要厘清并分离这两类效应。

Method: 在推理任务上对比两种自蒸馏：吸引式（向特权教师靠近）与排斥式（远离特权教师），观察其行为偏移；进而提出对比式自蒸馏，即向"正确答案条件教师"吸引、从"错误答案条件教师"排斥，并刻意剥离GRPO目标，单独研究纯自蒸馏目标的行为。

Result: 吸引式自蒸馏抑制探索性推理并产生更短、更自信的回复；排斥式自蒸馏增加响应长度、可能触发模型潜在思考模式的意外切换且最终不稳定。对比式自蒸馏中，两个教师共享的行为偏移大部分相互抵消，留下更直接反映正确性的token级信号；在non-thinking、instruct-only和already-thinking三类模型上均提升推理性能并维持稳定的响应长度。

Conclusion: 特权信息在自蒸馏中同时引入正确性信号与行为偏移，两者可被解耦：通过吸引与排斥的组合使共同行为偏移相互抵消，可在不依赖GRPO的情况下以纯自蒸馏目标有效提升推理能力并保持响应长度稳定。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation provides dense, token-level supervision by conditioning a model on privileged information and distilling the resulting teacher distribution back into the model. However, privileged information can change not only what the teacher knows, but also how it behaves, entangling correctness-relevant learning signals with unintended behavioral shifts. We study this effect in reasoning tasks by contrasting attractive self-distillation, which moves the model toward a privileged teacher, with repulsive self-distillation, which moves it away from a privileged teacher. We find that both objectives can induce strong and opposing behavioral shifts: attraction suppresses exploratory reasoning and promotes shorter, more confident responses, whereas repulsion increases response length, can trigger unintended switches into a model's latent thinking mode, and ultimately becomes unstable. Motivated by these observations, we study contrastive self-distillation, which combines attraction toward a correct-solution-conditioned teacher with repulsion from an incorrect-solution-conditioned teacher. In contrast to prior work that combines such distillation signals with a GRPO objective, we isolate the self-distillation objective and study its behavior on its own. We find that the shared behavioral shifts of the two teachers largely cancel, leaving a token-level signal that more directly reflects correctness. Across non-thinking, instruct-only, and already-thinking models, this contrastive objective improves reasoning performance while maintaining stable response lengths.

</details>


### [116] [A Hybrid Computational Intelligence Framework for scRNA-seq Imputation: Integrating scRecover and Random Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16923)
*Ali Anaissi,Deshao Liu,Yuanzhe Jia,Weidong Huang,Widad Alyassine,Junaid Akram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该摘要介绍了SCR-MF，一种结合dropout检测（scRecover）与非参数插补（missForest）的两阶段scRNA-seq插补流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: scRNA-seq可实现细胞分辨率转录组分析，但普遍存在的dropout事件会掩盖生物信号，因此需要可靠的插补方法来恢复表达信息。

Method: 提出模块化两阶段工作流SCR-MF：先使用scRecover进行原则性的dropout检测，再通过missForest执行稳健的非参数插补。

Result: 在公开与模拟数据集上，SCR-MF多数情况下性能与现有插补方法相当或更优，且具稳健性与可解释性，同时保持生物保真度与透明度；运行时间分析显示其在准确性与计算效率间取得有竞争力的平衡。

Conclusion: SCR-MF适用于中等规模单细胞数据集，兼顾插补精度、效率与生物学可解释性。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) enables transcriptomic profiling at cellular resolution but suffers from pervasive dropout events that obscure biological signals. We present SCR-MF, a modular two-stage workflow that combines principled dropout detection using scRecover with robust non-parametric imputation via missForest. Across public and simulated datasets, SCR-MF achieves robust and interpretable performance comparable to or exceeding existing imputation methods in most cases, while preserving biological fidelity and transparency. Runtime analysis demonstrates that SCR-MF provides a competitive balance between accuracy and computational efficiency, making it suitable for mid-scale single-cell datasets.

</details>


### [117] [Trading Depth for Time in Recurrent Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21605)
*Zeyi Huang,Xuehai He,Yong Jae Lee,Yelong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过潜在递归Transformer（LRT）对比“时间递归增加计算”与“增加物理深度”两种方式，发现在解码时每词元执行相同2L个Transformer块的情况下，插入一个latent thought token的L层模型能以约少48%的参数逼近2L层模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过时间递归提升计算深度，每步将上一词元的高层隐状态输入下一步计算。这引出一个自然问题：额外计算应更多用于增加时间步，还是增加物理深度？作者希望在受控条件下比较这两种增加计算的方式。

Method: 提出Latent Recurrent Transformers（LRT），解码时每个词元仅保留一次骨干前向传播。在相邻词元之间插入一个latent thought token，该thought token经过与词元相同的L层、共享骨干参数，为预测下一词元提供额外的隐状态精炼阶段。将这种L层LRT（含thought token）与不含thought token的2L层LRT对比：两者解码时每词元都执行2L个Transformer块，但thought-token模型参数更少。实验基于16层和20层的mixture-of-experts NanoChat骨干。

Result: 在16层与20层MoE NanoChat骨干上，一个thought token使较浅模型分别达到其双倍深度对应模型0.006和0.004 bits/byte以内的水平，分别恢复了67%和81%的性能改进，同时总参数量约减少48%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在循环Transformer中，时间维度上的“思考”（latent递归）是一种参数高效的方式，可替代单纯增加物理深度来获得计算增益。

Abstract: Recurrent Transformers increase computational depth through temporal recurrence, feeding each token's high-level hidden state into the computation of the next. This raises a natural question: is additional computation better spent on more temporal steps or greater physical depth? We investigate this question using Latent Recurrent Transformers (LRTs), which retain one backbone forward pass per vocabulary token during decoding and provide a controlled setting for comparing these two ways of adding computation. Specifically, we insert a latent thought token between consecutive vocabulary tokens. Each thought token passes through the same $L$ layers as a vocabulary token, sharing the backbone parameters and providing an additional stage of hidden-state refinement before predicting the next token. We compare this $L$-layer LRT against a $2L$-layer LRT without thought tokens. Both execute $2L$ Transformer blocks per vocabulary token during decoding, but the thought-token model uses fewer parameters. On 16- and 20-layer mixture-of-experts NanoChat backbones, one thought token brings the shallower model within 0.006 and 0.004 bits per byte of its double-depth counterpart, recovering 67% and 81% of the improvement with approximately 48% fewer total parameters. These results suggest that temporal thinking offers a parameter-efficient alternative to increasing physical depth in recurrent Transformers.

</details>


### [118] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究潜在世界动作模型（WAMs）中"潜在转移实现"这一架构选择，提出基于受控Koopman生成元的Latent Evolution Operator Network（LEON），通过上下文调制的算子传播与加性强迫建模潜在演化，在两类WAM框架中提升闭环性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs在潜在表示空间做预测以避免全外观生成，但潜在转移多由Transformer预测器实现，其归纳结构以token交互为中心而非时间演化。作者认为转移实现是与预测表示、预测-策略耦合相独立的架构选择，值得单独研究。

Method: 提出LEON，在学习到的可观空间中以上下文调制的算子传播加加性强迫建模潜在演化；基于受控Koopman生成元视角，围绕共享演化算子结构组织上下文相关的转移变化，同时保留加性变化的互补路径。

Result: 在受控动力系统上验证了演化特异性归纳偏置及算子传播与强迫的互补作用；在两种不同集成方式的WAM框架中，LEON提升闭环性能与鲁棒性，并在完全替换转移模块时仍有效。

Conclusion: 转移实现是潜在WAMs中具有实质影响的架构选择，LEON所体现的演化特异性归纳偏置可带来性能与鲁棒性收益。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [119] [Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows: Geodesic Symplectic Transport and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21647)
*Vincent Souveton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出黎曼神经哈密顿流（Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows），结合黎曼流形固定动能、学习标量势与显式测地线蛙跳积分器，并给出可解释性分析；在欧氏、双曲、球面空间上实验表明采样质量与计算开销优于黎曼连续归一化流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿归一化流因相空间映射可逆且保体积而具吸引力，但多数神经构造局限于欧氏空间，缺乏在一般黎曼流形上的推广与可解释性刻画。

Method: 在黎曼流形上固定动能项，学习标量势函数，并采用显式测地线蛙跳积分器（geodesic leapfrog integrator）构建哈密顿流。通过势函数诱导的隐式分布（implicit profile）与基分布之间相对分数（relative score）分析位置边缘的初期加速；定义匹配势（matched potential）作为隐式分布即目标的特例；对等距高斯情形给出相空间旋转解释，并用局部调和分析推广到一般目标各模态附近；将学习势与匹配势之差分解为基分布残差记忆与模型偏差，指出当位置基分布转移至动量（即位置基分布比目标更宽）时二者一致。

Result: 在欧氏、双曲、球面空间上的数值实验显示，采样质量与数值开销相对于黎曼连续归一化流具有竞争力，并验证了所学势函数的可解释性。

Conclusion: 黎曼神经哈密顿流为流形上的生成建模提供了可逆、保体积且可解释的框架，其学习势与匹配势间差距可理论分解，基分布与动量转移条件可保证一致性。

Abstract: Hamiltonian normalizing flows are attractive generative models because their phase-space maps are invertible and volume preserving, but most neural constructions are formulated in Euclidean space. We introduce Riemannian Neural Hamiltonian Flows, which combine the fixed kinetic energy of a Riemannian manifold, a learned scalar potential, and an explicit geodesic leapfrog integrator. Our analysis explains how the learned Hamiltonian can be made interpretable. Every normalizable potential defines an implicit profile, and the position marginal initially accelerates along the relative score between that profile and the base. The matched potential is the interpretable specialization for which the implicit profile is the target. In the isotropic Gaussian case, the mechanism corresponds to a phase-space rotation. A local harmonic analysis extends this result around each mode of a general target on a manifold. The gap between the learned and the matched potential is the sum of a residual memory of the base and a bias of the model, and the two potentials agree when the position base has been transferred to the momentum. This can be achieved when the former is broader than the target. Numerical experiments on Euclidean, hyperbolic, and spherical spaces show competitive sample quality and numerical cost against a Riemannian continuous normalizing flow, and confirm the interpretability of the learned potential.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Gaussian Worlds: Latent Geometry Matters for JEPAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21656)
*Léo Nicollier,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将分布匹配JEPA的可恢复性分析从欧氏空间推广到嵌入黎曼流形，证明球面均匀分布下的分布匹配可保证正交变换意义下的线性恢复，并给出比高斯情形更紧的近似恢复界，从而说明高斯唯一性并非分布匹配JEPA的普遍性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Klindt等，2026）在欧氏假设下证明：将表示匹配到高斯目标可在线性变换意义下恢复高斯隐变量，且高斯是唯一具有该保证的分布。这一"高斯唯一性"结论被视作分布匹配JEPA的普遍属性，但其分析依赖于欧氏几何假设，未考虑隐变量位于嵌入黎曼流形的情形。作者由此发问：非欧隐几何是否能允许其他可线性恢复的分布，从而挑战高斯唯一性的普适性。

Method: 将分布匹配JEPA的分析框架从欧氏空间推广到支撑于嵌入黎曼流形的隐变量，推导在何种隐几何与正样本对（positive-pair）动力学条件下，对齐与精确分布匹配能够保证线性恢复。重点分析隐变量在球面上均匀分布、且表示被匹配到同一球面分布的情形，刻画此时最优表示的形式。进一步推导近似恢复界，并比较球面世界与高斯世界的界紧性。实验在欧氏（高斯）、球面与环面隐空间上进行，并扩展到高维Clifford-torus世界。

Result: 理论结果表明：当隐变量在球面均匀分布且表示匹配到同一球面分布时，任意最优表示都可在线性意义上恢复隐状态，且仅相差一个正交变换。这说明存在非高斯的、可线性恢复的分布，故高斯唯一性不是分布匹配JEPA的普遍性质。此外推导的近似恢复界在球面世界严格比高斯世界更紧。实验显示，几何相容的目标在优化成功时带来更好的线性恢复，而目标不匹配则会扭曲隐结构；该优势在高维Clifford-torus世界中依然保持。

Conclusion: 分布匹配JEPA的可线性恢复性依赖于隐变量所处的几何结构；在球面等非欧流形上，匹配相应几何目标的分布同样可实现线性恢复，且近似恢复保证更紧。因此，JEPA的目标分布选择应与其隐几何相容，高斯并非普适最优解。

Abstract: Recent Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) prevent representation collapse by constraining learned representations to follow a prescribed target distribution, such as an isotropic Gaussian or the uniform distribution on a hypersphere. Klindt et al. (2026) showed that, under their Euclidean assumptions, matching a Gaussian target can recover Gaussian latent variables up to a linear transformation, and that the Gaussian is the unique distribution with this guarantee. We extend their analysis to latent variables supported on embedded Riemannian manifolds and derive conditions on the latent geometry and positive-pair dynamics under which alignment and exact distribution matching guarantee linear recovery. In particular, when the latent variables are uniformly distributed on a sphere and the representations are matched to the same spherical distribution, every optimal representation recovers the latent state up to an orthogonal transformation. This shows that Gaussian uniqueness is not a universal property of distribution-matched JEPAs: non-Euclidean latent geometries can admit other linearly recoverable distributions. We further derive an approximate-recovery bound that is strictly tighter for the spherical world than for the Gaussian world. Experiments on Gaussian, spherical, and toroidal latent spaces show that geometrically compatible targets yield better linear recovery when optimization succeeds, whereas mismatched targets distort the latent structure. This advantage persists in high-dimensional Clifford-torus worlds.

</details>


### [121] [Multi-Domain Clustering via Measure Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21664)
*Rafael Pereira Eufrazio,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Charles Casimiro Cavalcante*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于测度量化的多域聚类通用框架，通过学习共享簇原型并用概率度量（Sinkhorn散度或MMD）最小化各域测度与原型测度之差，配合小批量优化实现可扩展的拟合与分配，在5个多域基准上优于经典及多域聚类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类是数据分析的基础任务，通常由K-means等基于质心的方法处理。然而多域场景下缺乏一个统一、可扩展且能跨域共享结构的聚类框架。

Method: 将多域聚类建模为测度量化：给定多个域的样本，学习一组共享的簇原型，通过最小化每个域的概率测度与原型测度之间的概率度量（Sinkhorn散度或MMD）来优化原型。样本分配采用两种策略：最近质心法，或最优传输（域内所有样本耦合的协同分配）。引入小批量优化策略以降低内存与计算开销，同时保持聚类性能。

Result: 在图像、音频和传感器数据的5个多域基准上，基于Sinkhorn的方法一致优于经典聚类与多域聚类基线；当样本规模扩大到数十万时该优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 该框架为多域聚类提供了统一、可扩展的解决方案，Sinkhorn度量下的原型学习配合小批量优化兼具性能与可扩展性，适用于大规模多域数据。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, typically addressed through centroid-based methods such as K-means. In this work, we present a general framework for multi-domain clustering via measure quantization: given samples from multiple domains, we learn a shared set of cluster prototypes by minimizing a probability metric, such as the Sinkhorn divergence or the Maximum Mean Discrepancy, between each domain's probability measure and the measure of prototypes. Data points are then assigned to clusters either via nearest centroid, or via optimal transport, a collaborative strategy that couples all samples within a domain. A mini-batch optimization strategy makes both fitting and assignment scalable, reducing memory and computational cost while preserving clustering performance. Experimental results on 5 multi-domain benchmarks spanning image, audio and sensor data show that our Sinkhorn-based method consistently outperforms classical and multi-domain clustering baselines, and that this advantage persists when scaling to hundreds of thousands of samples.

</details>


### [122] [Optimization Geometry of Equivalent Brownian RKHS Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21693)
*Mahdi Mohammadigohari,Gustau Camps-Valls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控的有限布朗RKHS中研究等价参数化对优化算法的影响：即使在相同函数类、内蕴范数与近似空间下，节点、增量与谱坐标也会导致不同的优化几何与Adam行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同参数化可表示同一函数与内蕴范数，却可能诱导不同的优化算法行为；作者希望在固定模型、固定函数类与固定正则化下隔离并量化这种“坐标效应”。

Method: 构造有限布朗RKHS，采用节点、增量与谱坐标；借助有限元、RKHS插值、布朗协方差与混合边界DCT恒等式，显式给出共享假设类、布朗能量、近似算子与坐标映射；在映射初始化、相同标量步长与相同小批量下分析GD/SGD轨迹、增量GD的显式Euler解释与条件数界，并用标准Adam约定分析正交等变群。

Result: 节点与谱GD/SGD在映射后轨迹完全相同；增量GD等价于常布朗/Sobolev度量的显式Euler步，步长因子为1/h；对布朗正则最小二乘，κ2(H_inc)≤1+A/ρ且不随网格分辨率G变化（固定A、ρ及给定归一化）；标准Adam约定下通用正交等变群恰为带符号置换，块DCT-VIII不属于该群。Float64五组网格数值实验验证了有限恒等式、映射单层与递归轨迹、条件数预测及与定理一致的Adam分离。

Conclusion: 在保持所表示函数、内蕴正则项与近似空间不变的前提下，坐标选择会单独影响优化几何与自适应优化器行为；该受控框架可清晰隔离参数化效应。

Abstract: Equivalent finite parameterizations can represent the same functions and intrinsic norm yet induce different optimization algorithms. We study this effect in a controlled finite Brownian RKHS with nodal, increment, and spectral coordinates. Classical finite-element, RKHS-interpolation, Brownian-covariance, and mixed-boundary DCT identities make the shared hypothesis class, Brownian energy, approximation operator, and coordinate maps explicit. Our main results concern the optimization geometry of this fixed model. With mapped initialization, identical scalar steps, and identical minibatches, nodal and spectral GD/SGD have exactly the same mapped trajectories. Increment GD is an explicit Euler step for the constant Brownian/Sobolev metric, with factor $1/h$. For Brownian-regularized least squares, $κ_2(\mathbf H_{\mathrm{inc}})\le1+A/ρ$, independently of grid resolution $G$ for fixed $A$, $ρ>0$, and the stated normalization. Under the stated standard-Adam convention, the universal orthogonal equivariance group is exactly the signed permutations; the block DCT-VIII transform is not one. Float64 tests over five grids numerically verify the finite identities, mapped one-layer and recursive trajectories, conditioning predictions, and theorem-matched Adam separation. Thus coordinate effects are isolated without changing the represented functions, intrinsic regularizer, or approximation space.

</details>


### [123] [SpecQuant: Speculative Decoding with Multi-Parent Quantization for Adaptive LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21704)
*Harish KB,Jagadeeswaran M,Pradheep P,Yuvanesh S,Sivakumar T*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecQuant：一种免训练框架，将投机解码与多父量化结合，通过共享基座模型派生多量化变体并动态路由查询，在MMLU、AlpacaEval、GSM8K上实现35-43%加速且精度损失不超过2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上本地运行大语言模型受限于算力与内存。现有加速技术（量化、投机解码、自适应推理）虽能带来显著提速，但通常需要重训练、按架构调优或额外的草稿模型。

Method: SpecQuant从共享基座模型派生多个量化变体（INT4、FP8、FP16），并结合投机解码与多父量化，基于预测的查询复杂度进行动态路由：简单或事实性任务使用轻量变体，复杂推理任务或长上下文输入使用全精度模型。共享权重设计确保投机解码有足够的token接受率，且避免独立草稿父模型的兼容性问题。

Result: 在基于Qwen2.5的模型上，于MMLU、AlpacaEval、GSM8K数据集或基准测试中评估，实现35-43%的加速，且精度下降不超过2%。

Conclusion: SpecQuant无需特殊基础设施或专业知识，可实现跨多种硬件的实用化端侧LLM部署。

Abstract: Running large language models (LLMs) locally continues to be limited by restrictions of compute and memory on consumer hardware. The popular acceleration technologies, such as quantization, speculative decoding, and adaptive inferencing, offer substantial speed boosts but usually necessitate retraining, per architecture tuning, or draft models. SpecQuant is a trainingfree framework, that combines speculative decoding with multiparent quantization to perform adaptive, efficient inference of LLMs. SpecQuant derives multiple quantized variants (INT4, FP8, FP16) from a shared base model, and dynamically routes queries based on predicted complexity; lightweight variants are used for simple or factual tasks, and full-precision models are used for complex reasoning tasks or long-context inputs. The shared-weight design of SpecQuant ensures sufficient token acceptance for speculative decoding without compatibility issues using separate draft parent models. We evaluate SpecQuant on Qwen2.5 based models on the MMLU, AlpacaEval, and GSM8K datasets, or benchmarks, demonstrating 35-43% speedups without degrading accuracy greater than 2%, substantial within the LLM community. SpecQuant enables practical on-device LLM deployment across diverse hardware without special infrastructure or expertise.

</details>


### [124] [GEM-MPC: Balancing Exploration and Exploitation through Expert-Guided Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21735)
*Alvaro Serra-Gomez,Thomas Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEM-MPC是一种基于MPPI的强化学习方法，通过策略克隆、KL正则化探索策略以及门控先验蒸馏来改善规划与学习的协同，在低计算预算下持续优于既有基于规划的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维连续控制中的高效探索仍是强化学习核心难题。基于规划的方法结合在线规划与已学习策略及价值函数，但其组件在训练中易失配：采样策略可能偏离规划器行为，回放中的规划分布会随模型和价值函数更新而过时。重分析虽可刷新目标，但计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出GEM-MPC：(1) 用MPPI将两个策略——一个经训练以克隆规划器的策略，与一个围绕其进行探索的KL正则化策略——组合进规划过程，同时提供利用与引导探索；(2) 引入门控先验蒸馏(Gated Prior Distillation)，仅在存储的规划分布比当前先验提供更优目标时才从中学习，从而缓解过时规划数据的影响而无需完整重分析。

Result: 在多个连续控制基准上，GEM-MPC在更低计算预算下始终优于现有基于规划的基线方法。

Conclusion: GEM-MPC通过改进规划与学习的交互、并有选择性地利用规划分布，有效缓解了策略漂移与数据过时问题，在较低计算开销下实现了更优的连续控制性能。

Abstract: Effective exploration in high-dimensional continuous control remains a central challenge in reinforcement learning. Planning-based methods address this by combining online planning with learned policies and value functions, but their components can become misaligned during training: learned sampling policies may diverge from planner behavior, while planning distributions stored in replay become stale as the model and value function evolve. Reanalysis can refresh these targets, but at substantial computational cost. We propose GEM-MPC, an MPPI-based reinforcement learning method that improves the interaction between planning and learning. GEM-MPC uses MPPI to combine a policy trained to clone the planner with a KL-regularized policy that explores around it, providing complementary exploitation and guided exploration within planning. We further introduce Gated Prior Distillation, which selectively learns from stored planning distributions only when they provide a better target than the current prior, reducing the impact of stale planning data without requiring full reanalysis. Across continuous-control benchmarks, GEM-MPC consistently outperforms existing planning-based baselines under lower computational budgets.

</details>


### [125] [GraphSkillEvo: Evolutionary Optimization of Graph-Structured Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21749)
*Rui Sun,Zhi Zheng,Zhenkun Wang,Zhichao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM智能体的"技能"表示为图结构的自然语言产物，并据此构建了基于种群进化的技能优化框架GraphSkillEvo，在五个基准上超过基线SkillOpt。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化方法将技能表示为非结构化自然语言指令，存在两大问题：1）缺乏显式工作流级指导且冗余严重，LLM难以执行；2）无约束的自然语言技能搜索空间过大，导致优化效率低下。

Method: 将技能建模为图结构自然语言产物：节点表示执行步骤及其操作指导，有向边编码步骤间依赖上下文的转移关系；在该结构化表示上提出GraphSkillEvo，一个基于种群的进化优化框架，使用变异与交叉算子，通过维护多个候选技能并组合有效组件来探索结构化技能空间。

Result: 在五个智能体基准上的大量实验表明，GraphSkillEvo稳定优于强基线SkillOpt，在GPT-5.4-nano上平均准确率提升4.01%，在GPT-5.4上提升1.76%。

Conclusion: 图结构技能表示能提供清晰的工作流级指导并利于优化；基于种群进化的GraphSkillEvo相比纯LLM迭代自精炼具有更广、更全面的技能空间探索能力，是更有效的技能优化范式。

Abstract: Skills can improve the performance of Large Language Model (LLM) agents by providing task-specific procedural guidance, while skill optimization further improves their effectiveness through iterative refinement. However, existing skill optimization methods typically represent skills as unstructured natural-language instructions, creating two key challenges: 1) Unstructured skills often lack explicit workflow-level guidance and contain substantial redundancy, making them difficult for LLMs to execute; 2) the vast search space of unconstrained natural-language skills makes skill optimization ineffective. To address these challenges, we propose representing skills as graph-structured natural-language artifacts. In graph-structured skills, each node represents an execution step together with its operational guidance, while directed edges encode context-dependent transitions between steps. Compared to unstructured skills, graph-structured skills can provide clear workflow-level guidance. Moreover, the proposed graph-structured skill can also facilitate skill optimization. Building on this structured representation, we introduce GraphSkillEvo, a population-based evolutionary optimization framework with mutation and crossover operators for graph-structured skills. By maintaining multiple candidate skills and combining effective components, GraphSkillEvo enables broader and more comprehensive exploration of the structured skill space than purely LLM-based iterative self-refinement. Extensive experiments across five agent benchmarks demonstrate that GraphSkillEvo consistently outperforms the strong skill optimization baseline SkillOpt, improving average accuracy by 4.01% on GPT-5.4-nano and 1.76% on GPT-5.4. Our code is available at https://github.com/ruisun7/GraphSkillEvo.

</details>


### [126] [Bilevel Optimization of Topology and Hyperparameters (BOTH)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21758)
*Suryanarayanan Manoj Sanu,Miguel Anibal Bessa,Alejandro Marcos Aragón*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用自动微分对拓扑优化本身求导，获得“超梯度”，从而在优化设计的同时调节拓扑优化中的超参数，且可扩展到数千个超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化虽能自动化设计，但依赖众多“魔法数字”式超参数，其调参需深厚领域知识和大量试错；代理辅助的超参数优化又要求严格限制超参数数量。

Method: 利用自动微分对拓扑优化过程本身求导，得到超梯度，用其与主优化联合调节超参数；发现仅评估一到两个拓扑优化步骤即足够有信息，并将方法用于应力约束问题和柔度问题，后者采用密度场的神经参数化。

Result: 验证一到两步拓扑优化即可提供充分信息；方法能良好扩展到数千个超参数，代价仅相当于几次标准拓扑优化运行。

Conclusion: 超梯度方法为拓扑优化的超参数调节提供了高效、可扩展的途径，缓解了魔法数字问题。

Abstract: Topology optimization (TO) represents a significant step towards automating the design process: given a working simulation, TO can produce a viable prototype at the press of a button by differentiating the simulation and iteratively improving the design. In practice, however, TO is riddled with ``magic numbers''---hyperparameters whose tuning significantly affects the outcome. Finding the right values typically requires not only deep problem-specific knowledge but also extensive trial-and-error. While practitioners can use surrogate-assisted hyperparameter optimization as an alternative, this approach requires strictly limiting the number of hyperparameters through careful problem formulation. Here, we propose differentiating TO itself using automatic differentiation. This yields ``hypergradients'' that allow us to tune these hyperparameters in tandem with the primary optimization. We show that evaluating just one or two steps of TO is sufficiently informative and that the method scales favorably to thousands of hyperparameters at an expense comparable to only a few standard TO runs. We demonstrate this approach on stress-constrained and compliance problems, with the latter utilizing a neural parameterization of the density field.

</details>


### [127] [RegKT: Interpretable and Robust Deep Knowledge Tracing With IRT-Regularizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21791)
*Samuel Girard,Juan D. Pinto,Jill-Jênn Vie,Amel Bouzeghoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型正则化技术，旨在提升深度学习知识追踪模型的鲁棒性，同时改善其可解释性，以缓解小数据集下的过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习知识追踪模型精度不断提升，但可解释性下降，影响教育实践者的采纳；同时深度模型在小规模教育数据集上易过拟合。因此需要兼顾可解释性与鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的正则化技术，用于增强基于深度学习的知识追踪模型的鲁棒性，并同步提升其可解释性。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果或量化指标。

Conclusion: 该方法同时应对可解释性与过拟合两大挑战，使知识追踪更适用于真实教育场景。

Abstract: As deep learning models continue to advance, knowledge tracing models have achieved higher accuracy. However, these gains come at the cost of reduced interpretability, which is crucial for practitioners in educational settings to adopt new methodologies. Additionally, deep learning models are prone to overfitting, particularly when dealing with the small datasets that are common in educational applications. In this paper, we propose a novel regularization technique designed to enhance the robustness of deep-learning-based knowledge tracing models, while simultaneously improving their interpretability. Our method addresses both the interpretability and overfitting challenges, making it more feasible for real-world educational applications.

</details>


### [128] [Matrix AdaGrad: Row-wise and Column-wise Adaptive Subgradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21815)
*Wenpeng Zhang,Runsheng Yu,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向矩阵参数的在线镜像下降（OMD）框架，通过行/列近端函数推导出 Row-AdaGrad 与 Column-AdaGrad，并给出更紧的 regret 界，在矩阵分解与深度网络上验证了矩阵感知自适应的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdaGrad、Adam 等自适应优化方法的缩放机制主要针对向量参数，未显式利用矩阵结构；近期矩阵感知优化器虽展示了结构化优化的收益，但缺乏类似 AdaGrad 的通用理论框架来推导矩阵感知的适应性缩放。

Method: 构建带自适应近端函数的矩阵参数在线镜像下降（OMD）通用框架，通过在线 regret 最小化推导矩阵感知自适应优化；引入行向与列向矩阵近端函数，分析相应 regret 权衡，据此导出 Row-wise Matrix AdaGrad（Row-AdaGrad）与 Column-wise Matrix AdaGrad（Column-AdaGrad），其自适应缩放由累积的行向或列向梯度范数决定。

Result: 建立了 regret 保证，并证明在结构化梯度下，矩阵感知的 regret 界可严格优于逐元素 AdaGrad 的界；在矩阵分解与深度神经网络训练实验中，将自适应缩放与矩阵结构对齐带来了更优表现，包括在更大学习率与更深网络下更好的优化稳定性与可训练性。

Conclusion: 该工作为矩阵感知自适应优化提供了与 AdaGrad 类比的一般理论框架，表明对齐矩阵结构的自适应缩放能提升优化稳定性与深层网络可训练性。

Abstract: Adaptive optimization methods such as AdaGrad and Adam are widely used in modern neural-network training, but their adaptive scaling is primarily designed for vector-valued parameters and does not explicitly exploit matrix structure. Recent matrix-aware optimizers demonstrate the benefits of structured optimization, yet a general theoretical framework for deriving matrix-aware adaptivity comparable to that of AdaGrad remains lacking. In this work, we develop a general Online Mirror Descent framework with adaptive proximal functions for matrix-valued parameters, providing a principled approach to deriving matrix-aware adaptive optimization through online regret minimization. By introducing row-wise and column-wise matrix proximal functions and analyzing the resulting regret trade-off, we derive Row-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Row-AdaGrad) and Column-wise Matrix AdaGrad (Column-AdaGrad), with adaptive scaling determined by the accumulated row-wise or column-wise gradient norms. We establish regret guarantees and show that these matrix-aware bounds can be strictly tighter than those of entry-wise AdaGrad under structured gradients. Experiments on matrix factorization and deep neural-network training further demonstrate the benefits of aligning adaptive scaling with matrix structure, including improved optimization stability and trainability at larger learning rates and greater network depths.

</details>


### [129] [Federated Deep Clustering Networks for High-Dimensional and Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21829)
*Morris Stallmann,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Marcin Pietrasik,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出联邦深度聚类方法FedDCN，通过合成数据增强与几何正则化实现潜在空间对齐，提升非IID场景鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据聚类是无监督学习的基础任务，集中式场景下深度聚类方法成熟，但联邦学习下数据分布于各客户端且需隐私保护，相关研究较少。现有联邦深度聚类方法在客户端数据非独立同分布（non-IID）时性能不可靠，亟需提升鲁棒性与潜在空间对齐能力。

Method: 将Deep Clustering Networks推广到联邦场景，提出FedDCN，同时优化重构损失与聚类损失。为应对non-IID数据，FedDCN生成合成数据增强，并在学习目标中引入几何正则化以实现潜在空间对齐。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法在IID与非IID假设下均展现出有效性，并据此指出了未来研究方向。

Conclusion: FedDCN通过联合优化重构与聚类损失，并借助合成增强和几何正则化，有效缓解了联邦深度聚类在non-IID数据下的性能退化问题。

Abstract: Clustering high-dimensional data is a fundamental task in unsupervised machine learning with applications to a variety of domains. In the centralized data scenario, this task is commonly solved using deep clustering methods that utilize deep neural network architectures to learn clustering-friendly latent space representations. In Federated Learning, where data is distributed between clients and is private, deep clustering methods are less explored. In particular, recently introduced federated deep clustering methods, despite showing very promising performance, still fall short in reliably providing good performance if data across clients are non-identically-independently distributed. In this work, we introduce a generalization of Deep Clustering Networks to the federated scenario, named FedDCN, that simultaneously optimizes a reconstruction loss and a clustering loss. To ensure robustness and latent space alignment in non-identically-independently distributed data scenarios, FedDCN generates synthetic data augmentations, and its learning objective includes a geometric regularization for latent space alignment. Through experimental evaluation, the effectiveness of the approach under IID and non-IID assumptions is demonstrated, and future research directions are identified.

</details>


### [130] [The Weight Is Over - Interactive Diffusion on Consumer GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21849)
*Frieder Ganz,Maximilian Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文聚焦扩散模型在端侧推理中的落地问题，提出嵌入翻译器、速度/质量/内存权衡的扫描配方，以及可在近期GPU上实现亚秒级TTFI的交互式端侧图像生成编辑器三项贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧推理当前主要集中于语言模型，扩散流水线因内存开销大、延迟敏感，且需协调嵌入器、transformer、解码器及非标准化的后处理而难以普及；作者希望在性能、质量与模型体积之间取得权衡，以覆盖尽可能多的客户端设备。

Method: （1）设计嵌入翻译器，将小型文本编码器映射到大型编码器空间，以降低权重与延迟；（2）提出可复现的扫描配方，用于在扩散流水线中导航速度/质量/内存三角权衡。

Result: 实现了一个交互式端侧图像生成编辑器，在近期GPU上达到亚秒级的TTFI（首帧推理时间）。

Conclusion: 通过嵌入翻译与系统性权衡扫描，扩散流水线的端侧部署可在保证质量的同时显著降低内存与延迟，支持覆盖广泛客户端设备的交互式图像生成。

Abstract: On-device inference is booming, but the momentum is almost all in language models. Diffusion pipelines are memory hungry, latency-sensitive, and require orchestrating an embedder, a transformer, a decoder, and often further postprocessing that is not as standardized as LLM inference loops are. We navigate the trade-off between performance, quality, and model footprint to reach as many client devices in the wild as possible. We make three contributions: an embedding translator that maps a small text encoder into a large encoder space to cut weight and latency; a reproducible sweep recipe for navigating the speed/quality/memory triangle in diffusion pipelines; and an interactive on-device image generation editor achieving sub-second TTFI on recent GPUs.

</details>


### [131] [Neural Cellular Automata Learn General Features in their Hidden Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21870)
*Etienne Guichard,Stefano Nichele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经细胞自动机（NCA）隐藏通道的内部动态，提出将预训练教师模型的隐藏状态注入学生模型以引导早期优化的迁移学习机制；在少样本MNIST上以约9800参数超越同类循环/前馈结构，并揭示隐藏通道解耦特征提取与分类共识、收敛到正交状态、捕获尺度不变拓扑原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过度参数化虽带来强泛化，但在少样本场景下易过拟合与记忆化。NCA是参数高效的替代方案，但既有研究主要关注其输出，对内部隐藏通道的作用缺乏探索。作者希望挖掘隐藏状态动态作为参数高效迁移学习的计算基底。

Method: 1）分析NCA隐藏通道的内部动态；2）提出新的迁移学习机制，将预训练教师模型的隐藏状态注入学生模型，引导其早期优化；3）在少样本与尺度变化的MNIST基准上与循环、前馈架构对比；4）进行机制分析，考察隐藏通道如何解耦特征提取与分类一致性、是否收敛到相互正交状态、是否捕获尺度不变拓扑原语；5）用仅训练0-5数字的教师迁移特征，测试学生对未见类别的少样本表现。

Result: NCA在少样本与尺度变化MNIST上优于可比的循环与前馈架构，以极低参数预算（约9800参数）实现更优泛化。机制分析显示隐藏通道吸收形态复杂度，将特征提取与统一分类共识解耦，并收敛为相互正交状态；隐藏通道捕获的是通用、尺度不变的拓扑原语而非类别特定模板，使学生在未见类别上借助教师（仅训练0-5）迁移的特征获得强少样本性能。

Conclusion: 利用隐藏状态动态作为稳健、去中心化的计算基底，可支撑参数高效的迁移学习，为NCA内部表征的价值提供了新证据。

Abstract: Modern deep learning models achieve impressive generalization through over-parameterization, but this paradigm often struggles with overfitting and memorization in few-shot regimes. Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) offer a highly parameter-efficient alternative, yet research has focused primarily on their output, leaving the role of their internal hidden channels largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the internal dynamics of NCA hidden channels and introduce a novel transfer-learning mechanism that injects a pretrained teacher's hidden states into a student model to guide early optimization. Evaluated on few-shot and scale-variant MNIST benchmarks, NCAs outperform comparable recurrent and feed-forward architectures, demonstrating superior generalization with a minimal parameter budget (~9,800 parameters). Mechanistic analysis reveals that the hidden channels decouple feature extraction from uniform classification consensus by absorbing morphological complexity and converging to mutually orthogonal states. Furthermore, we demonstrate that these hidden channels capture general, scale-invariant topological primitives rather than class-specific templates. This allows a student model to achieve strong few-shot performance on unseen classes using features transferred from a teacher trained only on a subset of digits (0-5). Our results highlight the potential of utilizing hidden-state dynamics as a robust, decentralized computational substrate for parameter-efficient transfer learning

</details>


### [132] [Geometric Mean Pooling for Equal-Weight Multiplicative Coarse-Graining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21876)
*Ang-Kun Wu,Fangdi Wen,Jingtao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何平均池化（GMP），一种带符号池化算子，将特征符号的乘积与特征幅值的几何平均结合，用于替代平均池化和最大池化的加性与极值偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均池化具有加性偏置、最大池化具有极值偏置，二者均不适合需要等权乘性组合的任务；受量子多体物理中局部到全局组合的启发，作者希望设计一种保留联合符号信息与乘性尺度、且不引入可学习参数的池化算子。

Method: 提出GMP：融合特征符号的乘积与特征幅值的几何平均；证明非重叠层次化GMP可保持相应的全局乘性统计量；在合成序列任务、迭代粗粒化、图像分类和分子亲脂性回归上进行评估。

Result: 在合成任务上，GMP比平均池化和最大池化更准确地恢复基于乘积的信号，并在所测试的乘性输入噪声水平下保持预测性能；在图像与分子数据上，其有效性取决于表示方式、目标参数化和局部/全局池化的放置位置。

Conclusion: GMP是一种互补的、依赖具体情境的归纳偏置，适用于等权乘性组合合理的任务，而非标准池化算子的通用替代方案。

Abstract: As an alternative to the additive and extremal biases of average and max pooling, we introduce Geometric Mean Pooling (GMP), a signed pooling operator that combines the product of feature signs with the geometric mean of feature magnitudes. Motivated by local-to-global composition in quantum many-body physics, GMP retains both joint sign information and a characteristic multiplicative scale without introducing learnable pooling parameters. We show that non-overlapping hierarchical GMP preserves the corresponding global multiplicative statistic and evaluate it on synthetic sequence tasks, iterative coarse-graining, image classification, and molecular lipophilicity regression. On the synthetic tasks, GMP recovers product-based signals more accurately than average and max pooling and maintains predictive performance under the tested levels of multiplicative input noise. On image and molecular data, however, its effectiveness depends on the representation, target parameterization, and placement of local and global pooling. These results position GMP as a complementary, regime-dependent inductive bias for tasks in which equal-weight multiplicative composition is plausible, rather than as a universal replacement for standard pooling operators.

</details>


### [133] [Detecting Pretraining Data in Large Language Models from a Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21888)
*Chenye Ke,Zirui Liu,Qi Liu,Yan Zhuang,Jintao Zhang,Zhenya Huang,Shijin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Energy Transfer Detection (ETD)，通过在预测损失与预测熵构成的联合空间中引入“倾斜边界”进行熵校正，将预训练数据检测解释为宏观剩余自由能转移，从而提升成员与非成员的区分度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于似然的检测器在高似然情形下难以区分“训练暴露”与“强泛化”，仅使用水平决策边界会把可预测的非成员误判为成员。作者希望利用预测熵对损失进行相对校正，改善成员—非成员的可分性。

Method: 在预测损失与预测熵的联合空间中，用倾斜边界替代水平边界，以预测损失相对于预测熵进行评估。作者分析熵校正能在保留期望成员信号的同时降低方差，提升标准化后的成员—非成员分离；并将均值—方差分析推广到非零均值熵差的更一般情形。该熵调整分数具有 Helmholtz 自由能解释，据此提出 Energy Transfer Detection (ETD)，从宏观残差自由能转移视角进行检测。

Result: 大量实验表明，ETD 取得最佳平均检测性能：平均 AUROC 最高提升 3.5%，TPR@5%FPR 最高提升 5.1%，并在多种设置下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 熵校正的倾斜边界能更有效地区分预训练数据成员与非成员，且可由自由能视角统一解释，ETD 在检测性能与稳健性上均优于仅基于似然的方法。

Abstract: Detecting pretraining data in large language models is challenging because high likelihood can reflect either training exposure or strong generalization. In the joint space of prediction loss and predictive entropy, a likelihood-only detector uses a horizontal boundary and can mistake predictable non-members for members. Motivated by this, we introduce an inclined boundary that evaluates prediction loss relative to predictive entropy. Our analysis shows that entropy correction can preserve the expected membership signal while reducing its variance, thereby improving standardized member--non-member separation. We further extend the mean--variance analysis to the more general setting with a nonzero mean entropy gap. Interestingly, this entropy-adjusted score admits a Helmholtz free-energy interpretation, leading to Energy Transfer Detection (ETD), which views pretraining data detection from a macroscopic residual free-energy transfer perspective. Extensive experiments show that ETD achieves the best average detection performance, improving average AUROC by up to 3.5\% and TPR@5\%FPR by up to 5.1\%, while remaining robust across diverse settings.

</details>


### [134] [LLMs as Feature Engineers for Text-and-Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21894)
*Merwan Barlier,Blaz Skrlj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种迭代框架，利用生成器LLM与提取器LLM协同，从非结构化文本中自动抽取可解释、符合schema的类别特征以服务表格预测；通过将AUC排序反转等模型错误转为自然语言反馈来引导特征搜索，在三个公开数据集上相比无引导搜索提速至多3倍，且生成特征与TF-IDF、稠密嵌入结合时具强多视图互补性并主导SHAP重要性排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向表格预测模型的特征工程通常依赖人工设计，而非结构化文本中蕴含的语义特征难以被自动、可解释地抽取。现有自动化特征方法多为黑箱，缺乏schema约束与实例级可解释性，因此需要构建一个既能自动发现、又能提供语义审计线索的特征抽取流程。

Method: 采用迭代式搜索框架：生成器LLM提出语义定义，独立的提取器LLM将定义实例化为特征，下游表格模型评估其预测性能。为提升搜索效率，将显式模型错误（如AUC排序反转）翻译为自然语言反馈，用于指导LLM修正特定预测失败；由此形成错误驱动的闭环优化。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，错误驱动循环相较无引导搜索将特征发现速度提升至多3倍。生成特征展现出强多视图互补性，与TF-IDF和稠密嵌入结合后严格优于任何子集。同时，发现的特征在SHAP重要性排名中占主导地位，并为每次预测提供完全透明、语义化的审计轨迹。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，以错误反馈驱动LLM进行schema约束特征搜索，可在提升表格预测性能的同时保证实例级可解释性，为可解释自动化特征工程提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We introduce an iterative framework that automates the extraction of interpretable, schema-bound categorical features from unstructured text for tabular prediction models. To navigate the feature space, a generator LLM proposes semantic definitions, a separate extractor LLM materializes the features, and a downstream tabular model evaluates their predictive performance. We optimize this search by translating explicit model errors, such as AUC ranking inversions, into natural-language feedback, steering the LLM to resolve specific predictive failures. Evaluated across three public datasets, this error-driven loop accelerates feature discovery by up to $3\times$ compared to unguided search. Empirically, the generated features demonstrate strong multi-view complementarity, strictly outperforming any subset when combined with TF-IDF and dense embeddings. Finally, the framework guarantees instance-level interpretability: the discovered features dominate SHAP importance rankings and provide a fully transparent, semantic audit trail for every prediction.

</details>


### [135] [ExpBoN: Exponential-Noise Best-of-$n$ for Efficient Test-Time LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21899)
*Yanxiao Liu,Sicheng Wan,Deniz Gündüz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ExpBoN——一种基于指数噪声report-noisy-max机制的软Best-of-n采样方法，并整合到引导式推测推理框架中形成ExpGSI，实现高效且理论上更优的LLM推理时对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Best-of-n（BoN）采样虽简单有效，但硬最大化（hard maximization）只能对奖励与分布偏移之间的权衡提供粗糙控制。Soft BoN（Verdun et al. 2025）提供更平滑控制并收敛到KL正则化奖励最大化的最优分布，但仍有改进空间，尤其在收敛速度、误差刻画和推理效率方面。

Method: 1) 提出ExpBoN：基于指数噪声的report-noisy-max机制的软BoN方法，具有精确的有限n分解；2) 理论分析：证明其在总变差距离、期望奖励及双向KL散度上均呈指数快速收敛，并系统分析其收敛性与遗憾（regret）行为；3) 将ExpBoN集成到引导式推测推理（GSI）框架，形成ExpGSI，用于奖励引导的LLM高效对齐。

Result: 在MATH500、MMLU-STEM与Minerva Math上，使用Qwen2.5-Math和Qwen3模型族实验：ExpGSI在保持可比精度的同时显著降低计算成本。Qwen2.5-Math在各候选预算下估算计算量减少14%–39%；Qwen3在n=16时减少高达45%。

Conclusion: 本研究为指数噪声BoN及高效的推理时LLM对齐提供了理论与算法基础，ExpGSI在兼顾精度的前提下大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: Best-of-$n$ (BoN) sampling is a simple yet effective inference-time alignment method, but hard maximization provides only coarse control over the trade-off between reward and distribution shift. Soft Best-of-$n$ (Verdun et al. 2025) provides smoother control and converges to the optimal distribution associated with KL-regularized reward maximization. In this paper, we introduce ExpBoN, an alternative soft BoN method based on the exponential-noise report-noisy-max mechanism. It admits an exact finite-$n$ decomposition, which yields exponentially fast convergence in total variation, expected reward, and both directions of KL divergence. We provide comprehensive theoretical analyses of its convergence and regret behavior. We further integrate ExpBoN into the guided speculative inference (GSI) framework (Geuter, Mroueh, and AlvarezMelis 2025), resulting in ExpGSI, for efficient reward-guided LLM alignment. ExpGSI yields substantial reductions in computational cost while maintaining comparable accuracy. Experiments on MATH500, MMLU-STEM, and Minerva Math with the Qwen2.5-Math and Qwen3 model families show that ExpGSI reduces estimated computation by $14\%$-$39\%$ across candidate budgets for Qwen2.5-Math and by up to $45\%$ at $n=16$ for Qwen3. Overall, our results provide a theoretical and algorithmic foundation for exponential-noise BoN and efficient test-time LLM alignment.

</details>


### [136] [Intervention Granularity Matters: Coherent Treatment Bundles in Counterfactual Simulation with Clinical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21906)
*Fangzhou Wang,Yixuan Yang,Camilla Balzarotti,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 临床世界模型的反事实模拟中，干预的编辑粒度会实质影响模型响应：整体编辑治疗组合比单组件编辑产生更大预测状态偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实模拟需界定"何为一次干预"。临床干预以组合形式记录，对MIMIC-IV中945,707个患者小时的共现审计显示，如透析回路各参数等组件从不单独出现，因此单独修改某一组件会描述现实中不存在的状态。作者假设干预编辑粒度会改变世界模型的响应。

Method: 使用Clin-JEPA——一个以每小时治疗文本为条件、基于潜在空间的患者轨迹世界模型。在1,019次有创通气起始处，固定患者病史与其他治疗，对比"编辑单个呼吸机设置"与"编辑真实相似轨迹患者记录的完整配置"两种方式。

Result: 完整组合编辑使预测的下一状态偏移比任何单一设置编辑都更大，且在全部五种设置上表现一致；该差异在控制每次编辑对模型输入改变量后依然存在。

Conclusion: 干预粒度会实质性影响临床世界模型的响应：单组件编辑可能低估治疗敏感性，而感知组合（bundle-aware）的编辑或可为反事实治疗模拟提供更有依据的基础。

Abstract: Counterfactual simulation with a clinical world model means fixing a patient's history, changing the treatment, and reading off the predicted response. Doing so requires deciding what counts as one intervention. In clinical settings, interventions are documented as bundles: a co-occurrence audit of 945,707 patient-hours from MIMIC-IV shows groups of components, such as every parameter of a dialysis circuit, that never appear apart, so an edit that changes one component on its own describes an hour that never occurs in the data. We hypothesize that the granularity at which an intervention is edited changes how a world model responds, and test this with Clin-JEPA, a latent world model of patient trajectories conditioned on hourly treatment text. At 1,019 documented onsets of invasive ventilation, we keep the patient's history and other treatments fixed and compare editing one ventilator setting with editing the complete configuration recorded for a real patient with the most similar recent trajectory. The complete bundle moves the predicted next state further than any single setting, consistently across all five settings, and the difference remains after accounting for how much each edit changes the model's input. Intervention granularity therefore materially affects the response of a clinical world model: single-component edits may understate treatment sensitivity, and bundle-aware editing may offer a better-supported basis for counterfactual treatment simulation.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Kinematics: Benchmarking Simulation Fidelity for Muscle-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21909)
*Ayah G. Ahmad,Claire E. Borden,Maegan Tucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较基于SCONE/HyFyDy与MuJoCo/MyoSim的两套运动模仿强化学习流程，发现两者运动学精度相近，但HyFyDy的肌肉激活更贴合实验EMG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已证明两套MIRL流程均能高保真复现人体运动学，但其能否准确捕捉产生运动的神经肌肉行为尚不明确；这一问题对以肌肉激活模式、代谢成本为优化目标的机器人辅助设备设计与控制尤为关键。

Method: 使用同一套人体运动捕捉与肌电（EMG）测量数据，对SCONE/HyFyDy（强调详尽肌肌腱建模的生理真实性）与MuJoCo/MyoSim（强调计算效率与可扩展策略学习）两套流程进行系统性对比。

Result: 两者均能以相对精度产生相似的运动学结果；HyFyDy的肌肉激活与实验EMG更一致：肌肉激活的平均汇总指标（RMSE, r）分别为HyFyDy (0.164, 0.4) 与 MuJoCo (0.344, 0.11)。

Conclusion: HyFyDy更先进的生理真实性使其当前更适用于肌骨建模；但要推进机器人辅助设备设计，两套方案均需进一步发展，以在可GPU并行化的仿真环境中实现生理真实性。

Abstract: In this work, we conduct a systematic comparison of two state-of-the-art motion-imitation reinforcement learning (MIRL) pipelines, one built on SCONE/HyFyDy and one built on MuJoCo/MyoSim. HyFyDy emphasizes physiological realism through detailed musculotendon modeling, while MuJoCo prioritizes computational efficiency and scalable policy learning. While recent work has demonstrated that both pipelines reproduce human kinematics with high fidelity, it remains unclear if they accurately capture the underlying neuromuscular behavior that produced the movement. This limitation is particularly important for robotic assistive-device design and control, where outcome measures such as muscle activation patterns and metabolic cost are often used as optimization targets. To conduct a systematic comparison, our work compares both pipelines using a common set of human motion-capture and electromyography (EMG) measurements. The results find that while both pipelines produce similar kinematics with relative accuracy, the muscle activations from HyFyDy are more aligned with the experimental EMG, as supported by the average pooled (RMSE, r) values for muscle activations from HyFyDy and MuJoCo: (0.164, 0.4) and (0.344, 0.11), respectively. While we conclude that the more advanced physiological realism of HyFyDy currently makes it more suitable for musculoskeletal modeling, both require further development to bring physiological realism to GPU-parallelizable simulation environments and advance robotic assistive device design.

</details>


### [138] [Kinks vs. Smoothness: Identifiability of Real Analytic nICA for Laplace-like Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21926)
*Isaac Manring,Kejun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在生成函数为实解析、源分布密度函数一阶导数仅有有限个不连续点（如Laplace分布）的条件下，非线性独立成分分析可以实现可辨识性（精确恢复至平凡歧义），并通过对Standard Normalizing Flows和VAE的实验验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性ICA的核心挑战是从观测的复杂数据（如图像或金融时间序列）中恢复真实的潜在独立因子，而非其混淆版本。此前nICA的可辨识性结果通常依赖对源分布或生成函数的强假设，限制了实际适用性。作者希望放宽这些假设，使理论能覆盖更广泛的生成机制并直接适配现有训练流程。

Method: 理论层面：作者针对实解析生成函数，在源概率密度函数一阶导数仅有有限个不连续点（以Laplace分布为典型代表）的假设下，证明可辨识性（至平凡歧义）。证明思路利用源分布中的"拐点"(kinks)与实解析函数的平滑性之间的对比。由于实解析函数可由使用标准激活函数（tanh、softplus、GELU等）的Normalizing Flows或VAE逼近，该结果可几乎无需改动地应用于现有训练管线。实验层面：在真实数据与合成数据上，使用Normalizing Flows和VAE进行实验，验证其可辨识性质。

Result: 实验在真实与合成数据上通过Normalizing Flows和VAE展示了可辨识性质；在CelebA数据集上，方法成功恢复了若干可解释的潜在因子，这些因子控制了数据集中独特的属性。

Conclusion: 论文在较弱的假设下（实解析生成函数 + 源密度一阶导数有限个不连续点）建立了非线性ICA的可辨识性理论，涵盖Laplace分布等常见情形，且与标准Normalizing Flows/VAE训练流程兼容，并在合成与真实数据（包括CelebA）上验证了理论的有效性与潜在因子的可解释性。

Abstract: Many machine learning systems try to explain complex data - like images or financial time series - in terms of hidden, independent factors that generated them. Recovering the true underlying factors, rather than some scrambled version of them, is the central challenge of nonlinear Independent Component Analysis (nICA). We prove identifiability (exact recovery) up to trivial ambiguities for real analytic generating functions when source probability density functions have a finite number of discontinuities in the first derivative. The Laplace distribution is the most prominent example satisfying this assumption. Our proof relies on the contrast between kinks in the source distribution and the smoothness of real analytic functions. Real analytic functions comprise a broad class of generating mechanisms, and can be approximated with Normalizing Flows or Variational Autoencoders with standard activation functions (e.g., tanh, softplus, GELU), so our result applies with minimal changes to existing training pipelines. We perform experiments on real and synthetic data with both Normalizing Flows and Variational Auto-Encoders demonstrating their identifiability properties. In experiments on CelebA data we recover several interpretable latent factors controlling unique attributes across the dataset.

</details>


### [139] [Joint Remaining Useful Life Prediction and Capacity Estimation of Lithium-Ion Batteries Using Partial-Charging Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21932)
*Khoa Tran,Ho-Si-Hung Nguyen,Phone Wai Yan Moe,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Thi-Hoang-Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出跨专家框架，仅用部分充电测量数据联合预测RUL与容量，通过FiLM以短期表征调制长期表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RUL预测与容量估计通常依赖完整循环历史或实测满充容量，难以在实际部分充电场景下同时兼顾渐进退化与近期电池行为的表征。

Method: RUL专家以30循环历史中采样的10个循环之10分钟标称片段为输入，经预训练GRU编码器、2D-CNN与时间GRU；容量专家以10个连续循环的40分钟标称片段统计描述符为输入，经2D-CNN与Transformer；采用FLM模块以短期表征条件化长期表征实现联合预测。训练流程含监督式自编码器预训练、专家独立预训练及冻结专家的融合训练。

Result: 在两个公开电池老化数据集上，参考配置的RUL RMSE分别为143.69与161.10循环，容量误差为12.36与7.28mAh；在数据集I上融合使两项平均误差均低于单独专家。RUL与容量精度存在权衡：本方法在两数据集上取得所比较方法中最低的RUL RMSE，但多个基线容量误差更低。

Conclusion: 跨专家FiLM融合框架可在无实测满充容量条件下实现联合预测，并在RUL精度上表现最优，但RUL与容量精度间存在权衡，容量估计仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Joint remaining useful life (RUL) prediction and capacity estimation require representations of both gradual degradation and recent battery behavior. This paper presents a cross-expert framework using partial-charging measurements without measured historical full-cycle capacity as an input. The RUL Expert encodes nominal 10-min segments from ten cycles sampled within a 30-cycle history using a pretrained gated recurrent unit (GRU) encoder, a two-dimensional convolutional neural network (2D-CNN), and a temporal GRU. The Capacity Expert processes statistical descriptors of nominal 40-min segments from ten consecutive cycles using a 2D-CNN and a Transformer. A feature-wise linear modulation module uses the short-term representation to condition the long-term representation for joint prediction. Training comprises supervised autoencoder pretraining, independent expert pretraining, and fusion training with frozen experts. On two public battery-aging datasets, the reference configuration achieves mean RUL root-mean-square errors of 143.69 and 161.10 cycles and capacity errors of 12.36 and 7.28mAh, respectively. On Dataset I, fusion reduces both mean errors relative to either standalone expert. The results demonstrate a trade-off between RUL and capacity accuracy: the proposed method attains the lowest reported RUL RMSE among the compared methods on both datasets, whereas several baselines yield lower capacity errors.

</details>


### [140] [Learning to Move Cities: Deep Meta-Models and Reinforcement Policies for Calibration and Control in Urban Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21945)
*Adewumi Augustine Adepitan,Christopher J. Haruna,Oluwasegun Adegoke,Ayooluwatomiwa Ajiboye,Oluwatobi Oluwasakin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个共享潜在空间框架，用统一的交通动力学表示连接仿真器校准与强化学习控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通网络优化面临高保真仿真器校准和实时运行控制的双重挑战，传统优化方法在大规模网络上存在计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出组合式MLP自编码器，学习连接仿真输入（OD需求、网络参数）与输出（行程时间、拥堵模式）的低维流形，用于高效贝叶斯优化校准；并实现带经验回放和目标网络的深度Q学习智能体，通过调度与路径调整优化动态交通分配；将学习到的潜在表示同时用于降维校准和强化学习状态表示。

Result: 相比传统降维方法具有更优的样本效率，在固定计算预算下对观测数据拟合更好；基准网络实验中将系统总行程时间最多降低51%。

Conclusion: 该共享潜在空间公式为智能交通系统提供了从仿真器校准到自适应运行控制的统一路径，展示了深度学习在城市交通规划与管理中的变革潜力。

Abstract: Urban transportation networks present complex optimization challenges spanning calibration of high-fidelity simulators and real-time operational control. This paper presents a shared latent-space framework that connects simulator calibration and reinforcement learning control through a common learned representation of urban traffic dynamics. First, we develop a combinatorial MLP-autoencoder architecture that learns low-dimensional manifolds linking simulator inputs (origin-destination demand, network parameters) to outputs (travel times, congestion patterns), enabling efficient Bayesian optimization for calibration. This approach demonstrates superior sample efficiency compared to traditional dimension reduction methods, achieving better fit to observational data within fixed computational budgets. Second, we implement a deep Q-learning agent with experience replay and target networks to optimize dynamic traffic assignment through scheduling and routing adjustments. In empirical evaluations on benchmark networks, our approach reduces system-wide travel times by up to 51% compared to baseline operations. The learned latent representation is not only used to reduce the dimensionality of Bayesian calibration, but is also incorporated into the reinforcement learning state representation, allowing the control policy to operate on compressed and calibrated traffic dynamics. This shared latent-space formulation provides a unified pathway from simulator calibration to adaptive operational control within intelligent transportation systems. Our results highlight the transformative potential of deep learning methods in urban mobility planning and management, particularly for large-scale networks where traditional optimization approaches face computational bottlenecks.

</details>


### [141] [RACER: Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Human-AI Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21953)
*Joshua Strong,Emma Sun,Alexander Capstick,Pramit Saha,Cheng Ouyang,J. Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RACER，一种角色相对胜任度估计框架，通过去除绝对类别身份通道来估计未见专家的胜任度，实现无身份约束的延迟决策，并具有类别重标号不变性与贝叶斯对齐的理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体自适应延迟需利用少量上下文估计未见专家行为，但现有神经上下文编码器（如L2D-Pop）可能学到与绝对类别坐标绑定的路由捷径；IFD虽去除捷径，但其估计在类内恒定，无法刻画实例级专家专长。

Method: RACER在候选类别角色下估计专家对查询回答正确的后验预测概率，再与模型后验结合得到贝叶斯相关的专家正确概率；采用非参数与神经核池化估计器，利用候选角色关系、共享聚合与对称摘要，排除绝对类别身份通道。

Result: 理论证得类别重标号不变性，推导贝叶斯对齐的延迟代理损失，并给出将路由遗憾与分类器及胜任度估计误差关联的插值遗憾界。合成基准及PathMNIST病理上下文扩展实验中，隐藏亚型依赖下RACER能从更多上下文获益；CIFAR-100未见专家划分上取得最佳总体表现；VinDr-CXR与CheXpert基准上，RACER族在预算扫描延迟中具竞争力或最优，校准结果因指标与数据集而异。

Conclusion: RACER以角色相对方式估计专家胜任度，避免绝对类别身份捷径并支持实例级专长，理论与实验均显示其优于或媲美现有群体自适应延迟方法。

Abstract: Learning to defer asks a predictive system when to act autonomously and when to defer to a human expert. Population-adaptive deferral extends this problem to unseen experts using a small context set of expert behavior. Neural context encoders such as L2D-Pop can be query-dependent, but may learn routing shortcuts tied to absolute class coordinates. Identity-Free Deferral (IFD) removes such shortcuts through role-indexed classwise competence profiles, but its estimates are constant within each class and cannot capture instance-level expert specialization. We propose RACER---Role-Aligned Competence Estimation for Routing---a role-relative framework for estimating an unseen expert's competence from context. RACER estimates the posterior-predictive probability that the expert is correct on a query under each candidate class role, then combines these estimates with the model posterior to obtain the Bayes-relevant expert-correctness probability. Nonparametric and neural kernel-pooling estimators use candidate-role relations, shared aggregation, and symmetric summaries, excluding absolute class-identity channels. We prove coherent class-relabelling invariance, derive a Bayes-aligned deferral surrogate, and give a plug-in regret bound relating routing regret to classifier and competence-estimation error. On controlled synthetic benchmarks, including a PathMNIST histopathology context-scaling study with simulated experts, RACER benefits from additional context under hidden subtype dependence and gives the strongest aggregate performance on a separately sampled unseen-expert split in the CIFAR-100 synthetic experiments. On the radiologist and human--AI chest-radiography benchmarks (VinDr-CXR and CheXpert), the RACER family is competitive or best in budget-swept deferral, with calibration results varying across metrics and datasets.

</details>


### [142] [Time series generation with spectrally aligned latent flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21989)
*Camilo Carvajal Reyes,Felipe Tobar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出谱对齐潜空间流匹配时间序列生成器，通过傅里叶、小波、签名变换微调损失，使合成信号在平滑度与频谱内容等特征上与真实数据对齐，提升真实性与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间流模型是高效的时间序列生成方法，但潜压缩会引入伪影（如与底层数据集之间的频谱失配），从而限制其作为训练替代数据（surrogate）的可用性。

Method: 构建谱对齐的潜空间流匹配生成器：在潜空间训练流匹配以保留与合成样本适用性相关的动力学性质，并引入基于傅里叶、小波与签名（signature）变换等规范信号表示的微调损失。借助这些变换的可解释性，使合成信号在平滑度、目标频谱内容等特征上与真实信号对齐，而非仅依赖逐点重建损失。

Result: 在真实世界的长程单变量与多变量基准数据集上，将所提对齐模型与基础潜空间流模型及当前最优方法对比。定量结果显示，所提方法在反映信号真实性的指标与计算效率上更优，同时在局部结构上与训练集保持对齐。
